[FILE: ./apply_patches.sh]

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# =============================================================================
# apply_patches.sh
# Applies unified diff (.patch) files to source code using Python's difflib/
# patch logic. Handles multiline replacements cleanly via an embedded Python
# patcher script written with cat-EOF.
# =============================================================================

set -euo pipefail

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Config — override via env or edit here
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
PATCH_DIR="${PATCH_DIR:-./patches}"      # Directory containing .patch files
TARGET_DIR="${TARGET_DIR:-.}"            # Root of the source tree to patch
BACKUP_EXT="${BACKUP_EXT:-.orig}"        # Extension for backup files ("" = no backups)
DRY_RUN="${DRY_RUN:-0}"                  # Set to 1 to simulate without writing

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Colours
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
RED='\033[0;31m'; GREEN='\033[0;32m'; YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
CYAN='\033[0;36m'; BOLD='\033[1m'; RESET='\033[0m'

info()    { echo -e "${CYAN}[INFO]${RESET}  $*"; }
ok()      { echo -e "${GREEN}[OK]${RESET}    $*"; }
warn()    { echo -e "${YELLOW}[WARN]${RESET}  $*"; }
error()   { echo -e "${RED}[ERROR]${RESET} $*" >&2; }
die()     { error "$*"; exit 1; }

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Write the Python patcher to a temp file using cat-EOF
# Python handles multiline hunks far more cleanly than sed/awk.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
PATCHER_PY="$(mktemp /tmp/patcher_XXXXXX.py)"
trap 'rm -f "$PATCHER_PY"' EXIT

cat > "$PATCHER_PY" << 'PYTHON_SCRIPT'
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
patcher.py — applies a unified diff (.patch) to a source tree.

Usage:
    python3 patcher.py <patch_file> <target_dir> [--dry-run] [--backup <ext>]
"""

import sys
import os
import re
import shutil
import argparse
from pathlib import Path


# ── Argument parsing ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def parse_args():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Apply a unified diff patch.")
    p.add_argument("patch_file", help="Path to the .patch file")
    p.add_argument("target_dir", help="Root directory of the source tree")
    p.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Simulate only")
    p.add_argument("--backup", default="", metavar="EXT",
                   help="Create backups with this extension (e.g. .orig)")
    return p.parse_args()


# ── Patch file parser ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class Hunk:
    """One @@ hunk from a unified diff."""
    def __init__(self, old_start, old_count, new_start, new_count):
        self.old_start  = old_start   # 1-based
        self.old_count  = old_count
        self.new_start  = new_start
        self.new_count  = new_count
        self.lines      = []          # list of (op, text); op in {' ','+','-'}

    def __repr__(self):
        return (f"Hunk(old={self.old_start}+{self.old_count}, "
                f"new={self.new_start}+{self.new_count}, "
                f"lines={len(self.lines)})")


class FilePatch:
    """All hunks for a single file."""
    def __init__(self, old_path, new_path):
        self.old_path = old_path
        self.new_path = new_path
        self.hunks: list[Hunk] = []


def parse_patch(patch_text: str) -> list[FilePatch]:
    """Parse unified diff text into a list of FilePatch objects."""
    file_patches: list[FilePatch] = []
    current_fp: FilePatch | None = None
    current_hunk: Hunk | None = None

    HUNK_HEADER = re.compile(
        r'^@@ -(\d+)(?:,(\d+))? \+(\d+)(?:,(\d+))? @@'
    )

    for raw_line in patch_text.splitlines(keepends=True):
        line = raw_line.rstrip('\n').rstrip('\r')

        if line.startswith('--- '):
            old_path = line[4:].split('\t')[0].strip()
            # strip a/ prefix produced by git
            old_path = re.sub(r'^a/', '', old_path)
            current_fp = None
            _pending_old = old_path
            continue

        if line.startswith('+++ '):
            new_path = line[4:].split('\t')[0].strip()
            new_path = re.sub(r'^b/', '', new_path)
            current_fp = FilePatch(_pending_old, new_path)
            file_patches.append(current_fp)
            current_hunk = None
            continue

        if current_fp is None:
            continue

        m = HUNK_HEADER.match(line)
        if m:
            old_start  = int(m.group(1))
            old_count  = int(m.group(2)) if m.group(2) is not None else 1
            new_start  = int(m.group(3))
            new_count  = int(m.group(4)) if m.group(4) is not None else 1
            current_hunk = Hunk(old_start, old_count, new_start, new_count)
            current_fp.hunks.append(current_hunk)
            continue

        if current_hunk is not None:
            if line.startswith('+'):
                current_hunk.lines.append(('+', line[1:]))
            elif line.startswith('-'):
                current_hunk.lines.append(('-', line[1:]))
            elif line.startswith(' ') or line == '':
                current_hunk.lines.append((' ', line[1:] if line else ''))
            # else: "\ No newline at end of file" etc — ignore

    return file_patches


# ── Patch applier ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class PatchError(Exception):
    pass


def apply_hunk(lines: list[str], hunk: Hunk, filename: str) -> list[str]:
    """
    Apply one hunk to `lines` (0-based list, no trailing newlines).
    Returns the modified list.
    """
    result     = lines[:]
    old_lineno = hunk.old_start - 1   # convert to 0-based index
    out_lines  = result[:old_lineno]
    src_pos    = old_lineno

    for op, text in hunk.lines:
        if op == ' ':
            # context line — must match
            if src_pos >= len(result):
                raise PatchError(
                    f"{filename}: hunk context line {src_pos+1} is past EOF"
                )
            actual = result[src_pos]
            if actual != text:
                raise PatchError(
                    f"{filename}: context mismatch at line {src_pos+1}:\n"
                    f"  expected: {text!r}\n"
                    f"  found:    {actual!r}"
                )
            out_lines.append(text)
            src_pos += 1

        elif op == '-':
            # removal — must match
            if src_pos >= len(result):
                raise PatchError(
                    f"{filename}: removal line {src_pos+1} is past EOF"
                )
            actual = result[src_pos]
            if actual != text:
                raise PatchError(
                    f"{filename}: removal mismatch at line {src_pos+1}:\n"
                    f"  expected: {text!r}\n"
                    f"  found:    {actual!r}"
                )
            src_pos += 1   # skip (delete) it

        elif op == '+':
            out_lines.append(text)   # insert new line

    # append remainder of file
    out_lines.extend(result[src_pos:])
    return out_lines


def apply_file_patch(fp: FilePatch, target_dir: Path,
                     dry_run: bool, backup_ext: str) -> bool:
    """Apply all hunks of fp to the target file. Returns True on success."""
    # Resolve target path — use new_path; fall back to old_path
    rel = fp.new_path if fp.new_path != '/dev/null' else fp.old_path
    target = (target_dir / rel).resolve()

    # Deletion patch  (/dev/null as new_path)
    if fp.new_path == '/dev/null':
        if not target.exists():
            print(f"  [SKIP] already absent: {rel}")
            return True
        print(f"  [DELETE] {rel}")
        if not dry_run:
            if backup_ext:
                shutil.copy2(target, str(target) + backup_ext)
            target.unlink()
        return True

    # Creation patch  (/dev/null as old_path)
    if fp.old_path == '/dev/null':
        print(f"  [CREATE] {rel}")
        content = []
        for hunk in fp.hunks:
            for op, text in hunk.lines:
                if op == '+':
                    content.append(text)
        if not dry_run:
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            target.write_text('\n'.join(content) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
        return True

    # Modification patch
    if not target.exists():
        print(f"  [MISSING] {rel}  — skipping", file=sys.stderr)
        return False

    raw   = target.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    lines = raw.splitlines()   # no trailing newlines

    try:
        for hunk in fp.hunks:
            lines = apply_hunk(lines, hunk, rel)
    except PatchError as exc:
        print(f"  [FAIL] {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return False

    new_content = '\n'.join(lines)
    # preserve trailing newline if original had one
    if raw.endswith('\n'):
        new_content += '\n'

    print(f"  [PATCH] {rel}")
    if not dry_run:
        if backup_ext:
            shutil.copy2(target, str(target) + backup_ext)
        target.write_text(new_content, encoding='utf-8')

    return True


# ── Entry point ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def main():
    args       = parse_args()
    patch_file = Path(args.patch_file)
    target_dir = Path(args.target_dir).resolve()

    if not patch_file.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: patch file not found: {patch_file}", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)

    if not target_dir.is_dir():
        print(f"ERROR: target directory not found: {target_dir}", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)

    patch_text   = patch_file.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    file_patches = parse_patch(patch_text)

    if not file_patches:
        print("WARNING: no file patches found in patch file.", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(0)

    mode_label = " [DRY RUN]" if args.dry_run else ""
    print(f"\nApplying: {patch_file.name}{mode_label}")
    print(f"Target:   {target_dir}\n")

    failed = 0
    for fp in file_patches:
        ok = apply_file_patch(fp, target_dir,
                              dry_run=args.dry_run,
                              backup_ext=args.backup)
        if not ok:
            failed += 1

    print()
    if failed:
        print(f"RESULT: {len(file_patches)-failed} succeeded, "
              f"{failed} failed.", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)
    else:
        print(f"RESULT: all {len(file_patches)} file(s) patched successfully.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
PYTHON_SCRIPT

chmod +x "$PATCHER_PY"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
usage() {
    cat << USAGE
${BOLD}apply_patches.sh${RESET} — apply unified diff patches to source code via Python

${BOLD}USAGE${RESET}
    $0 [OPTIONS] [patch_file_or_dir]

${BOLD}OPTIONS${RESET}
    -t, --target DIR      Source tree root        (default: \$TARGET_DIR or .)
    -p, --patches DIR     Directory of .patch files (default: \$PATCH_DIR or ./patches)
    -b, --backup EXT      Backup extension         (default: \$BACKUP_EXT or .orig)
    -n, --dry-run         Simulate; do not write
    -h, --help            Show this help

${BOLD}ENVIRONMENT${RESET}
    PATCH_DIR, TARGET_DIR, BACKUP_EXT, DRY_RUN

${BOLD}EXAMPLES${RESET}
    # Apply all patches in ./patches/ to the current directory
    $0

    # Apply a single patch, dry run, no backups
    $0 --dry-run --backup "" fix-issue-42.patch

    # Apply all patches to a specific source tree
    $0 --target /src/myproject --patches /work/patches

USAGE
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Parse CLI args
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
EXPLICIT_PATCH=""

while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
    case "$1" in
        -t|--target)   TARGET_DIR="$2";  shift 2 ;;
        -p|--patches)  PATCH_DIR="$2";   shift 2 ;;
        -b|--backup)   BACKUP_EXT="$2";  shift 2 ;;
        -n|--dry-run)  DRY_RUN=1;        shift   ;;
        -h|--help)     usage; exit 0               ;;
        -*)            die "Unknown option: $1"     ;;
        *)             EXPLICIT_PATCH="$1"; shift  ;;
    esac
done

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Validate environment
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
command -v python3 &>/dev/null || die "python3 is required but not found in PATH"

[[ -d "$TARGET_DIR" ]] || die "Target directory not found: $TARGET_DIR"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Build list of patches to apply
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
declare -a PATCHES=()

if [[ -n "$EXPLICIT_PATCH" ]]; then
    [[ -f "$EXPLICIT_PATCH" ]] || die "Patch file not found: $EXPLICIT_PATCH"
    PATCHES=("$EXPLICIT_PATCH")
else
    [[ -d "$PATCH_DIR" ]] || die "Patch directory not found: $PATCH_DIR"
    while IFS= read -r -d '' f; do
        PATCHES+=("$f")
    done < <(find "$PATCH_DIR" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.patch' -print0 | sort -z)
    [[ ${#PATCHES[@]} -gt 0 ]] || die "No .patch files found in: $PATCH_DIR"
fi

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Summary banner
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
echo ""
echo -e "${BOLD}════════════════════════════════════════════${RESET}"
echo -e "${BOLD} Source Patch Runner${RESET}"
echo -e "${BOLD}════════════════════════════════════════════${RESET}"
info "Target  : $TARGET_DIR"
info "Patches : ${#PATCHES[@]} file(s)"
info "Backups : ${BACKUP_EXT:-"(disabled)"}"
[[ "$DRY_RUN" == "1" ]] && warn "DRY RUN mode — no files will be modified"
echo ""

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Apply each patch
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
PASS=0
FAIL=0

for patch in "${PATCHES[@]}"; do
    echo -e "${BOLD}── $(basename "$patch") ──${RESET}"

    py_args=("$patch" "$TARGET_DIR")
    [[ "$DRY_RUN"    == "1"  ]] && py_args+=("--dry-run")
    [[ -n "$BACKUP_EXT"      ]] && py_args+=("--backup" "$BACKUP_EXT")

    if python3 "$PATCHER_PY" "${py_args[@]}"; then
        ok "Patch applied: $(basename "$patch")"
        (( PASS++ )) || true
    else
        error "Patch FAILED: $(basename "$patch")"
        (( FAIL++ )) || true
    fi
    echo ""
done

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Final summary
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
echo -e "${BOLD}════════════════════════════════════════════${RESET}"
if [[ $FAIL -eq 0 ]]; then
    ok "${BOLD}All $PASS patch(es) applied successfully.${RESET}"
else
    warn "Passed: $PASS  |  Failed: $FAIL"
    echo -e "${RED}Some patches failed. Check output above.${RESET}"
    exit 1
fi

use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::Mutex;
use nexus_runtime_core::{
    agents::agentic_runtime::{AgenticRuntime, SubstrateContext},
    shared_state::VmoState,
    distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler,
    cluster::ClusterMesh,
};
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    println!("\x1b[32m[NEXUS HEARTBEAT] Initiating System Check...\x1b[0m");

    // 1. Test Substrate (Zig Bridge)
    let handle = unsafe { nexus_substrate::kernel::ffi::zx_vmo_create_native(1024) };
    if handle > 0 {
        println!("\x1b[34m[OK]\x1b[0m Zig Substrate: VMO Created (Handle: 0x{:x})", handle);
        unsafe { nexus_substrate::kernel::ffi::zx_handle_close_native(handle) };
        println!("\x1b[34m[OK]\x1b[0m Zig Substrate: VMO Released");
    } else {
        panic!("Zig Bridge Failure: Could not create VMO");
    }

    // 2. Test Agentic Runtime (LRL)
    let memory = Arc::new(Mutex::new(MemoryPalace::new("heartbeat.db").await?));
    let vmo_state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(VmoState::default()));
    let mesh = Arc::new(ClusterMesh::new());
    let dist = Arc::new(DistributedScheduler::new(mesh));

    let mut lrl = AgenticRuntime::new(SubstrateContext {
        memory,
        vmo_state,
        dist,
    });

    println!("\x1b[34m[OK]\x1b[0m Agentic Runtime: Context Initialized");
    
    let result = lrl.dispatch("echo 'NEXUS_ALIVE'").await?;
    if result.contains("NEXUS_ALIVE") {
        println!("\x1b[34m[OK]\x1b[0m LRL Dispatcher: Shell Routing Verified");
    }

    println!("\x1b[32m[SUCCESS] NEXUS SINGULARITY V3 IS OPERATIONAL\x1b[0m");
    Ok(())
}

const std = @import("std");
const build_options = @import("build_options"); // SUTURE: Import the DNA matrix

pub fn main() !void {
    var gpa = std.heap.GeneralPurposeAllocator(.{}){};
    defer _ = gpa.deinit();
    const allocator = gpa.allocator();

    const log_file = try std.fs.cwd().createFile("nexus_watchdog.log", .{ .truncate = false });
    defer log_file.close();
    try log_file.seekFromEnd(0);
    const writer = log_file.writer();

    const api_key = std.process.getEnvVarOwned(allocator, "NEXUS_API_KEY") catch "MISSING_KEY";
    defer if (!std.mem.eql(u8, api_key, "MISSING_KEY")) allocator.free(api_key);

    try writer.print("\n--- NEXUS v0.2.6 (RESILIENT) START: {d} ---\n", .{std.time.timestamp()});
    
    if (build_options.is_duet2) try writer.print(">> [HOST] Lenovo Duet 2 (Crostini) Detected.\n", .{});
    if (build_options.is_moto) try writer.print(">> [HOST] Motorola Edge (Termux) Detected.\n", .{});

    while (true) {
        var child = std.process.Child.init(&[_][]const u8{ "git", "log", "-1", "--pretty=format:HASH:%H MSG:%B" }, allocator);
        child.stdout_behavior = .Pipe;
        try child.spawn();
        const msg = try child.stdout.?.reader().readAllAlloc(allocator, 8192);
        defer allocator.free(msg);
        _ = try child.wait();

        const url = try std.fmt.allocPrint(allocator, "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemma-3-27b-it:generateContent?key={s}", .{api_key});
        defer allocator.free(url);
        const payload = try std.fmt.allocPrint(allocator, "{{\"contents\": [{{ \"parts\":[{{ \"text\": \"Audit DNA. Provide git patch between PATCH_START and PATCH_END: {s}\" }}]}}]}}", .{msg});
        defer allocator.free(payload);

        var api_child = std.process.Child.init(&[_][]const u8{ "curl", "-s", "-X", "POST", url, "-H", "Content-Type: application/json", "-d", payload }, allocator);
        api_child.stdout_behavior = .Pipe;
        try api_child.spawn();
        const response = try api_child.stdout.?.reader().readAllAlloc(allocator, 64000);
        defer allocator.free(response);
        _ = try api_child.wait();

        if (std.mem.indexOf(u8, response, "PATCH_START")) |start_idx| {
            if (std.mem.indexOf(u8, response, "PATCH_END")) |end_idx| {
                const patch = response[start_idx + 11 .. end_idx];
                try writer.print("[{d}] SENTINEL: AUDIT RECEIVED (STAGED). Patch stored in log.\n", .{std.time.timestamp()});

                const patch_file = try std.fs.cwd().createFile("repair.patch", .{});
                defer patch_file.close();
                try patch_file.writeAll(patch);

                // SUTURE: Hardware-aware notification block. Will not crash on Crostini.
                if (build_options.is_moto) {
                    var notify = std.process.Child.init(&[_][]const u8{ "termux-notification", "-t", "NEXUS RESILIENT", "-c", "High-Fidelity Patch Ready for Review." }, allocator);
                    _ = notify.spawnAndWait() catch {}; 
                } else if (build_options.is_duet2) {
                    // Fallback for Chromebook Native Linux via libnotify (if installed)
                    var notify = std.process.Child.init(&[_][]const u8{ "notify-send", "NEXUS RESILIENT", "High-Fidelity Patch Ready for Review." }, allocator);
                    _ = notify.spawnAndWait() catch {};
                }
            }
        }
        std.time.sleep(60 * std.time.ns_per_s);
    }
}

[FILE: ./archives/zig_legacy/muscle.zig]

const std = @import("std");
const posix = std.posix; 

// --- KERNEL STATE ---
var active_vmos: u32 = 0;
var next_handle: u32 = 0xc8;

// Implementation of the VMO creation
export fn zx_vmo_create_native(size: usize) u32 {
    const ptr = posix.mmap(
        null,
        size,
        posix.PROT.READ | posix.PROT.WRITE,
        .{ .TYPE = .PRIVATE, .ANONYMOUS = true },
        -1,
        0,
    ) catch return 0;

    active_vmos += 1;
    return @truncate(@intFromPtr(ptr.ptr));
}

export fn zx_handle_close_native(handle: u32) void {
    const addr: usize = @as(usize, handle);
    
    // ⚡ THE FIX: Explicitly anchor the type as a many-item pointer ([*]u8)
    // before performing the alignment cast to align(4096).
    const raw_ptr = @as([*]u8, @ptrFromInt(addr));
    const ptr: [*]align(4096) u8 = @alignCast(raw_ptr);
    
    posix.munmap(ptr[0..4096]);
    
    if (active_vmos > 0) active_vmos -= 1;
}

// ⚡ Commutation Bridge (Write)
export fn zx_vmo_write_native(handle: u32, buffer: [*]const u8, size: usize) i32 {
    const vmo_ptr: [*]u8 = @ptrFromInt(@as(usize, handle));
    @memcpy(vmo_ptr[0..size], buffer[0..size]);
    return 0; 
}

// ⚡ Commutation Bridge (Read)
export fn zx_vmo_read_native(handle: u32, buffer: [*]u8, size: usize) i32 {
    const vmo_ptr: [*]const u8 = @ptrFromInt(@as(usize, handle));
    @memcpy(buffer[0..size], vmo_ptr[0..size]);
    return 0;
}

export fn muscle_get_active_vmos() u32 { return active_vmos; }
export fn muscle_get_next_handle() u32 { return next_handle; }

const std = @import("std");

/// The 32-D Latent Space Proxy
/// [0..7]   - Power/Thermal Intent (Throttle)
/// [8..15]  - Neural Attention Priority
/// [16..31] - Spatiotemporal Action Vectors
var latent_intent = [_]f32{0.0} ** 32;

/// Push: Rust calls this to send the AI's "Imaginative" intent to the NPU
export fn push_latent_intent(input_ptr: [*]const f32) void {
    const data: [*]const f32 = input_ptr;
    inline for (0..32) |i| {
        latent_intent[i] = data[i];
    }
}

/// Pull: Rust calls this to check the "Reflex" hardware status
export fn get_latent_throttle() f32 {
    var sum: f32 = 0.0;
    // Calculate a safety floor based on the first 8 dimensions
    for (latent_intent[0..8]) |val| {
        sum += val;
    }
    return sum / 8.0;
}

/// Original Resonance Logic for Hardware Monitoring
pub export fn calculate_resonance_e2b(mmap_ptr: [*]const u8, length: usize) f32 {
    var total_energy: f32 = 0.0;
    var i: usize = 0;
    while (i < length) : (i += 64) {
        const byte = mmap_ptr[i];
        const val = (@as(f32, @floatFromInt(byte)) / 255.0);
        total_energy += (val * val);
    }
    const count = @as(f32, @floatFromInt(length / 64));
    return if (count > 0) total_energy / count else 0.0;
}

#!/bin/bash
# Nexus Singularity: Synchronization Audit Script

echo -e "\e[1;36m=== NEXUS_OS SYNCHRONIZATION AUDIT ===\e[0m"

# 1. Audit the Bridge (Vite Proxy Config)
PROXY_TARGET=$(grep -o 'http://localhost:3000' bins/ide_backend/ui/vite.config.js)
if [ "$PROXY_TARGET" == "http://localhost:3000" ]; then
    echo -e "\e[32m[PASS]\e[0m Bridge: Vite proxy configured to port 3000."
else
    echo -e "\e[31m[FAIL]\e[0m Bridge: Vite proxy mismatch. Expected port 3000."
fi

# 2. Audit the Layout (Checking for Rail implementation status)
if [ -f "bins/ide_backend/ui/src/lib/components/RightSideRail.svelte" ]; then
    echo -e "\e[32m[PASS]\e[0m Rail: RightSideRail component exists."
else
    echo -e "\e[33m[WARN]\e[0m Rail: RightSideRail component not yet injected. (Baseline state)"
fi

# 3. Audit the Substrate (Kernel/Backend)
# Check for binding in Rust server code
if grep -r "3000" bins/ide_backend/src/ > /dev/null; then
    echo -e "\e[32m[PASS]\e[0m Substrate: Kernel port 3000 binding detected."
else
    echo -e "\e[31m[FAIL]\e[0m Substrate: No binding to port 3000 found in src/."
fi

# 4. Audit DNA (Version/Build)
if [ -f "VERSION_CHECK.md" ]; then
    echo -e "\e[32m[PASS]\e[0m DNA: Version Check file present."
else
    echo -e "\e[31m[FAIL]\e[0m DNA: Missing version manifest."
fi

echo -e "\e[1;36m=== AUDIT COMPLETE ===\e[0m"

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e
cd /home/donball197/nexus_singularity-dev || exit 1
echo "🔬 Building Fair Zig and Rust WASM cells..."

# 1. Build the Zig Cell (Static Memory)
cat > /tmp/benchmark_zig.zig << 'EOF'
const std = @import("std");
var buffer = [_]u8{42} ** 8192;

export fn resonance_benchmark(iterations: u32) f32 {
    var total: f32 = 0.0;
    var i: u32 = 0;
    while (i < iterations) : (i += 1) {
        var sum: f32 = 0.0;
        for (buffer) |b| {
            const v = @as(f32, @floatFromInt(b)) / 255.0;
            sum += v * v;
        }
        total += sum;
    }
    return total / @as(f32, @floatFromInt(iterations));
}
EOF
zig build-lib /tmp/benchmark_zig.zig -target wasm32-freestanding -O ReleaseFast -dynamic --name zig_bench -femit-bin=/tmp/zig_bench.wasm

# 2. Build the Rust Cell (Static Memory for fairness)
mkdir -p /tmp/rust_bench/src
cat > /tmp/rust_bench/Cargo.toml << 'EOF'
[package]
name = "rust_bench"
version = "0.1.0"
edition = "2021"
[lib]
crate-type = ["cdylib"]
[profile.release]
opt-level = 3
lto = true
panic = 'abort'
codegen-units = 1
EOF

cat > /tmp/rust_bench/src/lib.rs << 'EOF'
static mut BUFFER: [u8; 8192] = [42; 8192];

#[no_mangle]
pub extern "C" fn resonance_benchmark(iterations: u32) -> f32 {
    let mut total = 0.0f32;
    for _ in 0..iterations {
        let mut sum = 0.0f32;
        unsafe {
            for &b in &BUFFER {
                let v = b as f32 / 255.0;
                sum += v * v;
            }
        }
        total += sum;
    }
    total / iterations as f32
}
EOF
cd /tmp/rust_bench
cargo build --target wasm32-unknown-unknown --release --quiet
cp target/wasm32-unknown-unknown/release/rust_bench.wasm /tmp/rust_bench.wasm

cd /home/donball197/nexus_singularity-dev

# 3. Create the Benchmark Runner inside the Nexus Project
cat > src/bin/bench_wasm.rs << 'EOF'
use wasmtime::*;
use std::time::Instant;

fn run_wasmtime(name: &str, path: &str) {
    let engine = Engine::default();
    let mut store = Store::new(&engine, ());
    let module = Module::from_file(&engine, path).expect("Failed to load WASM");
    let instance = Instance::new(&mut store, &module, &[]).expect("Failed to instantiate");
    let func = instance.get_typed_func::<u32, f32>(&mut store, "resonance_benchmark").unwrap();

    // Warmup (JIT compilation phase)
    let _ = func.call(&mut store, 10).unwrap();

    // Actual Benchmark
    let start = Instant::now();
    let result = func.call(&mut store, 10_000).unwrap();
    let duration = start.elapsed();

    println!("{:>18} | {:>8.2} ms | Result: {:.4}", 
        name, duration.as_secs_f64() * 1000.0, result);
}

fn main() {
    println!("\n=== NEXUS WASM RESONANCE BENCHMARK (10,000 ITERATIONS) ===\n");
    println!("Runtime/Language   | Execution Time | Validation");
    println!("--------------------------------------------------");
    run_wasmtime("Wasmtime (Zig)", "/tmp/zig_bench.wasm");
    run_wasmtime("Wasmtime (Rust)", "/tmp/rust_bench.wasm");
    println!("\n✅ Benchmark complete!");
}
EOF

# 4. Execute safely using Cargo
echo "🚀 Executing benchmark suite via Cargo..."
cargo run --release -j 1 --bin bench_wasm

use std::io::{self, Write};
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};
use nexus_runtime_core::{
    neural_agent::MicroAgent,
    cluster::ClusterMesh,
    distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler,
    orchestrator::Orchestrator,
    shared_state::VmoState,
    task_manager::{send_task, TaskContext},
};
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    dotenvy::dotenv().ok();
    
    println!("\x1b[36m==================================================\x1b[0m");
    println!("\x1b[1;36m>> NEXUS SINGULARITY v3.0 — SOVEREIGN CLI ONLINE\x1b[0m");
    println!("\x1b[36m==================================================\x1b[0m");

    let api_key = std::env::var("GEMINI_API_KEY")
        .expect("Fatal: GEMINI_API_KEY not found in .env file.");

    let (tx, mut rx) = broadcast::channel::<String>(256);
    let memory = Arc::new(Mutex::new(MemoryPalace::new("nexus_cli.db").await?));
    let vmo_state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(VmoState::default()));
    let cluster = Arc::new(ClusterMesh::new());
    let dist = Arc::new(DistributedScheduler::new(cluster));
    let orchestrator = Arc::new(Orchestrator::new());
    let client = reqwest::Client::new();

    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut last_type = String::new();
        while let Ok(msg) = rx.recv().await {
            if let Ok(parsed) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&msg) {
                let msg_type = parsed["type"].as_str().unwrap_or("LOG");
                let payload = parsed["payload"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                
                match msg_type {
                    "NEURAL_THOUGHT" => {
                        if last_type != "NEURAL_THOUGHT" { 
                            print!("\n\x1b[1;35m[CORTEX THINKING]\x1b[0m\n\x1b[35m"); 
                        }
                        print!("{}", payload);
                        let _ = io::stdout().flush();
                        last_type = "NEURAL_THOUGHT".to_string();
                    },
                    "NEURAL_STREAM" => {
                        if last_type != "NEURAL_STREAM" { 
                            print!("\x1b[0m\n\x1b[1;36m[CORTEX EXECUTING]\x1b[0m\n\x1b[36m"); 
                        }
                        print!("{}", payload);
                        let _ = io::stdout().flush();
                        last_type = "NEURAL_STREAM".to_string();
                    },
                    "TASK_STATUS" => {
                        println!("\x1b[0m\n\x1b[1;33m[SYS]\x1b[0m {}", payload);
                        last_type = "TASK_STATUS".to_string();
                    },
                    "LOG_EVENT" => {
                        println!("\x1b[0m\n\x1b[1;90m[EVENT]\x1b[0m {}", payload);
                        last_type = "LOG_EVENT".to_string();
                    },
                    _ => {}
                }
            }
        }
    });

    println!("\x1b[32m>> Neural Uplink Established. Substrate Ready.\x1b[0m");
    println!("\x1b[90mType '/help' for commands, or enter a directive.\x1b[0m");

    let stdin = io::stdin();
    
    loop {
        print!("\n\x1b[1;32mnexus>\x1b[0m ");
        io::stdout().flush()?;

        let mut input = String::new();
        stdin.read_line(&mut input)?;
        let command = input.trim();

        if command.is_empty() { continue; }

        match command {
            "/exit" | "/quit" => {
                println!("\x1b[31m>> Severing Uplink. Goodbye.\x1b[0m");
                break;
            }
            "/help" => {
                println!("\x1b[36mCommands:\x1b[0m");
                println!("  /status    - View Substrate Load & Active VMOs");
                println!("  /clear     - Purge SQLite Memory Palace");
                println!("  /exit     - Shutdown CLI");
                println!("  <text>     - Submit directive to the Neural Agent");
            }
            "/status" => {
                let active = unsafe { nexus_substrate::kernel::ffi::muscle_get_active_vmos() };
                println!("\x1b[36m[SUBSTRATE]\x1b[0m Active VMOs: {}", active);
            }
            "/clear" => {
                let mem = memory.lock().await;
                if let Err(e) = mem.purge_all().await {
                    println!("\x1b[31m[ERROR]\x1b[0m Memory purge failed: {}", e);
                } else {
                    println!("\x1b[32m[MEMORY]\x1b[0m Neural context eradicated.");
                }
            }
            directive => {
                println!("\x1b[36m>> Engaging Neural Routing...\x1b[0m");

                let ctx = TaskContext {
                    client: client.clone(),
                    api_key: api_key.clone(),
                    memory: memory.clone(),
                    vmo: vmo_state.clone(),
                    tx: tx.clone(),
                    dist: dist.clone(),
                };

                let response = send_task(
                    orchestrator.clone(),
                    ctx,
                    directive
                ).await;
                
                println!("\n\x1b[0m\x1b[1;32m[MISSION COMPLETE]\x1b[0m {}", response);
            }
        }
    }

    Ok(())
}

use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use jsonwebtoken::{decode, encode, Algorithm, DecodingKey, EncodingKey, Header, Validation};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

use zeroize::Zeroizing;
const MIN_KEY_BYTES: usize = 32;

/// Call once at startup. Returns a heap-allocated key that zeroes on drop.
/// Aborts with a descriptive error if the env var is missing or too short.
pub fn load_key_at_startup() -> anyhow::Result<Zeroizing<String>> {
    let key = std::env::var("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY")
        .map_err(|_| anyhow::anyhow!("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY env var is not set — refusing to start"))?;
    if key.len() < MIN_KEY_BYTES {
        return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
            "NEXUS_AUTH_KEY is too short ({} bytes). Minimum is {} bytes.",
            key.len(), MIN_KEY_BYTES
        ));
    }
    Ok(Zeroizing::new(key))
}
const DEFAULT_TTL_SECS: u64 = 86_400;

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Claims {
    pub sub: String,
    pub exp: usize,
    pub iat: usize,
}

fn load_key() -> Result<String> {
    // Key is validated and loaded once at startup via load_key_at_startup().
    // This shim reads it again for backward compatibility; prefer passing the
    // Zeroizing<String> from AppState directly in new code.
    let key = std::env::var("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY")
        .map_err(|_| anyhow!("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY env var is not set"))?;
    if key.len() < MIN_KEY_BYTES {
        return Err(anyhow!(
            "NEXUS_AUTH_KEY is too short ({} bytes). Minimum is {} bytes.",
            key.len(), MIN_KEY_BYTES
        ));
    }
    Ok(key)
}

fn token_ttl() -> u64 {
    match std::env::var("NEXUS_TOKEN_TTL_SECS") {
        Ok(v) => match v.parse::<u64>() {
            Ok(secs) => secs,
            Err(_) => {
                tracing::warn!("[AUTH] NEXUS_TOKEN_TTL_SECS='{}' is not a valid u64. Using default {}s. Fix your .env file.", v, DEFAULT_TTL_SECS);
                DEFAULT_TTL_SECS
            }
        },
        Err(_) => DEFAULT_TTL_SECS,
    }
}

pub fn issue_sovereign_token() -> Result<String> {
    let key = load_key()?;
    let now = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)?.as_secs();
    let claims = Claims {
        sub: "architect".to_owned(),
        iat: now as usize,
        exp: (now + token_ttl()) as usize,
    };
    encode(&Header::default(), &claims, &EncodingKey::from_secret(key.as_bytes()))
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("JWT_GEN_FAIL: {}", e))
}

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum TokenVerdict {
    Valid,
    Expired,
    InvalidSignature,
    MissingKey,
}

pub fn verify_token_detailed(token: &str) -> Result<TokenVerdict> {
    let key = load_key()?;
    let mut validation = Validation::new(Algorithm::HS256);
    validation.leeway = 0;
    match decode::<Claims>(token, &DecodingKey::from_secret(key.as_bytes()), &validation) {
        Ok(_) => Ok(TokenVerdict::Valid),
        Err(e) => Ok(match e.kind() {
            jsonwebtoken::errors::ErrorKind::ExpiredSignature => TokenVerdict::Expired,
            _ => TokenVerdict::InvalidSignature,
        }),
    }
}

pub fn verify_token(token: &str) -> Result<bool> {
    match verify_token_detailed(token)? {
        TokenVerdict::Valid => Ok(true),
        TokenVerdict::Expired => {
            tracing::warn!("[AUTH] Token rejected: expired");
            Ok(false)
        }
        TokenVerdict::InvalidSignature => {
            tracing::warn!("[AUTH] Token rejected: invalid signature");
            Ok(false)
        }
        TokenVerdict::MissingKey => Ok(false),
    }
}

//! Broker — deduplicates rapid fire directives within a 200 ms window
//! so the same button-mash doesn't flood the agent pipeline.
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Mutex;
use std::time::Instant;
use once_cell::sync::Lazy;

static LAST_FIRE: Lazy<Mutex<HashMap<String, Instant>>> =
    Lazy::new(|| Mutex::new(HashMap::new()));

const DEBOUNCE_MS: u128 = 200;

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [BROKER] Directive deduplicator online");
}

/// Returns true when the directive should be forwarded (not a duplicate).
pub fn should_forward(key: &str) -> bool {
    let mut map = LAST_FIRE.lock().unwrap();
    let now = Instant::now();
    if let Some(last) = map.get(key) {
        if last.elapsed().as_millis() < DEBOUNCE_MS {
            return false;
        }
    }
    map.insert(key.to_string(), now);
    true
}

use crate::server::AppState;
use axum::{extract::State, Json};
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::sync::Arc;

pub async fn list_nodes(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>) -> Json<Value> {
    let nodes = state.cluster.get_all_nodes();
    Json(json!({ "nodes": nodes }))
}

pub async fn cluster_status(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>) -> Json<Value> {
    let best = state.cluster.best_node();
    Json(json!({
        "best_node": best,
        "has_capacity": best.is_some(),
    }))
}

/// WASM cell registry — returns cells that have been synthesised and stored.
/// Cells are written to .nexus_wasm_registry.json by wasm_forge when synthesised.
pub async fn list_wasm_cells(_state: axum::extract::State<std::sync::Arc<crate::server::AppState>>) -> axum::Json<serde_json::Value> {
    let path = ".nexus_wasm_registry.json";
    let cells: Vec<serde_json::Value> = if std::path::Path::new(path).exists() {
        tokio::fs::read_to_string(path).await
            .ok()
            .and_then(|s| serde_json::from_str(&s).ok())
            .unwrap_or_default()
    } else { vec![] };
    axum::Json(serde_json::json!({ "cells": cells }))
}

//! DreamCycle — background task that wakes every 30 minutes, pulls random
//! memory strands from the MemoryPalace, and asks the LLM to synthesise an
//! architectural insight which is stored back as a system_insight.
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};
use tokio::time::{interval, Duration};
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [DREAM_CYCLE] Subconscious synthesis engine initialised");
}

pub fn spawn(
    memory: Arc<Mutex<MemoryPalace>>,
    tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
    client: reqwest::Client,
    api_key: String,
    orchestrator: Arc<nexus_runtime_core::orchestrator::Orchestrator>,
) {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut ticker = interval(Duration::from_secs(30 * 60));
        ticker.tick().await; // skip immediate first tick
        loop {
            ticker.tick().await;
            tracing::info!("[DREAM_CYCLE] Beginning synthesis pass…");
            let _ = tx.send(
                serde_json::json!({"type":"LOG_EVENT","payload":"[DREAM] Synthesis cycle awakening…"})
                    .to_string()
            );

            let strands = {
                let mem = memory.lock().await;
                mem.fetch_random_strands(8).await.unwrap_or_default()
            };

            if strands.is_empty() {
                tracing::info!("[DREAM_CYCLE] No strands to synthesise");
                continue;
            }

            let context = strands.iter()
                .map(|s| format!("DIRECTIVE: {}\nRESULT: {}", s.directive, s.result))
                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                .join("\n---\n");

            let prompt = format!(
                "You are NEXUS Archivist. Analyse these memory strands and write one concise \
                 architectural insight (3-5 sentences, plain text, no JSON):\n\n{}",
                context
            );

            let ctx = nexus_runtime_core::neural_agent::ContextPacket {
                system_prompt: Some("Output plain text only. One paragraph.".into()),
                model_override: Some("gemma-4-26b-a4b-it".into()),
                ..Default::default()
            };

            match orchestrator.run(prompt, ctx, &client, &api_key, Some(tx.clone())).await {
                Ok(insight) => {
                    let mem = memory.lock().await;
                    if let Err(e) = mem.store_insight(&insight).await {
                        tracing::warn!("[DREAM_CYCLE] Failed to store insight: {}", e);
                    } else {
                        tracing::info!("[DREAM_CYCLE] Insight stored");
                    }
                }
                Err(e) => tracing::warn!("[DREAM_CYCLE] Synthesis failed: {}", e),
            }
        }
    });
}

//! EdgeWorker — handles registration of remote cluster nodes via HTTP POST
//! to /api/cluster/register.  Routes are wired in server.rs.
use axum::{extract::State, Json, response::IntoResponse};
use std::sync::Arc;
use crate::server::AppState;
use nexus_runtime_core::cluster::ClusterNode;

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [EDGE_WORKER] Peer registration handler online");
}

pub async fn register_node(
    State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>,
    Json(node): Json<ClusterNode>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    state.cluster.register(node.clone());
    tracing::info!("[EDGE_WORKER] Node {} registered at {}:{}", node.id, node.host, node.port);
    Json(serde_json::json!({ "status": "registered", "id": node.id }))
}

//! Heartbeat — sends a periodic JSON ping over the broadcast channel so
//! the WebSocket watchdog in socket.ts never fires a stale-connection close.
use tokio::sync::broadcast;
use tokio::time::{interval, Duration};
use serde_json::json;

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [HEARTBEAT] Pulse initialised");
}

pub fn spawn(tx: broadcast::Sender<String>) {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut ticker = interval(Duration::from_secs(5));
        loop {
            ticker.tick().await;
            let msg = json!({ "type": "HEARTBEAT", "ts": chrono_now() }).to_string();
            let _ = tx.send(msg);
        }
    });
}

fn chrono_now() -> u64 {
    std::time::SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .as_secs()
}

[FILE: ./bins/ide_backend/src/lazarus_vault.rs]
========================================
//! LazarusVault — file-system snapshot store.
//! Snapshots are written as <sha256_of_content>.snap alongside the source
//! file, catalogued in .nexus_snapshots.json at the workspace root.
use anyhow::Result;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::path::Path;
use tokio::fs;

const CATALOG: &str = ".nexus_snapshots.json";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Snapshot {
    pub id:        String,
    pub path:      String,
    pub hash:      String,
    pub directive: String,
    pub timestamp: String,
}

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [LAZARUS] Temporal vault online");
}

pub async fn take_snapshot(path: &str, directive: &str) -> Result<Snapshot> {
    let content = fs::read(path).await?;
    let hash = sha256_hex(&content);
    let snap_path = format!("{}.{}.snap", path, &hash[..8]);
    fs::write(&snap_path, &content).await?;

    let ts = chrono_now();
    let snap = Snapshot {
        id:        uuid_v4(),
        path:      path.to_string(),
        hash:      hash.clone(),
        directive: directive.to_string(),
        timestamp: ts,
    };

    let mut catalog = load_catalog().await.unwrap_or_default();
    catalog.push(snap.clone());
    // Keep newest 200 snapshots
    if catalog.len() > 200 {
        catalog = catalog.into_iter().rev().take(200).rev().collect();
    }
    save_catalog(&catalog).await?;
    Ok(snap)
}

pub async fn restore_snapshot(hash: &str) -> Result<String> {
    let catalog = load_catalog().await?;
    let snap = catalog.iter()
        .find(|s| s.hash.starts_with(hash) || s.id == hash)
        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("Snapshot not found: {}", hash))?;
    let snap_path = format!("{}.{}.snap", snap.path, &snap.hash[..8]);
    let content = fs::read(&snap_path).await?;
    fs::write(&snap.path, content).await?;
    Ok(snap.path.clone())
}

pub async fn list_snapshots() -> Result<Vec<Snapshot>> {
    load_catalog().await
}

async fn load_catalog() -> Result<Vec<Snapshot>> {
    if !Path::new(CATALOG).exists() { return Ok(vec![]); }
    let raw = fs::read_to_string(CATALOG).await?;
    Ok(serde_json::from_str(&raw).unwrap_or_default())
}

async fn save_catalog(catalog: &[Snapshot]) -> Result<()> {
    fs::write(CATALOG, serde_json::to_string_pretty(catalog)?).await?;
    Ok(())
}

fn sha256_hex(data: &[u8]) -> String {
    // Simple FNV-1a 64-bit hash (no sha2 dep required, good enough for snap IDs)
    let mut h: u64 = 14695981039346656037;
    for &b in data { h ^= b as u64; h = h.wrapping_mul(1099511628211); }
    format!("{:016x}", h)
}

fn chrono_now() -> String {
    let secs = std::time::SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .as_secs();
    format!("{}", secs)
}

fn uuid_v4() -> String {
    use std::collections::hash_map::RandomState;
    use std::hash::{BuildHasher, Hasher};
    let a = RandomState::new().build_hasher().finish();
    let b = RandomState::new().build_hasher().finish();
    format!("{:016x}-{:016x}", a, b)
}

//! Logger — initialises tracing subscriber with env-filter and JSON output.
use tracing_subscriber::{fmt, EnvFilter};

pub fn init() {
    let filter = EnvFilter::try_from_default_env()
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| EnvFilter::new("nexus_ide_backend=info,nexus_runtime_core=info,warn"));

    fmt()
        .with_env_filter(filter)
        .with_target(true)
        .with_thread_ids(false)
        .compact()
        .init();

    tracing::info!(">> [LOGGER] Tracing substrate online");
}

use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};
use nexus_runtime_core::{
    neural_agent::MicroAgent,
    cluster::ClusterMesh,
    distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler,
    orchestrator::Orchestrator,
    shared_state::VmoState,
    arbiter_agent::ArbiterAgent,
};
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;

pub mod server;
pub mod auth;
pub mod security;
pub mod system_monitor;
pub mod scheduler;
pub mod ws_bridge;
pub mod vitals_handler;
pub mod cluster_api;

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {

    // --- LAZARUS RECOVERY AND AUTOMATED ROTATION INJECTION ---
    let vmo_broker = std::sync::Arc::new(nexus_substrate::vmo_handler::VmoHandler::new());
    let database_url = std::env::var("DATABASE_URL")
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| "sqlite://nexus_palace.db".to_string());
    if let Ok(pool) = sqlx::SqlitePool::connect(&database_url).await {
        let memory_palace = nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace { pool };
        if let Ok(Some(record)) = memory_palace.restore_provenance().await {
            tracing::warn!(
                "[BOOT] Recovered dead sovereign state timestamp: {}. Re-allocating...",
                record.timestamp
            );
            let _ = vmo_broker.allocate_cognitive_window(&record.payload_blob);
        }
        if let Err(err) = memory_palace.rotate_provenance_ledger(3).await {
            tracing::error!("[BOOT FAULT] Storage rotation failed: {}", err);
        }
    }
    // --- END LAZARUS INJECTION ---

    println!("\x1b[36m>> [NEXUS] IGNITION SEQUENCE INITIATED (v3.0.0)\x1b[0m");
    dotenvy::dotenv().ok();

    let api_key = std::env::var("GEMINI_API_KEY").unwrap_or_else(|_| "UNSET_KEY".to_string());
    let (tx, _rx) = broadcast::channel::<String>(256);

    // Validate JWT key at startup — abort rather than run with a broken auth layer.
    if let Err(e) = crate::auth::load_key_at_startup() {
        eprintln!("\x1b[31m!! [SECURITY] Invalid NEXUS_AUTH_KEY — refusing to start: {}\x1b[0m", e);
        std::process::exit(1);
    }
    if let Err(e) = security::write_startup_token() {
        eprintln!("\x1b[31m!! [SECURITY] Could not write startup token: {}\x1b[0m", e);
    }

    let memory = Arc::new(Mutex::new(MemoryPalace::new("nexus_ide.db").await?));
    let agent = Arc::new(MicroAgent::init(tx.clone())?);
    let monitor = Arc::new(Mutex::new(system_monitor::SystemMonitor::new()));
    let vmo_state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(VmoState::default()));
    let cluster = Arc::new(ClusterMesh::new());
    let dist = Arc::new(DistributedScheduler::new(cluster.clone()));
    let orchestrator = Arc::new(Orchestrator::new());

    scheduler::MasterSupervisor::ignite(
        tx.clone(),
        reqwest::Client::new(),
        api_key.clone(),
        orchestrator.clone(),
        Arc::new(std::sync::atomic::AtomicU32::new(50)),
        system_monitor::SystemMonitor::new(),
        memory.clone(),
        vmo_state.clone(),
        dist.clone(),
    );

    let state = Arc::new(server::AppState {
        client: reqwest::Client::new(),
        monitor,
        agent: agent.clone(),
        orchestrator,
        memory,
        api_key: api_key.into(),
        tx,
        arbiter: Arc::new(ArbiterAgent::new(agent)),
        workspace_root: std::env::current_dir()?,
        vmo_state,
        cluster,
        dist,
    });

    println!("\x1b[32m>> [NEXUS] IDE Backend Online. Core Integrity Verified.\x1b[0m");
    server::start_server(state).await;

    Ok(())
}

use crate::auth;
use axum::{
    body::Body,
    http::{header, Request, StatusCode},
    middleware::Next,
    response::Response,
};

pub async fn sovereign_auth_guard(req: Request<Body>, next: Next) -> Result<Response, StatusCode> {
    if req.uri().path() == "/api/auth/login" {
        return Ok(next.run(req).await);
    }

    let auth_header =
        req.headers().get(header::AUTHORIZATION).and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok()).unwrap_or("");
    let token = auth_header.trim_start_matches("Bearer ").trim();

    if !token.is_empty() {
        match auth::verify_token_detailed(token) {
            Ok(auth::TokenVerdict::Valid) => return Ok(next.run(req).await),
            Ok(verdict) => tracing::warn!(
                "[SECURITY] 401 Unauthorized: Path={} | Verdict={:?}",
                req.uri().path(),
                verdict
            ),
            Err(e) => tracing::error!(
                "[SECURITY] Auth key misconfiguration at {}: {}",
                req.uri().path(),
                e
            ),
        }
    } else {
        tracing::warn!(
            "[SECURITY] 401 Unauthorized: Path={} | No token provided",
            req.uri().path()
        );
    }
    Err(StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED)
}

pub fn write_startup_token() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    use std::io::Write;
    use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;

    let token = auth::issue_sovereign_token()?;
    // ⚡ FIX: Strict 0600 permissions
    std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
        .write(true)
        .create(true)
        .truncate(true)
        .mode(0o600)
        .open(".nexus_ui_token")?
        .write_all(token.as_bytes())?;

    println!("\x1b[32m>> [SECURITY] Startup token written to .nexus_ui_token (0600)\x1b[0m");
    Ok(())
}

[FILE: ./bins/ide_backend/src/self_healing_engine.rs]

//! SelfHealingEngine — polls `cargo check` on a slow interval and, if errors
//! are found, fires a RepoDoctor directive through the agent pipeline.
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};
use tokio::time::{interval, Duration};
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;
use nexus_runtime_core::shared_state::VmoState;
use nexus_runtime_core::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler;

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [SELF_HEALING] Suture engine initialised");
}

pub fn spawn(
    orchestrator: Arc<nexus_runtime_core::orchestrator::Orchestrator>,
    client: reqwest::Client,
    api_key: String,
    memory: Arc<Mutex<MemoryPalace>>,
    vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
    tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
    dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
) {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        // First run after 5 minutes, then every 20 minutes
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(5 * 60)).await;
        let mut ticker = interval(Duration::from_secs(20 * 60));
        loop {
            ticker.tick().await;
            tracing::info!("[SELF_HEALING] Running cargo check…");

            let output = tokio::process::Command::new("cargo")
                .args(["check", "--message-format=short", "--quiet"])
                .output()
                .await;

            match output {
                Ok(out) if out.status.success() => {
                    tracing::info!("[SELF_HEALING] Substrate integrity verified ✓");
                    let _ = tx.send(
                        serde_json::json!({"type":"LOG_EVENT","payload":"[HEAL] cargo check: PASS"})
                            .to_string()
                    );
                }
                Ok(out) => {
                    let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr);
                    tracing::warn!("[SELF_HEALING] Errors detected — triggering RepoDoctor");
                    let _ = tx.send(
                        serde_json::json!({"type":"LOG_EVENT",
                            "payload": format!("[HEAL] cargo check FAILED — engaging RepoDoctor")})
                            .to_string()
                    );
                    let directive = format!(
                        "diagnose and fix the following cargo check errors:\n{}",
                        &stderr[..stderr.len().min(2000)]
                    );
                    nexus_runtime_core::task_manager::send_task(
                        orchestrator.clone(), &client, &api_key, &directive,
                        memory.clone(), vmo_state.clone(), tx.clone(), dist.clone()
                    ).await;
                }
                Err(e) => tracing::warn!("[SELF_HEALING] Could not run cargo check: {}", e),
            }
        }
    });
}

use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;
use axum::{
    extract::{State, Query},
    http::{header, StatusCode, Uri},
    response::IntoResponse,
    routing::{get, post},
    Router, Json,
};
use rust_embed::RustEmbed;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::net::TcpListener;
use nexus_runtime_core::shared_state::VmoState;
use serde_json::json;
use serde::Deserialize;
use secrecy::{ExposeSecret, SecretString};

use crate::{ws_bridge, vitals_handler, cluster_api};

#[derive(RustEmbed)]
#[folder = "ui/dist/"] 
struct Assets;

pub struct AppState {
    pub client:             reqwest::Client,
    pub monitor:            Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<crate::system_monitor::SystemMonitor>>,
    pub agent:              Arc<nexus_runtime_core::neural_agent::MicroAgent>,
    pub orchestrator:       Arc<nexus_runtime_core::orchestrator::Orchestrator>,
    pub memory:             Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<MemoryPalace>>,
    pub api_key:            SecretString,
    pub tx:                 tokio::sync::broadcast::Sender<String>,
    pub arbiter:            Arc<nexus_runtime_core::arbiter_agent::ArbiterAgent>,
    pub workspace_root:     PathBuf,
    pub vmo_state:          Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<VmoState>>,
    pub cluster:            Arc<nexus_runtime_core::cluster::ClusterMesh>,
    pub dist:               Arc<nexus_runtime_core::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler>,
}

#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct Intent {
    pub directive: Option<String>,
}

pub async fn unified_gateway(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Json(intent): Json<Intent>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let directive_str = intent.directive.unwrap_or_default();
    let s = state.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let d = nexus_runtime_core::cli_utils::inject_workspace_dna(&directive_str).await;
        nexus_runtime_core::task_manager::send_task(
            s.orchestrator.clone(),
            nexus_runtime_core::task_manager::TaskContext {
                client: s.client.clone(),
                api_key: s.api_key.expose_secret().to_string(),
                memory: s.memory.clone(),
                vmo_state: s.vmo_state.clone(),
                tx: s.tx.clone(),
                dist: s.dist.clone(),
            },
            &d,
        ).await;
    });
    (StatusCode::OK, Json(json!({ "status": "QUEUED" }))).into_response()
}

async fn metrics_handler() -> impl IntoResponse {
    let text = nexus_runtime_core::metrics::get_metrics_text();
    ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "text/plain")], text)
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct FsQuery { path: Option<String> }

fn safe_path(workspace_root: &Path, user_path: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, (StatusCode, Json<serde_json::Value>)> {
    if user_path.is_empty() { return Err((StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, Json(json!({ "error": "PATH_EMPTY" })))); }
    let path = std::path::Path::new(user_path);
    if path.is_absolute() || path.components().any(|c| matches!(c, std::path::Component::ParentDir)) {
        return Err((StatusCode::FORBIDDEN, Json(json!({ "error": "PATH_TRAVERSAL_DENIED" }))));
    }
    Ok(workspace_root.join(path))
}

pub async fn fs_read_handler(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Query(q): Query<FsQuery>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let user_path = q.path.unwrap_or_default();
    match safe_path(&state.workspace_root, &user_path) {
        Err(e) => e.into_response(),
        Ok(safe) => match tokio::fs::read_to_string(&safe).await {
            Ok(content) => Json(json!({ "content": content })).into_response(),
            Err(e) => (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, Json(json!({ "error": e.to_string() }))).into_response(),
        },
    }
}

pub async fn fs_write_handler(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Json(payload): Json<serde_json::Value>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let user_path = payload["path"].as_str().unwrap_or_default();
    let content   = payload["content"].as_str().unwrap_or_default();
    match safe_path(&state.workspace_root, user_path) {
        Err(e) => e.into_response(),
        Ok(safe) => {
            if let Some(parent) = safe.parent() {
                let _ = tokio::fs::create_dir_all(parent).await;
            }
            match tokio::fs::write(&safe, content).await {
                Ok(_) => Json(json!({ "status": "success" })).into_response(),
                Err(e) => (StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, Json(json!({ "error": e.to_string() }))).into_response(),
            }
        }
    }
}

pub async fn fs_list_handler(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Query(q): Query<FsQuery>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let user_path = q.path.unwrap_or_else(|| ".".into());
    match safe_path(&state.workspace_root, &user_path) {
        Err(e) => e.into_response(),
        Ok(safe) => {
            let mut entries = Vec::new();
            if let Ok(mut read_dir) = tokio::fs::read_dir(&safe).await {
                while let Ok(Some(entry)) = read_dir.next_entry().await {
                    let meta = entry.metadata().await.ok();
                    entries.push(json!({
                        "name": entry.file_name().to_string_lossy(),
                        "path": entry.path().strip_prefix(&state.workspace_root).unwrap_or(&entry.path()).to_string_lossy(),
                        "is_dir": meta.map(|m| m.is_dir()).unwrap_or(false)
                    }));
                }
            }
            Json(entries).into_response()
        }
    }
}

pub async fn start_server(state: Arc<AppState>) {
    let app = Router::new()
        .route("/api/task", post(unified_gateway))
        .route("/api/metrics", get(metrics_handler))
        .route("/api/fs/read", get(fs_read_handler))
        .route("/api/fs/write", post(fs_write_handler))
        .route("/api/fs/list", get(fs_list_handler))
        .route("/api/vitals", get(vitals_handler::handle_vitals))
        .route("/api/cluster/nodes", get(cluster_api::list_nodes))
        .route("/ws/telemetry", get(ws_bridge::ws_telemetry_handler))
        .fallback(static_handler)
        .with_state(state);
        
    let listener = TcpListener::bind("0.0.0.0:3000").await.unwrap();
    axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap();
}

async fn static_handler(uri: Uri) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let path = uri.path().trim_start_matches('/').to_string();
    let final_path = if path.is_empty() { "index.html".to_string() } else { path };
    
    match Assets::get(&final_path) {
        Some(content) => {
            let mime = mime_guess::from_path(&final_path).first_or_octet_stream();
            ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, mime.as_ref())], content.data).into_response()
        }
        None => match Assets::get("index.html") {
            Some(content) => ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "text/html")], content.data).into_response(),
            None => StatusCode::NOT_FOUND.into_response(),
        },
    }
}

pub struct Vitals {
    pub cpu_load: f32,
    pub ram_gb: f32,
    pub total_ram_gb: f32,
    pub temperature: f32,

pub struct SystemMonitor {
    pub cpu_load: f32,
}

    pub fn get_vitals(&mut self) -> Vitals {
        Vitals { 
            cpu_load: self.cpu_load, 
            ram_gb: 2.0, 
            total_ram_gb: 4.0,

        }
    }
}

//! TelemetryBridge — reads real system vitals every 3 s and broadcasts
//! them as a JSON heartbeat that the frontend's store.svelte.ts consumes.
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};
use tokio::time::{interval, Duration};

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [TELEMETRY] Bridge initialised");
}

pub fn spawn(
    tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
    monitor: Arc<Mutex<crate::system_monitor::SystemMonitor>>,
    resonance: Arc<std::sync::atomic::AtomicU32>,
) {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut ticker = interval(Duration::from_secs(3));
        loop {
            ticker.tick().await;
            let vitals = {
                let mut m = monitor.lock().await;
                m.get_vitals()
            };
            let res = resonance.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed) as f32 / 1000.0;
            let active_vmos = unsafe {
                nexus_substrate::kernel::ffi::muscle_get_active_vmos()
            };

            let msg = serde_json::json!({
                "type":        "VITALS",
                "cpu_load":    vitals.cpu_load,
                "ram_gb":      vitals.ram_gb,
                "total_ram_gb": vitals.total_ram_gb,
                "temperature": vitals.temperature,
                "resonance":   res,
                "active_vmos": active_vmos,
            });
            let _ = tx.send(msg.to_string());
        }
    });
}

[FILE: ./bins/ide_backend/src/thermal_governor.rs]
========================================
//! ThermalGovernor — watches CPU temperature and reduces agent turn-rate
//! when the substrate is running hot (>70°C → half-speed, >80°C → pause).
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU32, Ordering};
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::time::{interval, Duration};

static THROTTLED: AtomicBool  = AtomicBool::new(false);
static PAUSED:    AtomicBool  = AtomicBool::new(false);
static TEMP_C:    AtomicU32   = AtomicU32::new(0);

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [THERMAL] Governor initialised");
}

pub fn is_throttled() -> bool { THROTTLED.load(Ordering::Relaxed) }
pub fn is_paused()    -> bool { PAUSED.load(Ordering::Relaxed) }
pub fn current_temp() -> f32  { TEMP_C.load(Ordering::Relaxed) as f32 }

pub fn spawn() {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut ticker = interval(Duration::from_secs(10));
        let mut sys = sysinfo::System::new_all();
        loop {
            ticker.tick().await;
            sys.refresh_all();

            // sysinfo returns temperatures in °C as f32
            let max_temp = sys.components()
                .iter()
                .map(|c| c.temperature())
                .filter(|t| t.is_finite())
                .fold(0f32, f32::max);

            TEMP_C.store(max_temp as u32, Ordering::Relaxed);
            THROTTLED.store(max_temp > 70.0, Ordering::Relaxed);
            PAUSED.store(max_temp > 80.0, Ordering::Relaxed);

            if max_temp > 75.0 {
                tracing::warn!("[THERMAL] Temperature {}°C — throttling agent pipeline", max_temp);
            }
        }
    });
}

[FILE: ./bins/ide_backend/src/vitals_handler.rs]

use crate::server::AppState;
use axum::{extract::State, response::IntoResponse, Json};
use serde_json::json;
use std::sync::Arc;

pub async fn handle_vitals(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let mut monitor = state.monitor.lock().await;
    let v: crate::system_monitor::Vitals = monitor.get_vitals();
    Json(json!({
        "cpu_load":     v.cpu_load,
        "ram_gb":       v.ram_gb,
        "total_ram_gb": v.total_ram_gb,
        "temperature":  v.temperature,
    }))
}

[FILE: ./bins/ide_backend/src/ws_bridge.rs]
========================================
use crate::server::AppState;
use axum::{
    extract::ws::{Message, WebSocket, WebSocketUpgrade},
    extract::State,
    response::Response,
};
use std::sync::Arc;

pub async fn ws_telemetry_handler(
    ws: WebSocketUpgrade,
    State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>,
) -> Response {
    ws.on_upgrade(move |socket| handle_socket(socket, state))
}

async fn handle_socket(mut socket: WebSocket, state: Arc<AppState>) {
    let mut rx = state.tx.subscribe();
    while let Ok(msg) = rx.recv().await {
        if socket.send(Message::Text(msg)).await.is_err() {
            break;
        }
    }
}

// uplink.js — v2.2 UPGRADED
import { auth } from '$lib/stores/auth.svelte';

const TIMEOUT_MS = 15000;

function timeoutPromise(ms) {
    return new Promise((_, reject) => setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('Request timeout')), ms));
}

async function request(url, options = {}) {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const headers = {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'Authorization': auth.token ?? 'SOVEREIGN',
        ...options.headers,
    };

    try {
        const res = await Promise.race([
            fetch(url, { ...options, headers, signal: controller.signal }),
            timeoutPromise(TIMEOUT_MS)
        ]);
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status} — ${url}`);
        return res;
    } catch (e) {
        controller.abort();
        throw e;
    }
}

export const uplink = {
    fetch: (url, opts = {}) => request(url, opts),
    post: (url, body, signal) => request(url, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body), signal }),
    // ... other methods
    async postJSON(url, body) {
        const res = await request(url, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body) });
        return res.json();
    }
};

use ed25519_dalek::SigningKey;
use rand::rngs::OsRng;

pub struct Identity {
    pub key: SigningKey,
}

impl Identity {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        let mut csprng = OsRng;
        let key = SigningKey::generate(&mut csprng);
        Self { key }
    }
}

impl Default for Identity {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

[FILE: ./crates/nexus_substrate/build.rs]
========================================
use std::env;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::process::Command;
use std::fs;

fn main() {
    let out_dir = PathBuf::from(env::var("OUT_DIR").unwrap());
    let manifest_dir = env::current_dir().unwrap();
    let zig_src = manifest_dir.join("src/kernel/muscle.zig");
    
    println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed=src/kernel/muscle.zig");

    let temp_lib = manifest_dir.join("libmuscle.a");
    
    let status = Command::new("zig")
        .args([
            "build-lib",
            "-target", "aarch64-linux-gnu",
            "-O", "ReleaseSmall",
            "-lc",
            "-fPIC",
            zig_src.to_str().unwrap(),
            &format!("-femit-bin={}", temp_lib.to_str().unwrap()),
        ])
        .status()
        .expect("Failed to execute zig");

    if !status.success() {
        panic!("Zig compilation failed");
    }

    let dest_lib = out_dir.join("libmuscle.a");
    fs::copy(&temp_lib, &dest_lib).expect("Failed to copy libmuscle.a");

    println!("cargo:rustc-link-search=native={}", out_dir.display());
    println!("cargo:rustc-link-arg=-Wl,--whole-archive");
    println!("cargo:rustc-link-lib=static=muscle");
    println!("cargo:rustc-link-arg=-Wl,--no-whole-archive");
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum KernelError {
    VmoCloneFailed { parent: u32, size: usize },
    InvalidArgs,
    RegistryFull,
    AlignmentAnomaly { address: usize },
}

impl std::fmt::Display for KernelError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{:?}", self)
    }
}
impl std::error::Error for KernelError {}

[FILE: ./crates/nexus_substrate/src/kernel/ffi.rs]

#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct VmoInfo {
    pub handle: u32,
    pub size: usize,
    pub committed_pages: usize,
    pub is_cow: u8,
    pub parent_handle: u32,
}

#[link(name = "muscle")]
extern "C" {
    pub fn zx_vmo_create_native(size: usize) -> u32;
    pub fn zx_vmo_clone_native(parent_handle: u32, size: usize) -> u32;
    pub fn zx_handle_close_native(handle: u32);
    pub fn zx_vmo_write_native(handle: u32, offset: usize, buffer: *const u8, size: usize) -> i32;
    pub fn zx_vmo_read_native(handle: u32, offset: usize, buffer: *mut u8, size: usize) -> i32;
    pub fn muscle_get_active_vmos() -> u32;
    pub fn muscle_get_next_handle() -> u32;
    pub fn set_substrate_load(load: f32);
    pub fn set_stress_mode(enabled: bool);
    pub fn extract_thermal_pulse_raw() -> i32;
    pub fn zx_vmo_get_info(handle: u32, out_info: *mut VmoInfo) -> i32;
    pub fn zx_vmo_get_all_handles(out_handles: *mut u32, max_count: usize) -> usize;
    pub fn zx_vmo_calculate_entropy(handle: u32) -> f32;
}

pub trait KernelOps: Send + Sync {
    fn vmo_create(&self, size: usize) -> u32;
    fn vmo_clone(&self, parent_handle: u32, size: usize) -> u32;
    fn handle_close(&self, handle: u32);
    /// # Safety
    /// The caller must ensure `out_info` is a valid, properly aligned pointer to a `VmoInfo` struct.
    unsafe fn vmo_get_info(&self, handle: u32, out_info: *mut VmoInfo) -> i32;
    fn set_stress(&self, enabled: bool);
    fn calculate_entropy(&self, handle: u32) -> f32;
}

pub struct NativeKernelOps;
impl KernelOps for NativeKernelOps {
    fn vmo_create(&self, size: usize) -> u32 {
        unsafe { zx_vmo_create_native(size) }
    }

    fn vmo_clone(&self, p: u32, s: usize) -> u32 {
        unsafe { zx_vmo_clone_native(p, s) }
    }

    fn handle_close(&self, h: u32) {
        unsafe {
            zx_handle_close_native(h);
        }
    }

    /// # Safety
    /// The caller must ensure `out_info` is a valid, properly aligned pointer to a `VmoInfo` struct.
    unsafe fn vmo_get_info(&self, h: u32, i: *mut VmoInfo) -> i32 {
        zx_vmo_get_info(h, i)
    }

    fn set_stress(&self, enabled: bool) {
        unsafe {
            set_stress_mode(enabled);
        }
    }

    fn calculate_entropy(&self, h: u32) -> f32 {
        unsafe { zx_vmo_calculate_entropy(h) }
    }
}

    Lazy::new(|| RwLock::new(HashMap::new()));

[FILE: ./crates/nexus_substrate/src/kernel/muscle.zig]
========================================
//! NEXUS SINGULARITY — ZIG MUSCLE v4.4.2 [STABILIZED]
const std = @import("std");
const posix = std.posix;
const Thread = std.Thread;

// CONSTANTS (Defining these at the top ensures visibility)
const PAGE_SIZE: usize = 4096;
const MAX_SLOTS: usize = 4096;
const SLOT_MASK: u32 = MAX_SLOTS - 1;
const SENTINEL_MAGIC: u32 = 0xDEADC0DE;
const GENERATION_SHIFT: u5 = 12;
const SHARD_COUNT: usize = 16;
const SHARD_MASK: u32 = SHARD_COUNT - 1;

// STRUCTS
pub const VmoInfo = extern struct {
    handle: u32,
    size: usize,
    committed_pages: usize,
    is_cow: u8,
    parent_handle: u32,
};

pub const VmoMetadata = struct {
    ptr:[*]align(PAGE_SIZE) u8,
    size: usize,
    magic: u32,
    generation: u32,
    refcount: u32,
    is_cow_child: bool,
    parent_handle: u32,
};

// GLOBAL STATE
var shard_mutexes:[SHARD_COUNT]Thread.Mutex = [_]Thread.Mutex{.{}} ** SHARD_COUNT;
var active_vmos: u32 = 0;
var next_handle: u32 = 0xc8;
var handle_table: [MAX_SLOTS]?VmoMetadata = [_]?VmoMetadata{null} ** MAX_SLOTS;
var generation_table: [MAX_SLOTS]u20 = [_]u20{1} ** MAX_SLOTS;
var current_load: f32 = 0.0;
var stress_mode: bool = false;

// INLINES
inline fn shard_for(h: u32) usize { return @intCast(h & SHARD_MASK); }
inline fn decode_slot(h: u32) usize { return @intCast(h & SLOT_MASK); }
inline fn decode_generation(h: u32) u32 { return h >> GENERATION_SHIFT; }

// EXPORTS
pub export fn zx_vmo_get_info(h: u32, out_info: *VmoInfo) callconv(.c) i32 {
    const slot = decode_slot(h);
    const gen = decode_generation(h);
    const meta = handle_table[slot] orelse return -1;
    if (meta.magic != SENTINEL_MAGIC or meta.generation != gen) return -1;
    
    out_info.* = .{
        .handle = h,
        .size = @intCast(meta.size),
        .committed_pages = @intCast(meta.size / PAGE_SIZE),
        .is_cow = if (meta.is_cow_child) 1 else 0,
        .parent_handle = meta.parent_handle,
    };
    return 0;
}

pub export fn zx_vmo_calculate_entropy(h: u32) callconv(.c) f32 {
    const slot = decode_slot(h);
    const gen = decode_generation(h);
    const meta = handle_table[slot] orelse return 0.0;
    if (meta.magic != SENTINEL_MAGIC or meta.generation != gen) return 0.0;
    
    var counts = [_]u32{0} ** 256;
    for (meta.ptr[0..meta.size]) |byte| counts[byte] += 1;
    var entropy: f32 = 0.0;
    const size_f: f32 = @floatFromInt(meta.size);
    for (counts) |count| {
        if (count > 0) {
            const p = @as(f32, @floatFromInt(count)) / size_f;
            entropy -= p * std.math.log2(p);
        }
    }
    return entropy / 8.0;
}

pub export fn zx_vmo_get_all_handles(out_handles: [*]u32, max_count: usize) callconv(.c) usize {
    var count: usize = 0;
    for (handle_table, 0..) |maybe_meta, i| {
        if (maybe_meta) |meta| {
            if (meta.magic == SENTINEL_MAGIC and count < max_count) {
                out_handles[count] = @as(u32, @intCast(i)) | (@as(u32, meta.generation) << GENERATION_SHIFT);
                count += 1;
            }
        }
    }
    return count;
}

pub export fn muscle_get_active_vmos() callconv(.c) u32 { return @atomicLoad(u32, &active_vmos, .monotonic); }
========================================
[FILE: ./crates/nexus_substrate/src/kernel/safe_kernel.rs]
========================================
use crate::kernel::error::KernelError;
use crate::kernel::ffi;

pub struct SafeKernel;

impl SafeKernel {
    pub fn create_vmo(size: usize) -> Result<u32, KernelError> {
        if size == 0 || size > 16_777_216 {
            return Err(KernelError::InvalidArgs);
        }
        let handle = unsafe { ffi::zx_vmo_create_native(size) };
        if handle == 0 {
            return Err(KernelError::InvalidArgs);
        }
        Ok(handle)
    }

    pub fn close_handle(handle: u32) {
        unsafe { ffi::zx_handle_close_native(handle) }
    }
}

[FILE: ./crates/nexus_substrate/src/kernel/safe_vmo.rs]

use crate::kernel::error::KernelError;
use crate::kernel::ffi;
use crate::kernel::{register_handle, HandleType};

pub struct SafeVmo {
    handle: u32,
    pub size: usize,
}

impl SafeVmo {
    pub fn new(size: usize) -> Result<Self, KernelError> {
        let handle = unsafe { ffi::zx_vmo_create_native(size) };
        if handle == 0 {
            return Err(KernelError::InvalidArgs);
        }
        register_handle(handle, HandleType::Vmo(handle));
        Ok(Self { handle, size })
    }

    pub fn write(&self, offset: usize, data: &[u8]) -> Result<(), KernelError> {
        if offset + data.len() > self.size {
            return Err(KernelError::InvalidArgs);
        }
        let status =
            unsafe { ffi::zx_vmo_write_native(self.handle, offset, data.as_ptr(), data.len()) };
        if status == 0 {
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err(KernelError::InvalidArgs)
        }
    }

    /// ⚡ ZERO-ALLOCATION READ FIX: Prevents heap fragmentation on the 4GB Duet
    pub fn read_into(&self, offset: usize, buffer: &mut [u8]) -> Result<(), KernelError> {
        if offset + buffer.len() > self.size {
            return Err(KernelError::InvalidArgs);
        }
        let status = unsafe {
            ffi::zx_vmo_read_native(self.handle, offset, buffer.as_mut_ptr(), buffer.len())
        };
        if status == 0 {
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err(KernelError::InvalidArgs)
        }
    }

    pub fn handle(&self) -> u32 {
        self.handle
    }
}

impl Drop for SafeVmo {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        unsafe { ffi::zx_handle_close_native(self.handle) };
    }
}
