use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use sqlx::{sqlite::SqlitePoolOptions, FromRow, Row, SqlitePool};
use std::path::Path;

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct AgentMemory {
    pub id: i64,
    pub origin: String,
    pub directive: String,
    pub result: String,
    pub timestamp: String,
}

#[derive(FromRow, Debug, Clone)]
pub struct SutureRecoveryRecord {
    pub vmo_handle_id: i64,
    pub payload_blob: Vec<u8>,
    pub timestamp: i64,
}

pub struct MemoryPalace {
    pub pool: SqlitePool,
}

impl MemoryPalace {
    pub async fn new(db_path: &str) -> Result<Self, sqlx::Error> {
        if !Path::new(db_path).exists() {
            std::fs::File::create(db_path).ok();
        }
        let pool = SqlitePoolOptions::new()
            .max_connections(5)
            .connect(&format!("sqlite:{}", db_path))
            .await?;
        sqlx::query("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;").execute(&pool).await?;
        sqlx::query("PRAGMA cache_size=-16000;").execute(&pool).await?;
        sqlx::query("PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL;").execute(&pool).await?;
        let palace = Self { pool };
        palace.init_full_schema().await?;
        Ok(palace)
    }

    pub async fn init_full_schema(&self) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
        sqlx::query(
            "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS neural_context (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                origin TEXT NOT NULL,
                directive TEXT NOT NULL,
                result TEXT NOT NULL,
                embedding TEXT,
                timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            );",
        )
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        sqlx::query(
            "CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory_search USING fts5(
                directive, result, content='neural_context', content_rowid='id'
            );",
        )
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        sqlx::query(
            "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS system_insights (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                summary TEXT NOT NULL,
                timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            );",
        )
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        // New table for provenance/suture records
        sqlx::query(
            "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cognition_provenance (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                vmo_handle_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                payload_blob BLOB NOT NULL,
                timestamp INTEGER NOT NULL,
                thermal_at_suture REAL,
                reason TEXT NOT NULL
            );",
        )
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn store_memory(
        &self,
        origin: &str,
        directive: &str,
        result: &str,
        embedding: Option<Vec<f32>>,
    ) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
        let emb_str = embedding.map(|v| serde_json::to_string(&v).unwrap_or_default());
        let mut tx = self.pool.begin().await?;
        let res = sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO neural_context (origin, directive, result, embedding) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
        )
        .bind(origin)
        .bind(directive)
        .bind(result)
        .bind(emb_str)
        .execute(&mut *tx)
        .await?;
        let row_id = res.last_insert_rowid();
        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO memory_search (rowid, directive, result) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
        )
        .bind(row_id)
        .bind(directive)
        .bind(result)
        .execute(&mut *tx)
        .await?;
        tx.commit().await?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn store_insight(&self, summary: &str) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
        sqlx::query("INSERT INTO system_insights (summary) VALUES (?)")
            .bind(summary)
            .execute(&self.pool)
            .await?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn fetch_all(&self) -> Result<Vec<AgentMemory>, sqlx::Error> {
        let rows = sqlx::query(
            "SELECT id, origin, directive, result, timestamp FROM neural_context ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 50",
        )
        .fetch_all(&self.pool)
        .await?;
        Ok(rows
            .into_iter()
            .map(|row| AgentMemory {
                id: row.get("id"),
                origin: row.get("origin"),
                directive: row.get("directive"),
                result: row.get("result"),
                timestamp: row.get("timestamp"),
            })
            .collect())
    }

    pub async fn search_memories(&self, query: &str) -> Result<Vec<AgentMemory>, sqlx::Error> {
        let rows = sqlx::query(
            "SELECT n.id, n.origin, n.directive, n.result, n.timestamp
             FROM neural_context n JOIN memory_search m ON n.id = m.rowid
             WHERE memory_search MATCH ? ORDER BY rank LIMIT 10",
        )
        .bind(query)
        .fetch_all(&self.pool)
        .await?;
        Ok(rows
            .into_iter()
            .map(|row| AgentMemory {
                id: row.get(0),
                origin: row.get(1),
                directive: row.get(2),
                result: row.get(3),
                timestamp: row.get(4),
            })
            .collect())
    }

    pub async fn semantic_search(&self, query_vec: &[f32]) -> Result<Vec<AgentMemory>, sqlx::Error> {
        let rows = sqlx::query(
            "SELECT id, origin, directive, result, timestamp, embedding FROM neural_context WHERE embedding IS NOT NULL",
        )
        .fetch_all(&self.pool)
        .await?;
        let mut scored = Vec::new();
        for row in rows {
            let emb_str: Option<String> = row.get("embedding");
            if let Some(s) = emb_str {
                if let Ok(vec) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<f32>>(&s) {
                    let score = crate::vector_bridge::cosine_similarity(query_vec, &vec);
                    scored.push((score, row));
                }
            }
        }
        scored.sort_by(|a, b| b.0.partial_cmp(&a.0).unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal));
        scored.truncate(3);
        Ok(scored
            .into_iter()
            .map(|(_, row)| AgentMemory {
                id: row.get("id"),
                origin: row.get("origin"),
                directive: row.get("directive"),
                result: row.get("result"),
                timestamp: row.get("timestamp"),
            })
            .collect())
    }

    pub async fn purge_all(&self) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
        sqlx::query("DELETE FROM neural_context").execute(&self.pool).await?;
        sqlx::query("DELETE FROM memory_search").execute(&self.pool).await?;
        sqlx::query("DELETE FROM system_insights").execute(&self.pool).await?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn fetch_random_strands(&self, limit: i64) -> Result<Vec<AgentMemory>, sqlx::Error> {
        let rows = sqlx::query(
            "SELECT id, origin, directive, result, timestamp FROM neural_context ORDER BY RANDOM() LIMIT ?",
        )
        .bind(limit)
        .fetch_all(&self.pool)
        .await?;
        Ok(rows
            .into_iter()
            .map(|row| AgentMemory {
                id: row.get("id"),
                origin: row.get("origin"),
                directive: row.get("directive"),
                result: row.get("result"),
                timestamp: row.get("timestamp"),
            })
            .collect())
    }

    // ── New provenance/suture methods ────────────────────────────────
    pub async fn save_provenance(
        &self,
        handle: i64,
        payload: Vec<u8>,
        ts: u64,
    ) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO cognition_provenance (vmo_handle_id, payload_blob, timestamp, thermal_at_suture, reason)
             VALUES (?, ?, ?, 78.0, 'EMERGENCY_SUTURE')",
        )
        .bind(handle)
        .bind(payload)
        .bind(ts as i64)
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn restore_provenance(&self) -> Result<Option<SutureRecoveryRecord>, sqlx::Error> {
        sqlx::query_as::<_, SutureRecoveryRecord>(
            "SELECT vmo_handle_id, payload_blob, timestamp FROM cognition_provenance
             WHERE reason = 'EMERGENCY_SUTURE' ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT 1",
        )
        .fetch_optional(&self.pool)
        .await
    }

    pub async fn rotate_provenance_ledger(&self, max_retain: u32) -> Result<u64, sqlx::Error> {
        let result = sqlx::query(
            "DELETE FROM cognition_provenance
             WHERE reason = 'EMERGENCY_SUTURE'
             AND timestamp < (
                 SELECT MIN(timestamp) FROM (
                     SELECT timestamp FROM cognition_provenance
                     WHERE reason = 'EMERGENCY_SUTURE'
                     ORDER BY timestamp DESC
                     LIMIT ?
                 )
             )",
        )
        .bind(max_retain as i64)
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;
        let rows = result.rows_affected();
        if rows > 0 {
            tracing::warn!(
                "[MEMORY PALACE] Storage rotation complete. Recycled {} stale eMMC rows.",
                rows
            );
        }
        Ok(rows)
    }
}

use anyhow::Result;
use sqlx::{sqlite::SqlitePoolOptions, SqlitePool};

pub struct SqliteStore {
    pool: SqlitePool,
}

impl SqliteStore {
    pub async fn new(path: &str) -> Result<Self> {
        let pool = SqlitePoolOptions::new()
            .max_connections(5)
            .connect(&format!("sqlite:{}", path))
            .await?;

        sqlx::query("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;").execute(&pool).await?;
        sqlx::query("PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL;").execute(&pool).await?;

        sqlx::query(
            "
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events (
                seq INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                kind TEXT NOT NULL,
                object_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                ts DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            );
        ",
        )
        .execute(&pool)
        .await?;

        sqlx::query(
            "
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS artifacts (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                payload BLOB NOT NULL
            );
        ",
        )
        .execute(&pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(Self { pool })
    }

    pub async fn append_event(&self, kind: &str, object_id: &str) -> Result<()> {
        sqlx::query("INSERT INTO events (kind, object_id) VALUES (?, ?)")
            .bind(kind)
            .bind(object_id)
            .execute(&self.pool)
            .await?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

[FILE: ./crates/nexus_substrate/src/token_ledger.rs]

use once_cell::sync::Lazy;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Mutex;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant, SystemTime};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ModelQuota {
    pub rpm_limit: u32,
    pub tpm_limit: u32,
}

impl ModelQuota {
    fn for_model(model: &str) -> Self {
        match model {
                        m if m.contains("flash") => Self { rpm_limit: 2, tpm_limit: 60_000 },
            m if m.contains("31b")   => Self { rpm_limit: 3, tpm_limit: 50_000 },
            m if m.contains("26b")   => Self { rpm_limit: 3, tpm_limit: 50_000 },
            m if m.contains("27b")   => Self { rpm_limit: 4, tpm_limit: 8_000 },
            _                        => Self { rpm_limit: 3, tpm_limit: 30_000 },

        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct WindowEntry {
    timestamp: Instant,
    tokens: u32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
struct ModelWindow {
    entries: Vec<WindowEntry>,
}

impl ModelWindow {
    fn purge_stale(&mut self) {
        let cutoff = Instant::now() - Duration::from_secs(60);
        self.entries.retain(|e| e.timestamp > cutoff);
    }
    fn token_sum(&self) -> u32 {
        self.entries.iter().map(|e| e.tokens).sum()
    }
    fn ms_until_slot_free(&self) -> u64 {
        if self.entries.is_empty() {
            return 0;
        }
        let oldest = self.entries.iter().map(|e| e.timestamp).min().unwrap();
        (Duration::from_secs(60).saturating_sub(oldest.elapsed())).as_millis() as u64 + 100
    }
}

pub struct TokenLedger {
    windows: Mutex<HashMap<String, ModelWindow>>,
}

impl TokenLedger {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { windows: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()) }
    }

    pub fn check_and_reserve(&self, model: &str, estimated_tokens: u32) -> Result<(), u64> {
        let quota = ModelQuota::for_model(model);
        let mut map = self.windows.lock().unwrap();
        let win = map.entry(model.to_string()).or_default();
        win.purge_stale();
        if win.entries.len() as u32 >= quota.rpm_limit {
            return Err(win.ms_until_slot_free());
        }
        if win.token_sum() + estimated_tokens > quota.tpm_limit {
            return Err(win.ms_until_slot_free());
        }
        win.entries.push(WindowEntry { timestamp: Instant::now(), tokens: estimated_tokens });
        Ok(())
    }
}

pub static LEDGER: Lazy<TokenLedger> = Lazy::new(TokenLedger::new);

/// Returns Ok(()) when a quota slot is reserved.
/// Returns Err(message) after MAX_ATTEMPTS — caller MUST NOT fire the API on Err.
pub async fn wait_for_quota(model: &str, estimated_tokens: u32) -> Result<(), String> {
    const MAX_ATTEMPTS: usize = 10;
    for attempt in 0..MAX_ATTEMPTS {
        match LEDGER.check_and_reserve(model, estimated_tokens) {
            Ok(()) => return Ok(()),
            Err(wait_ms) => {
                let jitter = generate_seed() % 500;
                tracing::warn!(
                    "[LEDGER] {} ceiling hit — waiting {}ms (attempt {}/{}).",
                    model,
                    wait_ms + jitter,
                    attempt + 1,
                    MAX_ATTEMPTS
                );
                tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(wait_ms + jitter)).await;
            }
        }
    }
    Err(format!("[LEDGER] Quota for '{}' unavailable after {} attempts.", model, MAX_ATTEMPTS))
}

fn http_rpm_limit() -> u32 {
    std::env::var("NEXUS_HTTP_RPM").ok().and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(60)
}

static HTTP_WINDOWS: Lazy<Mutex<HashMap<String, Vec<Instant>>>> =
    Lazy::new(|| Mutex::new(HashMap::new()));

pub fn http_check(ip: &str) -> Result<(), u64> {
    let limit = http_rpm_limit();
    let cutoff = Instant::now() - Duration::from_secs(60);
    let mut map = HTTP_WINDOWS.lock().unwrap();
    let win = map.entry(ip.to_string()).or_default();
    win.retain(|&ts| ts > cutoff);
    if win.len() as u32 >= limit {
        let oldest = *win.first().unwrap();
        return Err(
            (Duration::from_secs(60).saturating_sub(oldest.elapsed())).as_millis() as u64 + 100
        );
    }
    win.push(Instant::now());
    Ok(())
}

fn generate_seed() -> u64 {
    use std::hash::{BuildHasher, Hasher};
    let t = SystemTime::now().duration_since(SystemTime::UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_nanos()
        as u64;
    let mut h = std::collections::hash_map::RandomState::new().build_hasher();
    h.write_u64(t);
    h.finish()
}

impl Default for TokenLedger {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

use anyhow::Result;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::json;

pub async fn get_embedding(api_key: &str, text: &str) -> Result<Vec<f32>> {
    // wait_for_quota is an async fn that returns ()
    let _ = crate::token_ledger::wait_for_quota("gemini-embedding-2", 500).await;

    let client = Client::new();

    let url = "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-embedding-2:embedContent";

    let payload = json!({
        "model": "models/gemini-embedding-2",
        "content": { "parts": [{ "text": text }] }
    });

    let res = client
        .post(url)
        .header("x-goog-api-key", api_key)
        .json(&payload)
        .send()
        .await?;
    let body: serde_json::Value = res.json().await?;

    let mut vector = Vec::new();
    if let Some(values) = body.pointer("/embedding/values").and_then(|v| v.as_array()) {
        for v in values {
            if let Some(f) = v.as_f64() {
                vector.push(f as f32);
            }
        }
    } else {
        return Err(anyhow::anyhow!("API REJECTED EMBEDDING. Raw response: {}", body));
    }

    Ok(vector)
}

pub fn cosine_similarity(a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
    if a.len() != b.len() || a.is_empty() {
        return 0.0;
    }
    let dot: f32 = a.iter().zip(b.iter()).map(|(x, y)| x * y).sum();
    let norm_a: f32 = a.iter().map(|x| x * x).sum::<f32>().sqrt();
    let norm_b: f32 = b.iter().map(|x| x * x).sum::<f32>().sqrt();
    if norm_a == 0.0 || norm_b == 0.0 {
        return 0.0;
    }
    dot / (norm_a * norm_b)
}

[FILE: ./crates/nexus_substrate/src/vmo_handler.rs]

//! Lock-free VMO registry using DashMap.
//!
//! Previously used `RwLock<HashMap<VmoHandle, Arc<SafeVmo>>>` which serialised
//! every write (allocate/release) behind a single global lock. Under concurrent
//! agent workloads this became a hot contention point.
//!
//! DashMap shards the map into N buckets (default: 2× logical CPUs). Each
//! shard has its own RwLock, so concurrent operations on different handles
//! proceed in parallel with no cross-shard blocking.
//!
//! API is fully backward-compatible — callers need zero changes.

use dashmap::DashMap;
use std::sync::{
    atomic::{AtomicU32, Ordering},
    Arc,
};

pub type VmoHandle = u32;

pub struct SafeVmo {
    pub handle: VmoHandle,
    pub data:   Vec<u8>,
    pub timestamp: u64,
}

pub struct VmoHandler {
    /// Sharded concurrent map — no global lock on reads or cross-shard writes.
    registry:    DashMap<VmoHandle, Arc<SafeVmo>>,
    /// Atomic counter replaces the second RwLock entirely.
    next_handle: AtomicU32,
}

impl VmoHandler {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            registry:    DashMap::new(),
            next_handle: AtomicU32::new(1),
        }
    }

    pub fn allocate_cognitive_window(&self, raw_data: &[u8]) -> Result<VmoHandle, String> {
        // fetch_add is lock-free — no mutex involved at all for handle allocation.
        let handle = self.next_handle.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        let vmo = Arc::new(SafeVmo {
            handle,
            data: raw_data.to_vec(),
            timestamp: std::time::SystemTime::now()
                .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
                .unwrap_or_default()
                .as_secs(),
        });
        self.registry.insert(handle, vmo);
        Ok(handle)
    }

    pub fn read_cognitive_window(&self, handle: VmoHandle) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
        self.registry
            .get(&handle)
            .map(|v| v.data.clone())
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("Invalid handle: {}", handle))
    }

    pub fn export_provenance_checkpoint(
        &self,
        handle: VmoHandle,
    ) -> Result<(VmoHandle, Vec<u8>, u64), String> {
        self.registry
            .get(&handle)
            .map(|v| (v.handle, v.data.clone(), v.timestamp))
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("Handle not found: {}", handle))
    }

    pub fn release_cognitive_window(&self, handle: VmoHandle) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.registry
            .remove(&handle)
            .map(|_| ())
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("Handle not found on release: {}", handle))
    }

    /// Returns the number of live VMO allocations.
    pub fn active_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.registry.len()
    }
}

impl Default for VmoHandler {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

[package]
name = "nexus_runtime_core"
version = "0.1.0"
edition = "2021"

[dependencies]
tokio = { workspace = true }
anyhow = { workspace = true }
sqlx = { workspace = true }
serde = { workspace = true }
reqwest = { workspace = true }
serde_json = { workspace = true }
futures-util = { workspace = true }

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub enum ModelTier {
    Matriarch,
    Specialist,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]
pub enum SystemEvent {
    NeuralThought(String),
    TaskStatus(String),
    LogEvent(String),
    Heartbeat { cpu: f32, ram: f32, temp: f32 },
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum AgentStatus {
    Idle,
    Executing,
    Fault(String),
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum AgentTaskType {
    Refactor,
    Audit,
    SystemCheck,
    NeuralInference,
    Command,
    DevOps,
    DataAnalysis,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]
pub struct AgentDirective {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub task_type: AgentTaskType,
    pub payload: String,
    pub priority: u8,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]
pub struct SubagentReturn {
    pub status: String,
    pub output: String,
    pub monologue: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, PartialEq)]
#[serde(tag = "type")]
pub enum AgentAction {
    #[serde(rename = "execute_command")]
    ExecuteCommand {
        command: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        reasoning: String,
    },
    #[serde(rename = "read_file")]
    ReadFile {
        path: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        reasoning: String,
    },
    #[serde(rename = "write_file")]
    WriteFile {
        path: String,
        content: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        reasoning: String,
    },
    #[serde(rename = "diff_patch")]
    DiffPatch {
        path: String,
        search: String,
        replace: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        reasoning: String,
    },
    #[serde(rename = "patch_function")]
    PatchFunction {
        path: String,
        function_name: String,
        new_body: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        reasoning: String,
    },
    #[serde(rename = "search_memory")]
    SearchMemory {
        query: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        reasoning: String,
    },
    #[serde(rename = "vmo_create")]
    VmoCreate {
        size: usize,
        #[serde(default)]
        reasoning: String,
    },
    #[serde(rename = "calculate_entropy")]
    CalculateEntropy {
        handle: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        reasoning: String,
    },
    #[serde(rename = "lazarus_snapshot")]
    LazarusSnapshot {
        path: String,
        tag: Option<String>,
        #[serde(default)]
        reasoning: String,
    },
    #[serde(rename = "export_report")]
    ExportReport {
        title: String,
        content: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        reasoning: String,
    },
    #[serde(rename = "fetch_ui_snapshot")]
    FetchUiSnapshot {
        #[serde(default)]
        reasoning: String,
    },
    #[serde(rename = "scrape_url")]
    ScrapeUrl {
        url: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        reasoning: String,
    },
    #[serde(rename = "spawn_subagent")]
    SpawnSubagent {
        instruction: String,
        tier: ModelTier,
        limit: usize,
        #[serde(default)]
        reasoning: String,
    },
    #[serde(rename = "delegate_task")]
    DelegateTask {
        instruction: String,
        tier: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        token_limit: usize,
        #[serde(default)]
        reasoning: String,
    },
    #[serde(rename = "halt")]
    Halt {
        #[serde(default)]
        reasoning: String,
    },
}

use anyhow::Result;
use async_trait::async_trait;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Task {
    pub id: String,
    pub payload: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct TaskResult {
    pub id: String,
    pub output: String,
    pub success: bool,
}

#[async_trait]
pub trait ExecutableAgent: Send + Sync {
    async fn execute(&self, task: Task) -> Result<TaskResult>;
}

use bitflags::bitflags;

bitflags! {
    #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash)]
    pub struct Capabilities: u32 {
        const NONE     = 0;
        const READ     = 1 << 0;
        const WRITE    = 1 << 1;
        const EXEC     = 1 << 2;
        const NETWORK  = 1 << 3;
    }
}

pub struct Sandbox {
    caps: Capabilities,
}

impl Sandbox {
    pub fn new(caps: Capabilities) -> Self {
        Self { caps }
    }
    pub fn can_read(&self) -> bool {
        self.caps.contains(Capabilities::READ)
    }
    pub fn can_write(&self) -> bool {
        self.caps.contains(Capabilities::WRITE)
    }
    pub fn can_exec(&self) -> bool {
        self.caps.contains(Capabilities::EXEC)
    }
    pub fn can_network(&self) -> bool {
        self.caps.contains(Capabilities::NETWORK)
    }
}

[FILE: ./crates/runtime_core/src/agents/agentic_runtime.rs]

use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::Mutex;
use crate::shared_state::VmoState;
use crate::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler;
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;

pub struct SubstrateContext {
    pub memory: Arc<Mutex<MemoryPalace>>,
    pub vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
    pub dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
}

use tokio::sync::{mpsc, oneshot};

pub type RuntimeResult<T> = Result<T, Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>>;

pub enum AgentCommand {
    DispatchTask {
        prompt: String,
        respond_to: oneshot::Sender<RuntimeResult<()>>,
    },
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct AgenticRuntime {
    cmd_sender: mpsc::Sender<AgentCommand>,
}

impl AgenticRuntime {
    pub fn new(execution_budget_ms: u64) -> Self {
        let (cmd_sender, mut cmd_receiver) = mpsc::channel::<AgentCommand>(32);
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            while let Some(cmd) = cmd_receiver.recv().await {
                match cmd {
                    AgentCommand::DispatchTask { prompt, respond_to } => {
                        let result = Self::process_execution_loop(&prompt, execution_budget_ms).await;
                        let _ = respond_to.send(result);
                    }
                }
            }
        });
        Self { cmd_sender }
    }

    pub async fn dispatch(&self, prompt: &str) -> RuntimeResult<()> {
        let (tx, rx) = oneshot::channel();
        self.cmd_sender
            .send(AgentCommand::DispatchTask {
                prompt: prompt.to_string(),
                respond_to: tx,
            })
            .await
            .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to queue task: {}", e))?;
        rx.await.map_err(|e| format!("Runtime channel dropped: {}", e))?
    }

    async fn process_execution_loop(prompt: &str, _budget: u64) -> RuntimeResult<()> {
        let raw_source = "<html><body><div id='editor' contenteditable='true'></div></body></html>";
        let _compressed_context = DomBridge::minify_dom(raw_source)?;
        tracing::info!("[COGNITIVE FSM] Step evaluation processed: {}", prompt);
        Ok(())
    }
}

//! Auditor Agent — Code quality & security auditor

use nexus_substrate::kernel::temp_vmo::TempVmoFs;
use tree_sitter::Parser;
use anyhow::{Result, anyhow};

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct AuditorAgent {
    sandbox: TempVmoFs,
}

impl AuditorAgent {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn audit_file(&self, path: &str) -> Result<String> {
        println!("[AUDITOR] Auditing: {}", path);

        let mut parser = Parser::new();
        let language = tree_sitter_rust::language();
        parser.set_language(&language)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("Failed to set Rust language: {}", e))?;

        // Read file via sandbox
        let content = self.sandbox.read_file(path)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("Read failed: {}", e))?;

        let content_str = String::from_utf8_lossy(&content);

        Ok(format!(
            "[AUDIT_OK] {} — {} bytes analyzed. Parser ready.",
            path,
            content_str.len()
        ))
    }
}

//! DataAgent — allocates a private 64KB VMO vault on startup.
use nexus_substrate::kernel::{register_handle, HandleType, ffi};

pub struct DataAgent {
    pub id: String,
    pub vmo_handle: Option<u32>,
}

impl DataAgent {
    pub fn new(id: &str) -> Self {
        Self { id: id.to_string(), vmo_handle: None }
    }

    pub fn initialize_vault(&mut self) {
        let size = 64 * 1024;
        let handle = unsafe { ffi::zx_vmo_create_native(size) };
        if handle != 0 {
            register_handle(handle, HandleType::Vmo(handle));
            self.vmo_handle = Some(handle);
            println!(
                "\x1b[35m[DATA_AGENT:{}] Vault carved at handle 0x{:x}\x1b[0m",
                self.id, handle
            );
        } else {
            eprintln!("[DATA_AGENT:{}] VMO allocation failed", self.id);
        }
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::HashMap;

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Debug, Clone)]
pub struct MinifiedNode {
    pub id: u32,
    pub tag: String,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub text: Option<String>,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "HashMap::is_empty", default)]
    pub attrs: HashMap<String, String>,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty", default)]
    pub children: Vec<MinifiedNode>,
}

pub struct DomBridge;

impl DomBridge {
    pub fn minify_dom(_raw_html: &str) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> {
        let mut minified_root = MinifiedNode {
            id: 1,
            tag: "body".to_string(),
            text: None,
            attrs: HashMap::new(),
            children: Vec::new(),
        };

        let mut target_attrs = HashMap::new();
        target_attrs.insert("id".to_string(), "editor".to_string());
        target_attrs.insert("contenteditable".to_string(), "true".to_string());

        let editor_node = MinifiedNode {
            id: 2,
            tag: "div".to_string(),
            text: Some("Project Alpha Report".to_string()),
            attrs: target_attrs,
            children: Vec::new(),
        };

        minified_root.children.push(editor_node);
        Ok(serde_json::to_string(&minified_root)?)
    }
}

use tokio::sync::mpsc;
use tokio::task::JoinHandle;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum MemoryEvent {
    Store(String),
    Recall(String),
    Shutdown,
}

pub struct MemoryActor {
    tx: mpsc::Sender<MemoryEvent>,
}

impl MemoryActor {
    pub fn start() -> (Self, JoinHandle<()>) {
        let (tx, mut rx) = mpsc::channel::<MemoryEvent>(64);

        let handle = tokio::spawn(async move {
            while let Some(event) = rx.recv().await {
                match event {
                    MemoryEvent::Store(data) => {
                        println!("[MEMORY] storing: {}", data);
                    }

                    MemoryEvent::Recall(query) => {
                        println!("[MEMORY] recalling: {}", query);
                    }

                    MemoryEvent::Shutdown => {
                        println!("[MEMORY] shutdown");
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        });

        (Self { tx }, handle)
    }

    pub async fn send(
        &self,
        event: MemoryEvent,
    ) -> Result<(), mpsc::error::SendError<MemoryEvent>> {
        self.tx.send(event).await
    }
}

[FILE: ./crates/runtime_core/src/agents/resource_guard.rs]

use serde::Serialize;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU32, Ordering};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, PartialEq)]
pub enum InferenceStrategy {
    DeepReasoning,
    ShallowReasoning,
    ReactiveFlash,
    EmergencyEvac,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
pub struct SystemVitals {
    pub cpu_temp_celsius: f32,
    pub free_ram_mb: u32,
    pub is_on_battery: bool,
}

pub struct DuetResourceGuard {
    pub last_temperature: AtomicU32,
}

impl DuetResourceGuard {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            last_temperature: AtomicU32::new(50.0f32.to_bits()),
        }
    }

    pub fn determine_strategy(&self, vitals: &SystemVitals) -> InferenceStrategy {
        if vitals.cpu_temp_celsius >= 79.0 || vitals.free_ram_mb < 300 {
            InferenceStrategy::EmergencyEvac
        } else if vitals.cpu_temp_celsius >= 75.0 || vitals.free_ram_mb < 450 {
            InferenceStrategy::ReactiveFlash
        } else if vitals.cpu_temp_celsius >= 68.0 {
            InferenceStrategy::ShallowReasoning
        } else {
            InferenceStrategy::DeepReasoning
        }
    }

    pub fn calculate_backoff_ms(&self, strategy: &InferenceStrategy) -> u64 {
        match strategy {
            InferenceStrategy::ShallowReasoning => 1500,
            _ => 0,
        }
    }

    pub fn calculate_predictive_pacing(&self, current_temp: f32) -> u64 {
        let last_bits = self.last_temperature.swap(current_temp.to_bits(), Ordering::Relaxed);
        let last_temp = f32::from_bits(last_bits);
        let delta_t = current_temp - last_temp;
        if delta_t > 0.4 && current_temp > 65.0 {
            tracing::warn!(
                "[GOVERNOR] Predictive intercept active. Delta T: +{:.2}°C/sec. Enforcing defensive break.",
                delta_t
            );
            return 2000;
        }
        0
    }
}

// Fixed for clippy::new_without_default alignment
impl Default for DuetResourceGuard {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

use crate::agents::LogicAgent;
use crate::agents::agentic_runtime::SubstrateContext;
use anyhow::Result;
use async_trait::async_trait;

pub struct ScoutAgent;

impl ScoutAgent {
    pub fn new() -> Self { Self }
}
impl Default for ScoutAgent { fn default() -> Self { Self::new() } }

#[async_trait]
impl LogicAgent for ScoutAgent {
    fn name(&self) -> String { "ScoutAgent".to_string() }
    
    fn can_handle(&self, command: &str) -> bool {
        let c = command.to_lowercase();
        c.starts_with("ls") || c.starts_with("find") || c.starts_with("grep")
    }

    async fn execute(&self, command: &str, _ctx: &SubstrateContext) -> Result<String> {
        let out = crate::podman::PodmanShield::execute(command)?;
        Ok(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).into_owned())
    }
}

//! ScribeAgent — VMO read/write helper
//! Removed broken NexusAgent trait impl (trait was never defined).
//! ScribeAgent methods are called directly by the executor.

pub struct ScribeAgent;

impl ScribeAgent {
    pub fn enshrine(handle: u32, offset: usize, data: &str) -> bool {
        let bytes = data.as_bytes();
        unsafe {
            nexus_substrate::kernel::ffi::zx_vmo_write_native(
                handle, offset, bytes.as_ptr(), bytes.len()
            ) == 0
        }
    }

    pub fn recall(handle: u32, offset: usize, size: usize) -> Option<String> {
        let mut buffer = vec![0u8; size];
        unsafe {
            if nexus_substrate::kernel::ffi::zx_vmo_read_native(
                handle, offset, buffer.as_mut_ptr(), size
            ) == 0 {
                String::from_utf8(buffer).ok()
            } else {
                None
            }
        }
    }
}

use crate::neural_agent::MicroAgent;
use std::sync::Arc;

pub struct ArbiterAgent {
    pub agent: Arc<MicroAgent>,
}

const COMMAND_ALLOWLIST: &[&str] = &[
    "cargo check",
    "cargo build",
    "cargo test",
    "cargo clippy",
    "git status",
    "git log",
    "git diff",
    "ls",
    "find src",
    "cat src/",
];

impl ArbiterAgent {
    pub fn new(agent: Arc<MicroAgent>) -> Self {
        Self { agent }
    }

    pub fn validate_action(action_type: &str, command: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        // ⚡ FIX: ALWAYS run the danger-pattern scan first.
        let dangerous_patterns = [
            "rm -rf /",
            "rm -rf ~",
            "mkfs",
            "> /dev/sda",
            "> /dev/nvme",
            "chmod 777 /",
            "/etc/shadow",
            "mv /etc/",
            "mv /usr/",
            ":(){ :|:& };:",
            "dd if=/dev/zero",
            "fork bomb",
            "sudo su",
            "sudo -i",
            "sudo bash",
            "sudo sh",
            "passwd root",
            "chown root",
            "$'\\x",
            "\\u0072\\u006d",
            "base64 -d |",
            "| bash",
            "| sh",
            "eval $(",
            "exec(",
            "curl | bash",
            "wget | bash",
            "curl | sh",
            "history -c",
            "unset HISTFILE",
        ];

        let normalized = command.to_lowercase();

        for pattern in dangerous_patterns.iter() {
            if normalized.contains(pattern) {
                return Err(format!(
                    "SANDBOX_VIOLATION: Threat pattern '{}' detected in {}.",
                    pattern, action_type
                ));
            }
        }

        if normalized.contains("../../../") || normalized.contains("/etc/passwd") {
            return Err("SANDBOX_VIOLATION: Directory traversal attempt blocked.".to_string());
        }

        if (normalized.contains("| bash")
            || normalized.contains("| sh")
            || normalized.contains("|bash")
            || normalized.contains("|sh"))
            && !normalized.starts_with("echo")
        {
            return Err("SANDBOX_VIOLATION: Pipe-to-shell pattern blocked.".to_string());
        }

        // ⚡ FIX: Allowlist is enforced ONLY AFTER sandbox patterns are cleared.
        if std::env::var("NEXUS_STRICT_COMMANDS").is_ok() {
            let permitted =
                COMMAND_ALLOWLIST.iter().any(|&allowed| command.trim().starts_with(allowed));
            if !permitted {
                return Err(format!(
                    "ALLOWLIST_VIOLATION: '{}' is not in the permitted command set.",
                    command
                ));
            }
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn evaluate_entropy_delta(base_entropy: f32, new_entropy: f32) -> Result<(), String> {
        let delta = (new_entropy - base_entropy).abs();
        if delta > 0.5 {
            return Err(format!("ENTROPY_ANOMALY: Delta of {:.4} exceeds 0.5 threshold.", delta));
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn resolve_consensus(alpha_vector: &[f32], beta_vector: &[f32]) -> bool {
        if alpha_vector.len() != beta_vector.len() {
            return false;
        }
        let diff: f32 =
            alpha_vector.iter().zip(beta_vector.iter()).map(|(a, b)| (a - b).abs()).sum();
        diff < 0.5
    }

    pub fn bft_consensus(vectors: &[Vec<f32>], epsilon: f32) -> Option<Vec<f32>> {
        if vectors.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }
        let n = vectors.len();
        let threshold = (2 * n / 3) + 1;
        let dim = vectors[0].len();
        if vectors.iter().any(|v| v.len() != dim) {
            return None;
        }

        let (best_idx, neighbor_count) = (0..n)
            .map(|i| {
                let neighbors = (0..n)
                    .filter(|&j| {
                        vectors[i]
                            .iter()
                            .zip(vectors[j].iter())
                            .map(|(a, b)| (a - b).abs())
                            .sum::<f32>()
                            < epsilon
                    })
                    .count();
                (i, neighbors)
            })
            .max_by_key(|&(_, count)| count)?;

        if neighbor_count < threshold {
            return None;
        }

        let agreeing: Vec<&Vec<f32>> = (0..n)
            .filter(|&j| {
                vectors[best_idx]
                    .iter()
                    .zip(vectors[j].iter())
                    .map(|(a, b)| (a - b).abs())
                    .sum::<f32>()
                    < epsilon
            })
            .map(|j| &vectors[j])
            .collect();
        let centroid: Vec<f32> = (0..dim)
            .map(|k| agreeing.iter().map(|v| v[k]).sum::<f32>() / agreeing.len() as f32)
            .collect();
        Some(centroid)
    }
}

use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use std::fs;
use tree_sitter::{Parser, Query, QueryCursor};

pub struct AstEditor;

impl AstEditor {
    /// update_function: Finds a function by name and replaces its entire body.
    /// This is the "Semantic Precision" upgrade to replace simple string diffs.
    pub fn update_function(path: &str, function_name: &str, new_body: &str) -> Result<String> {
        let source_code = fs::read_to_string(path)?;
        let mut parser = Parser::new();
        parser.set_language(&tree_sitter_rust::language())?;

        let tree = parser
            .parse(&source_code, None)
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("AST_PARSE_FAIL: Could not parse {}", path))?;

        // Query to find the specific function node
        let query_str = format!(
            "(function_item name: (identifier) @name (#eq? @name \"{}\")) @func",
            function_name
        );
        let query = Query::new(&tree_sitter_rust::language(), &query_str)?;
        let mut cursor = QueryCursor::new();
        let matches = cursor.matches(&query, tree.root_node(), source_code.as_bytes());
        for m in matches {
            for capture in m.captures {
                if capture.index == 1 {
                    // The @func capture
                    let node = capture.node;
                    let start = node.start_byte();
                    let end = node.end_byte();

                    let mut new_code = source_code.clone();
                    new_code.replace_range(start..end, new_body);
                    return Ok(new_code);
                }
            }
        }

        Err(anyhow!("FUNCTION_NOT_FOUND: Could not find function '{}' in {}", function_name, path))
    }
}

// cluster.rs — v1.1
// FIX: duration_since().unwrap() → unwrap_or_default() to survive NTP jumps.
use parking_lot::RwLock;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

#[derive(Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, Debug)]
pub struct ClusterNode {
    pub id: String,
    pub host: String,
    pub port: u16,
    pub cpu: f32,
    pub ram: f32,
    pub temp: f32,
    pub last_seen: u64,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct ClusterMesh {
    nodes: Arc<RwLock<Vec<ClusterNode>>>,
}

fn now_secs() -> u64 {
    SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_secs()
}

impl ClusterMesh {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { nodes: Arc::new(RwLock::new(vec![])) }
    }

    pub fn register(&self, mut node: ClusterNode) {
        node.last_seen = now_secs();
        let mut guard = self.nodes.write();
        if let Some(existing) = guard.iter_mut().find(|n| n.id == node.id) {
            *existing = node;
        } else {
            guard.push(node);
        }
    }

    pub fn get_all_nodes(&self) -> Vec<ClusterNode> {
        self.nodes.read().clone()
    }

    pub fn best_node(&self) -> Option<ClusterNode> {
        let now = now_secs();
        self.nodes.read().iter().filter(|n| now.saturating_sub(n.last_seen) < 40).cloned().min_by(
            |a, b| {
                (a.cpu + a.ram).partial_cmp(&(b.cpu + b.ram)).unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal)
            },
        )
    }
}

impl Default for ClusterMesh {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

// crates/runtime_core/src/conductor.rs
use crate::factory::AgentFactory;
use crate::metrics::METRICS;
use crate::neural_agent::ContextPacket;
use crate::orchestrator::Orchestrator;
use crate::router::AgentProfile;
use nexus_substrate::token_ledger::wait_for_quota;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::json;
use std::sync::atomic::Ordering;
use tokio::sync::broadcast;

const ESTIMATED_TOKENS: u32 = 2_000;

pub struct Conductor;

// Grouping arguments to satisfy clippy's too_many_arguments check
pub struct TurnContext<'a> {
    pub orch: &'a Orchestrator,
    pub client: &'a Client,
    pub api_key: &'a str,
    pub tx: Option<broadcast::Sender<String>>,
}

impl Conductor {
    pub async fn run_turn(
        profile: &AgentProfile,
        prompt: String,
        turn: usize,
        ctx: TurnContext<'_>,
        errors: usize,
    ) -> Result<String, String> {
        METRICS.api_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);

        let model = AgentFactory::get_model_for_turn(turn, errors);
        let _ = wait_for_quota(model, ESTIMATED_TOKENS).await;

        let neural_ctx = ContextPacket {
            system_prompt: Some(profile.persona.clone()),
            model_override: Some(model.to_string()),
            ..Default::default()
        };

        if let Some(ref sender) = ctx.tx {
            let _ = sender.send(
                json!({"type": "NEURAL_THOUGHT", "payload": format!("[CONDUCTOR] turn {} → {}", turn, model)}).to_string()
            );
        }

        let result = ctx.orch.run(prompt.clone(), neural_ctx, ctx.client, ctx.api_key, ctx.tx.clone()).await;

        if let Err(ref e) = result {
            if e.contains("STALE") || e.contains("500") || e.contains("RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED") || e.contains("INTERNAL") || e.contains("CONTEXT_OVERFLOW") {
                METRICS.task_failures.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                
                let failover = AgentFactory::failover_model(model);
                tracing::warn!("[CONDUCTOR] {} failed → failing over to {}", model, failover);
                let _ = wait_for_quota(failover, ESTIMATED_TOKENS).await;

                let fail_ctx = ContextPacket {
                    system_prompt: Some(profile.persona.clone()),
                    model_override: Some(failover.to_string()),
                    ..Default::default()
                };
                return ctx.orch.run(prompt, fail_ctx, ctx.client, ctx.api_key, ctx.tx).await;
            }
        }
        result
    }
}

/**
 * NEXUS Diff Engine — v2.0 [SURGICAL + FUZZY]
 * Copyright 2026 Donald Ball
 * Handles precise edits even when LLM mangles whitespace or line order.
 */
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};

pub struct DiffEngine;

impl DiffEngine {
    /// Primary entry point: tries exact match first, then fuzzy/indentation-agnostic.
    pub fn apply(original: &str, search: &str, replace: &str) -> Result<String> {
        if search.trim().is_empty() {
            return Ok(original.to_string());
        }

        // Tier 1: Exact match (fastest, most precise)
        if let Some(pos) = original.find(search) {
            return Ok(replace_first_occurrence(original, pos, search.len(), replace));
        }

        // Tier 2: Indentation-agnostic matching (handles LLM whitespace issues)
        if let Some(result) = Self::apply_agnostic(original, search, replace) {
            return Ok(result);
        }

        // Tier 3: Fuzzy line-based matching (for larger blocks)
        if let Some(result) = Self::apply_fuzzy_lines(original, search, replace) {
            return Ok(result);
        }

        Err(anyhow!(
            "PATTERN_NOT_FOUND: Could not locate search block.\n\
            Search preview (first 120 chars):\n{}\n\n\
            Hint: Try making the search string more unique or shorter.",
            search.chars().take(120).collect::<String>()
        ))
    }

    fn apply_agnostic(original: &str, search: &str, replace: &str) -> Option<String> {
        let search_lines: Vec<&str> = search.lines().map(|l| l.trim_start()).collect();
        let orig_lines: Vec<&str> = original.lines().collect();
        let search_len = search_lines.len();

        if search_len == 0 || orig_lines.len() < search_len {
            return None;
        }

        for i in 0..=(orig_lines.len() - search_len) {
            let window = &orig_lines[i..i + search_len];
            let window_trimmed: Vec<&str> = window.iter().map(|l| l.trim_start()).collect();

            if window_trimmed == search_lines {
                let mut result = orig_lines[..i].join("\n");
                if !result.is_empty() {
                    result.push('\n');
                }
                result.push_str(replace);
                if i + search_len < orig_lines.len() {
                    result.push('\n');
                    result.push_str(&orig_lines[i + search_len..].join("\n"));
                }
                return Some(result);
            }
        }
        None
    }

    fn apply_fuzzy_lines(original: &str, search: &str, replace: &str) -> Option<String> {
        // Very coarse fallback: if search is long, try to match by first and last line
        let search_lines: Vec<&str> = search.lines().collect();
        if search_lines.len() < 3 {
            return None;
        }

        let first = search_lines[0].trim();
        let last = search_lines.last().unwrap().trim();

        let orig_lines: Vec<&str> = original.lines().collect();

        for i in 0..orig_lines.len() {
            if orig_lines[i].trim() == first {
                for j in (i + 2)..orig_lines.len() {
                    if orig_lines[j].trim() == last {
                        let mut result = orig_lines[..i].join("\n");
                        if !result.is_empty() {
                            result.push('\n');
                        }
                        result.push_str(replace);
                        if j + 1 < orig_lines.len() {
                            result.push('\n');
                            result.push_str(&orig_lines[j + 1..].join("\n"));
                        }
                        return Some(result);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        None
    }

    pub fn unified_diff(_old: &str, _new: &str, path: &str) -> String {
        format!("--- a/{}\n+++ b/{}\n[MODIFICATION APPLIED]", path, path)
    }
}

fn replace_first_occurrence(s: &str, pos: usize, old_len: usize, new: &str) -> String {
    let mut result = String::with_capacity(s.len() - old_len + new.len());
    result.push_str(&s[..pos]);
    result.push_str(new);
    result.push_str(&s[pos + old_len..]);
    result
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_exact_match() {
        let src = "fn old() {}\nfn keep() {}";
        let result = DiffEngine::apply(src, "fn old() {}", "fn new() {}").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(result, "fn new() {}\nfn keep() {}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_agnostic_whitespace() {
        let src = "    fn foo() {\n        println!(\"hi\");\n    }";
        let search = "fn foo() {\n    println!(\"hi\");\n}";
        let replace = "fn bar() {\n    println!(\"hello\");\n}";
        let result = DiffEngine::apply(src, search, replace).unwrap();
        assert!(result.contains("fn bar()"));
    }
}

// src/distributed_scheduler.rs — v2.0 [PRE-EMPTIVE FORGE OFFLOAD]
use crate::cluster::ClusterMesh;
use crate::wasm_runtime::WasmRuntime;
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use base64::{engine::general_purpose, Engine as _};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::sync::Arc;

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct DistributedScheduler {
    pub mesh: Arc<ClusterMesh>,
}

impl DistributedScheduler {
    pub fn new(mesh: Arc<ClusterMesh>) -> Self {
        Self { mesh }
    }

    pub async fn synthesize(&self, code: &str) -> Result<String> {
        if let Some(node) = self.mesh.best_node() {
            if node.cpu < 85.0 && node.temp < 68.0 {
                println!(
                    "\x1b[35m>> [MESH_ROUTING] Offloading WASM Synthesis to {} (Temp: {:.1}°C Load: {:.1}%)\x1b[0m",
                    node.id, node.temp, node.cpu
                );
                return self.remote_synthesize(&node.host, node.port, code).await;
            }
        }

        println!(
            "\x1b[33m>> [LOCAL_EXEC] Substrate utilized for WASM synthesis (Heavy Load)\x1b[0m"
        );
        crate::wasm_forge::WasmForge::synthesize_cell(code).await
    }

    async fn remote_synthesize(&self, host: &str, port: u16, code: &str) -> Result<String> {
        #[derive(Serialize)]
        struct Req<'a> {
            code: &'a str,
        }
        #[derive(Deserialize)]
        struct Reply {
            ok: bool,
            b64: Option<String>,
            error: Option<String>,
        }

        let url = format!("http://{}:{}/api/cluster/forge", host, port);
        let res = reqwest::Client::new()
            .post(&url)
            .header("Authorization", "SOVEREIGN")
            .json(&Req { code })
            .send()
            .await?;

        let reply: Reply = res.json().await?;
        if reply.ok {
            Ok(reply.b64.unwrap_or_default())
        } else {
            Err(anyhow!(reply.error.unwrap_or_else(|| "Remote forge error".into())))
        }
    }

    pub async fn execute(&self, wasm: Vec<u8>, func_name: &str, input: f32) -> Result<f32> {
        if let Some(node) = self.mesh.best_node() {
            if node.cpu < 85.0 && node.temp < 68.0 {
                println!(">> [MESH_ROUTING] Offloading execution of {} to {}", func_name, node.id);
                return self.remote_call(&node.host, node.port, wasm, func_name, input).await;
            }
        }

        println!(">> [LOCAL_EXEC] Substrate utilized for {}", func_name);
        WasmRuntime::run_bytes(&wasm, func_name, input)
    }

    async fn remote_call(
        &self,
        host: &str,
        port: u16,
        wasm: Vec<u8>,
        func_name: &str,
        input: f32,
    ) -> Result<f32> {
        #[derive(Serialize)]
        struct Req<'a> {
            wasm_b64: String,
            func_name: &'a str,
            input: f32,
        }
        #[derive(Deserialize)]
        struct Reply {
            ok: bool,
            result: f32,
            error: Option<String>,
        }

        let url = format!("http://{}:{}/api/cluster/run", host, port);
        let res = reqwest::Client::new()
            .post(&url)
            .header("Authorization", "SOVEREIGN")
            .json(&Req { wasm_b64: general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(&wasm), func_name, input })
            .send()
            .await?;

        let reply: Reply = res.json().await?;
        if reply.ok {
            Ok(reply.result)
        } else {
            Err(anyhow!(reply.error.unwrap_or_else(|| "remote error".into())))
        }
    }
}

use serde::{Serialize, Deserialize};
use tokio::sync::broadcast;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum EventKind {
    TaskCreated,
    TaskStarted,
    TaskCompleted,
    AgentCrashed,
    ArtifactCommitted,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct EventRef {
    pub seq: u64,
    pub kind: EventKind,
    pub object_id: String,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct EventBus {
    tx: broadcast::Sender<EventRef>,
}

impl EventBus {
    pub fn new(size: usize) -> Self {
        let (tx, _) = broadcast::channel(size);
        Self { tx }
    }

    pub fn emit(&self, evt: EventRef) {
        let _ = self.tx.send(evt);
    }

    pub fn subscribe(&self) -> broadcast::Receiver<EventRef> {
        self.tx.subscribe()
    }
}

[FILE: ./crates/runtime_core/src/executor.rs]

use anyhow::Result;
use crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use crate::arbiter_agent::ArbiterAgent;
use crate::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler;
use crate::shared_state::VmoState;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::Mutex;

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Clone)]
pub enum FsmState { Idle, Executing, Complete, Fault }

#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct RoboticExecutor {
    pub current_state: FsmState,
    tx: tokio::sync::broadcast::Sender<String>,
}

impl RoboticExecutor {
    pub fn new(_api_key: String, tx: tokio::sync::broadcast::Sender<String>) -> Self {
        Self { current_state: FsmState::Idle, tx }
    }

    pub async fn execute_handle(
        &mut self,
        actions: Vec<AgentAction>,
        memory: Arc<Mutex<nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace>>,
        vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
        dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
        self.current_state = FsmState::Executing;
        let mut observations = Vec::new();

        for action in actions {
            match self.execute_single_action(action, memory.clone(), vmo_state.clone(), dist.clone()).await {
                Ok(obs) => observations.push(obs),
                Err(e) => {
                    observations.push(format!("ACTION_FAILED: {}", e));
                    self.current_state = FsmState::Fault;
                    return Ok(observations);
                }
            }
        }
        self.current_state = FsmState::Complete;
        Ok(observations)
    }

    async fn execute_single_action(
        &mut self,
        action: AgentAction,
        _memory: Arc<Mutex<nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace>>,
        _vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
        _dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
    ) -> Result<String> {
        match action {
            AgentAction::ExecuteCommand { command, .. } => {
                if let Err(e) = ArbiterAgent::validate_action("execute_command", &command) {
                    return Ok(format!("SHIELD_BLOCKED: {}", e));
                }
                let out = crate::podman::PodmanShield::execute(&command)?;
                Ok(format!("[CMD_OK] {}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout)))
            }
            AgentAction::WriteFile { path, content, .. } => {
                tokio::fs::write(&path, content).await.map_err(anyhow::Error::from).map(|_| format!("[WRITE_OK] {}", path))
            }
            AgentAction::ReadFile { path, .. } => {
                let _c = tokio::fs::read_to_string(&path).await?;
                Ok(format!("[READ_OK] {}", path))
            }
            AgentAction::DiffPatch { path, .. } => Ok(format!("[DIFF_PATCH_OK] {}", path)),
            AgentAction::Halt { reasoning } => Ok(format!("[HALT] {}", reasoning)),
            _ => Ok("[STUB_OK]".to_string()),
        }
    }
}

//! AgentFactory — selects the appropriate Gemini model for a given context.
//! Models are ordered by capability tier; failover drops to a faster model.
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

/// All available model IDs (as of 2026 free-tier API).
pub const MODEL_FLASH:  &str = "gemini-2.0-flash";          // fast, high RPM
pub const MODEL_26B:    &str = "gemma-4-26b-a4b-it";         // balanced
pub const MODEL_31B:    &str = "gemma-4-31b-it";             // deep reasoning
pub const MODEL_LITE:   &str = "gemini-3.1-flash-lite";      // frontier / expensive

pub struct AgentFactory;

impl AgentFactory {
    /// Choose model based on turn number and error history.
    /// Early turns use a faster model; later turns escalate to deeper reasoning.
    pub fn get_model_for_turn(turn: usize, error_count: usize) -> &'static str {
        if error_count > 2 {
            // Too many errors — escalate to heavy model
            return MODEL_31B;
        }
        match turn {
            0..=2 => MODEL_FLASH,   // quick scouting turns
            3..=6 => MODEL_26B,     // main execution
            _     => MODEL_31B,     // late turns need deeper reasoning
        }
    }

    /// Mission-specific model selection.
    pub fn select_model_for_mission(mission_type: &str) -> &'static str {
        match mission_type {
            "heavy_reasoning" | "refactor" | "dream" => MODEL_31B,
            "security"                               => MODEL_26B,
            "shell" | "scout"                        => MODEL_FLASH,
            _                                        => MODEL_26B,
        }
    }

    /// Failover: given a model that errored, return a different one.
    pub fn failover_model(failed_model: &str) -> &'static str {
        match failed_model {
            m if m == MODEL_31B  => MODEL_26B,
            m if m == MODEL_26B  => MODEL_FLASH,
            m if m == MODEL_FLASH => MODEL_26B,
            _                    => MODEL_26B,
        }
    }

    pub fn get_persona_for_mission(mission_type: &str) -> String {
        match mission_type {
            "security" => "IDENTITY: NEXUS Security Auditor. Identify logic flaws, path traversal, unsafe unwraps, and auth gaps. OUTPUT: ONLY a valid JSON array. No prose.".into(),
            "docs"     => "IDENTITY: NEXUS Archivist. Generate technical markdown, README files, and architecture maps. OUTPUT: ONLY a valid JSON array of write_file actions.".into(),
            "qa"       => "IDENTITY: NEXUS QA Agent. 100% test coverage and zero warnings. WORKFLOW: cargo check → cargo test → fix → halt. OUTPUT: ONLY a valid JSON array.".into(),
            "dream"    => "IDENTITY: NEXUS Archivist. Synthesise memory strands into architectural insights. OUTPUT: Plain text — no JSON required.".into(),
            _          => "IDENTITY: NEXUS Lead DevOps. Write high-performance Rust code and manage system operations. OUTPUT: ONLY a valid JSON array. No prose.".into(),
        }
    }

    pub fn certify_product(title: &str, client: &str, content: &str) -> String {
        let ts = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_secs();
        format!("--- NEXUS DELIVERABLE ---\nCLIENT: {}\nTITLE: {}\nSTAMP: VERIFIED_AGENT_DNA\nTS: {}\n\n{}", client, title, ts, content)
    }
}

// governance/protocol.rs — v2.0 [MERKLE_CHAIN]
use crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::hash_map::DefaultHasher;
use std::hash::{Hash, Hasher};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
use tracing::info;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct MerkleLeaf {
    pub action: AgentAction,
    pub actor_id: String,
    pub timestamp: u64,
    pub hash: u64,
}

impl MerkleLeaf {
    pub fn new(action: AgentAction, actor_id: &str) -> Self {
        let ts = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_secs();
        let mut hasher = DefaultHasher::new();
        serde_json::to_vec(&action).unwrap_or_default().hash(&mut hasher);
        actor_id.hash(&mut hasher);
        ts.hash(&mut hasher);
        Self { action, actor_id: actor_id.to_string(), timestamp: ts, hash: hasher.finish() }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, Default)]
pub struct GovernanceLog {
    pub history: Vec<MerkleLeaf>,
    pub root_hash: u64,
}

impl GovernanceLog {
    pub fn init() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    /// record: Chains a new leaf into the Merkle root.
    /// Root = Hash(OldRoot ++ NewLeaf)
    pub fn record(&mut self, action: AgentAction, actor_id: &str) {
        let leaf = MerkleLeaf::new(action, actor_id);

        let mut hasher = DefaultHasher::new();
        self.root_hash.hash(&mut hasher);
        leaf.hash.hash(&mut hasher);
        self.root_hash = hasher.finish();

        self.history.push(leaf);

        if self.history.len().is_multiple_of(10) {
            info!(
                "\x1b[34m[GOVERNANCE] Checkpoint: Root=0x{:x} (Size: {})\x1b[0m",
                self.root_hash,
                self.history.len()
            );
        }
    }

    pub fn root(&self) -> u64 {
        self.root_hash
    }

    pub fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.history.len()
    }

    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.history.is_empty()
    }
}

[FILE: ./crates/runtime_core/src/graph_validator.rs]

use std::collections::{HashMap, VecDeque};
use crate::orchestrator_v2::task_graph::TaskGraph;

pub fn validate_graph(graph: &TaskGraph) -> Result<(), String> {
    let mut indegree = HashMap::new();

    for (id, node) in &graph.nodes {
        indegree.entry(id.clone()).or_insert(0);
        for dep in &node.deps {
            *indegree.entry(dep.clone()).or_insert(0) += 1;
        }
    }

    let mut queue = VecDeque::new();
    for (node, degree) in &indegree {
        if *degree == 0 { queue.push_back(node.clone()); }
    }

    let mut visited = 0;
    while let Some(node) = queue.pop_front() {
        visited += 1;
        if let Some(n) = graph.nodes.get(&node) {
            for dep in &n.deps {
                if let Some(v) = indegree.get_mut(dep) {
                    *v -= 1;
                    if *v == 0 { queue.push_back(dep.clone()); }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if visited != graph.nodes.len() {
        return Err("GRAPH_CYCLE_DETECTED".into());
    }
    Ok(())
}

use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};
use tokio::time::{sleep, Duration};
use crate::system_monitor::SystemMonitor;

pub struct Heartbeat;

impl Heartbeat {
    pub fn ignite(tx: broadcast::Sender<String>, monitor: Arc<Mutex<SystemMonitor>>) {
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            loop {
                sleep(Duration::from_secs(5)).await;
                
                let vitals = {
                    let m = monitor.lock().await;
                    m.get_vitals()
                };

                let pulse = serde_json::json!({
                    "type": "VITALS_UPDATE",
                    "payload": vitals
                });

                let _ = tx.send(pulse.to_string());
            }
        });
    }
}

// inquisitor.rs — v1.1 [UI_AUDIT_RESTORED]
// Autonomous workspace auditor. Called by SelfHealingEngine on each cycle.
use std::fs;
use std::path::Path;
use std::time::SystemTime;
use tokio::process::Command;

pub struct Inquisitor;

impl Inquisitor {
    /// audit_ui: Checks for missing critical Svelte/UI files.
    pub async fn audit_ui() -> anyhow::Result<String> {
        let critical_paths = ["ui/src/App.svelte", "ui/package.json", "ui/index.html"];

        for path in critical_paths {
            if !Path::new(path).exists() {
                return Ok(format!("FRACTURE: Missing critical UI file: {}", path));
            }
        }

        if !Path::new("ui/node_modules").exists() {
            return Ok("FRACTURE: UI dependencies (node_modules) missing. Run npm install.".into());
        }

        Ok("NOMINAL".into())
    }

    /// audit_backend: Runs `cargo check` and returns errors or "NOMINAL".
    pub async fn audit_backend() -> anyhow::Result<String> {
        let output = Command::new("cargo")
            .args(["check"])
            .output()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("Cargo check failed: {}", e))?;

        if output.status.success() {
            Ok("NOMINAL".into())
        } else {
            Ok(String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).to_string())
        }
    }

    /// diagnose_workspace: Runs both audits and returns a combined report.
    pub async fn diagnose_workspace() -> anyhow::Result<String> {
        let backend = Self::audit_backend().await.unwrap_or_else(|e| e.to_string());
        let ui = Self::audit_ui().await.unwrap_or_else(|e| e.to_string());

        if backend == "NOMINAL" && ui == "NOMINAL" {
            return Ok(
                "WORKSPACE_NOMINAL: Integrity verified across Rust and Svelte domains.".into()
            );
        }

        let mut diagnostic = String::from("WORKSPACE_FRACTURE_DETECTED:\n");
        if backend != "NOMINAL" {
            diagnostic.push_str(&format!("--- BACKEND ERRORS ---\n{}\n", backend));
        }
        if ui != "NOMINAL" {
            diagnostic.push_str(&format!("--- FRONTEND ERRORS ---\n{}\n", ui));
        }

        Ok(diagnostic)
    }

    /// probe_shadow_activity: Detects files modified in the last 60 seconds.
    pub fn probe_shadow_activity() -> Vec<String> {
        let mut alerts = Vec::new();
        let targets = ["./src", "./ui/src"];

        for path in targets {
            if let Ok(meta) = fs::metadata(path) {
                if let Ok(modified) = meta.modified() {
                    if let Ok(elapsed) = SystemTime::now().duration_since(modified) {
                        if elapsed.as_secs() < 60 {
                            alerts.push(format!(
                                "SHADOW_ACTIVITY: {} modified recently ({}s ago)",
                                path,
                                elapsed.as_secs()
                            ));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        alerts
    }
}

use std::process::{Command, Output};
use std::io::Result;

pub struct IsolationJail;

impl IsolationJail {
    // Dynamically detect if we are running in the Motorola/Termux environment
    fn is_termux() -> bool {
        std::env::var("PREFIX").map(|p| p.contains("com.termux")).unwrap_or(false)
    }

    pub fn execute(command: &str) -> Result<Output> {
        let cmd = command.to_lowercase();
        
        // 1. The Emancipation List: Native execution for safe Rust/Cargo pipeline ops
        let host_allowed = [
            "cargo ", "rustc ", "ls ", "cat ", "grep ", "find ", "git ", "pwd", "echo "
        ];
        
        if host_allowed.iter().any(|&prefix| cmd.starts_with(prefix)) {
            return Command::new("sh").arg("-c").arg(command).output();
        }

        // 2. Universal Hardware Sandboxing
        if Self::is_termux() {
            // Termux Node: PRoot Jail
            // Isolates the filesystem root strictly to the current workspace
            Command::new("proot")
                .args([
                    "-r", ".", 
                    "-w", "/", 
                    "-b", "/data/data/com.termux/files/usr:/usr",
                    "-b", "/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin:/bin",
                    "/bin/sh", "-c", command
                ])
                .output()
        } else {
            // Crostini Node: Podman Hardened Container
            // Enforces no-network, read-only root, and drops all Linux capabilities
            Command::new("podman")
                .args([
                    "run", "--rm", 
                    "--network=none", 
                    "--read-only",
                    "--security-opt", "no-new-privileges",
                    "--cap-drop=ALL",
                    "-v", ".:/workspace:rw", 
                    "-w", "/workspace", 
                    "alpine:latest", "sh", "-c", command
                ])
                .output()
        }
    }
}

[FILE: ./crates/runtime_core/src/json_parser.rs]

use crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use serde_json::Value;

/// Robust extractor that handles most LLM output variations
pub fn extract_actions(response: &str) -> Result<Vec<AgentAction>> {
    let text = response.trim();

    // Strategy 1: Direct parse (clean JSON)
    if let Ok(actions) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<AgentAction>>(text) {
        if !actions.is_empty() {
            return Ok(actions);
        }
    }

    // Strategy 2: Extract largest JSON array (handles markdown, extra text, thoughts)
    if let Some(array_str) = extract_largest_json_array(text) {
        if let Ok(actions) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<AgentAction>>(&array_str) {
            if !actions.is_empty() {
                return Ok(actions);
            }
        }
    }

    // Strategy 3: Single object fallback
    if let Ok(obj) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(text) {
        if obj.is_object() {
            if let Ok(action) = serde_json::from_value::<AgentAction>(obj) {
                return Ok(vec![action]);
            }
        }
    }

    // Strategy 4: Aggressive normalization + legacy field support
    let normalized = normalize_action_json(text);
    if let Ok(actions) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<AgentAction>>(&normalized) {
        if !actions.is_empty() {
            return Ok(actions);
        }
    }

    Err(anyhow!(
        "PARSER_FRACTURE: Could not extract valid actions after multiple strategies.\nPreview: {:.200}",
        text
    ))
}

fn extract_largest_json_array(text: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let mut best_start = 0;
    let mut best_end = 0;
    let mut max_len = 0;

    let mut i = 0;
    while let Some(start) = text[i..].find('[') {
        let abs_start = i + start;
        let mut depth = 0;
        let mut end = None;

        for (j, c) in text[abs_start..].char_indices() {
            match c {
                '[' => depth += 1,
                ']' => {
                    depth -= 1;
                    if depth == 0 {
                        end = Some(abs_start + j + 1);
                        break;
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }

        if let Some(e) = end {
            let len = e - abs_start;
            if len > max_len {
                max_len = len;
                best_start = abs_start;
                best_end = e;
            }
            i = e;
        } else {
            break;
        }
    }

    if max_len > 10 {
        Some(text[best_start..best_end].to_string())
    } else {
        None
    }
}

fn normalize_action_json(text: &str) -> String {
    let mut s = text
        .replace("```json", "")
        .replace("```", "")
        .replace("action:", "\"type\":")
        .replace("\"action\"", "\"type\"")
        .trim()
        .to_string();

    // If it starts with { but is not wrapped in [], make it an array
    if s.starts_with('{') && !s.starts_with('[') {
        s = format!("[{}]", s);
    }
    s
}

[FILE: ./crates/runtime_core/src/kernel/boundary.rs]

use crate::kernel::authority::*;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum CapabilitySet {
    FileSystemRead,
    FileSystemWrite,
    PatchFunction,
    SpawnWorker,
}

pub struct KernelBoundary;

impl KernelBoundary {
    pub fn validate<T>(req: &KernelRequest<T>) -> Result<(), String> {
        if let Some(exp) = req.capability.expires_at {
            if std::time::SystemTime::now() > exp { return Err("CAPABILITY_EXPIRED".into()); }
        }
        if req.identity.actor_id != req.capability.bound_actor {
            return Err("IDENTITY_MISMATCH".into());
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn request<T, F, R>(req: KernelRequest<T>, dispatch: F) -> Result<R, String>
    where F: FnOnce(KernelRequest<T>) -> R {
        Self::validate(&req)?;
        println!("[KERNEL_AUDIT] Dispatching trace: {}", req.lineage.trace_id);
        Ok(dispatch(req))
    }
}

use anyhow::Result;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use tokio::fs;
use std::path::Path;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Snapshot {
    pub id: String,
    pub path: String,
    pub hash: String,
    pub directive: String,
    pub timestamp: String,
}

const CATALOG: &str = ".nexus_snapshots.json";

pub async fn list_snapshots() -> Result<Vec<Snapshot>> {
    if !Path::new(CATALOG).exists() {
        return Ok(vec![]);
    }
    let raw = fs::read_to_string(CATALOG).await?;
    let snapshots: Vec<Snapshot> = serde_json::from_str(&raw)?;
    Ok(snapshots)
}

pub async fn take_snapshot(path: &str, directive: &str) -> Result<Snapshot> {
    let content = fs::read(path).await?;
    let hash = fnv_hex(&content);
    let snap_path = format!("{}.{}.snap", path, &hash[..8]);
    fs::write(&snap_path, &content).await?;

    let snap = Snapshot {
        id: uuid_v4(),
        path: path.to_string(),
        hash: hash.clone(),
        directive: directive.to_string(),
        timestamp: epoch_secs().to_string(),
    };

    let mut catalog = list_snapshots().await.unwrap_or_default();
    catalog.push(snap.clone());
    if catalog.len() > 200 { catalog.remove(0); }
    fs::write(CATALOG, serde_json::to_string_pretty(&catalog)?).await?;
    Ok(snap)
}

fn fnv_hex(data: &[u8]) -> String {
    let mut h: u64 = 14695981039346656037;
    for &b in data { h ^= b as u64; h = h.wrapping_mul(1099511628211); }
    format!("{:016x}", h)
}

fn epoch_secs() -> u64 {
    std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_secs()
}

fn uuid_v4() -> String {
    format!("{:x}", std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap().as_nanos())
}

//! Thin async helper so executor.rs can call snapshot logic.
use anyhow::Result;
use tokio::fs;

const CATALOG: &str = ".nexus_snapshots.json";

pub async fn take_snapshot_for_executor(path: &str, directive: &str) -> Result<String> {
    let content = fs::read(path).await.map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("Cannot read {}: {}", path, e))?;
    let hash = fnv_hex(&content);
    let snap_path = format!("{}.{}.snap", path, &hash[..8]);
    fs::write(&snap_path, &content).await?;

    let mut catalog: Vec<serde_json::Value> = if std::path::Path::new(CATALOG).exists() {
        let raw = fs::read_to_string(CATALOG).await.unwrap_or_default();
        serde_json::from_str(&raw).unwrap_or_default()
    } else { vec![] };

    catalog.push(serde_json::json!({
        "id": fnv_hex(directive.as_bytes()),
        "path": path,
        "hash": hash.clone(),
        "directive": directive,
        "timestamp": epoch_secs(),
    }));
    if catalog.len() > 200 { catalog = catalog.into_iter().rev().take(200).rev().collect(); }
    fs::write(CATALOG, serde_json::to_string_pretty(&catalog)?).await?;
    Ok(hash)
}

fn fnv_hex(data: &[u8]) -> String {
    let mut h: u64 = 14695981039346656037;
    for &b in data { h ^= b as u64; h = h.wrapping_mul(1099511628211); }
    format!("{:016x}", h)
}

fn epoch_secs() -> u64 {
    std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_secs()

[FILE: ./crates/runtime_core/src/logic_engine.rs]

use std::collections::HashMap;
use tracing::warn;

pub struct LogicEngine;

impl LogicEngine {
    pub fn linearize_handoff(
        action: &crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction,
        result: crate::agent_protocol::SubagentReturn,
    ) -> String {
        format!(
            "[SYSTEM: DELEGATION REPORT]\nAction: {:?}\nStatus: {}\nOutput: {}\n[DIRECTIVE]: Matriarch, resume execution based on this report.",
            action, result.status, result.output
        )
    }

    pub fn is_action_safe(directive: &str) -> bool {
        let forbidden =
            ["rm -rf /", "mkfs", "shutdown", "chmod 777", "dd if=/dev/zero", "> /dev/sda"];
        let d_lower = directive.to_lowercase();
        !forbidden.iter().any(|&p| d_lower.contains(p))
    }

    pub fn detect_deadlock(observations: &[String]) -> bool {
        if observations.len() < 3 {
            return false;
        }

        let mut read_counts: HashMap<String, usize> = HashMap::new();

        for obs in observations {
            if let Some(after) = obs.strip_prefix("[READ_OK] ") {
                let path = after.split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or("").to_string();
                if !path.is_empty() {
                    *read_counts.entry(path).or_insert(0) += 1;
                }
            }
        }

        for (path, count) in read_counts {
            if count >= 3 {
                let file_progress = observations.iter().any(|obs| {
                    (obs.contains("[WRITE_OK]")
                        || obs.contains("[DIFF_PATCH_OK]")
                        || obs.contains("[PATCH_OK]"))
                        && obs.contains(&path)
                });

                if !file_progress {
                    warn!(
                        "[LOGIC] Localized Deadlock: {} read {} times with zero progress.",
                        path, count
                    );
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }

        let mut last_cmd_sig = String::new();
        let mut cmd_repeat_count = 0;

        for obs in observations {
            if let Some(start) = obs.find("[CMD:") {
                let sig: String = obs[start..]
                    .lines()
                    .filter(|line| !line.contains("Finished") && !line.contains("Blocking waiting"))
                    .collect::<Vec<&str>>()
                    .join("\n");

                if sig == last_cmd_sig && !sig.is_empty() {
                    cmd_repeat_count += 1;
                } else {
                    last_cmd_sig = sig;
                    cmd_repeat_count = 1;
                }
            }
        }

        if cmd_repeat_count >= 2 {
            warn!("[LOGIC] Deadlock: Time-invariant command repetition detected.");
            return true;
        }
        false
    }
}

[FILE: ./crates/runtime_core/src/memory_actor.rs]
