use tokio::sync::{mpsc, oneshot};
use std::collections::HashMap;

pub enum MemoryCommand {
    Store { key: String, value: Vec<u8> },
    Load { key: String, respond_to: oneshot::Sender<Option<Vec<u8>>> },
}

pub struct MemoryActor {
    rx: mpsc::Receiver<MemoryCommand>,
}

impl MemoryActor {
    pub fn new(rx: mpsc::Receiver<MemoryCommand>) -> Self {
        Self { rx }
    }

    pub async fn run(mut self) {
        let mut db = HashMap::new();
        while let Some(cmd) = self.rx.recv().await {
            match cmd {
                MemoryCommand::Store { key, value } => { db.insert(key, value); }
                MemoryCommand::Load { key, respond_to } => {
                    let _ = respond_to.send(db.get(&key).cloned());
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * src/metrics.rs — v4.1 Atomic Sovereign
 * Copyright 2026 Donald Ball
 * Lightweight, lock-free, zero-dependency metrics using only std atomics.
 * Fully compatible with Prometheus scraping.
 */
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::LazyLock;

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct NexusMetrics {
    pub tasks_total: AtomicU64,
    pub task_failures: AtomicU64,
    pub arbiter_blocks: AtomicU64,
    pub heal_successes: AtomicU64,
    pub heal_failures: AtomicU64,
    pub api_latency_ms_sum: AtomicU64,
    pub api_calls: AtomicU64,
    pub circuit_opens: AtomicU64,
}

impl NexusMetrics {
    const fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            tasks_total: AtomicU64::new(0),
            task_failures: AtomicU64::new(0),
            arbiter_blocks: AtomicU64::new(0),
            heal_successes: AtomicU64::new(0),
            heal_failures: AtomicU64::new(0),
            api_latency_ms_sum: AtomicU64::new(0),
            api_calls: AtomicU64::new(0),
            circuit_opens: AtomicU64::new(0),
        }
    }

    /// Increment total tasks processed
    pub fn inc_task(&self) {
        tracing::info!("Task counter incremented");
        self.tasks_total.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    /// Increment task failures
    pub fn inc_failure(&self) {
        self.task_failures.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    /// Increment arbiter blocks (safety interventions)
    pub fn inc_arbiter_block(&self) {
        self.arbiter_blocks.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    /// Increment successful self-healing sutures
    pub fn inc_heal_success(&self) {
        self.heal_successes.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    /// Increment failed self-healing attempts
    pub fn inc_heal_failure(&self) {
        self.heal_failures.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    /// Increment circuit breaker trips
    pub fn inc_circuit_open(&self) {
        self.circuit_opens.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    /// Record API call with latency (used by Orchestrator)
    pub fn record_api_call(&self, latency_ms: u128) {
        self.api_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        self.api_latency_ms_sum.fetch_add(latency_ms as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    /// Compute average latency (integer ms)
    pub fn avg_latency_ms(&self) -> u64 {
        let calls = self.api_calls.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
        if calls == 0 {
            0
        } else {
            self.api_latency_ms_sum.load(Ordering::Relaxed) / calls
        }
    }
}

pub static METRICS: LazyLock<NexusMetrics> = LazyLock::new(NexusMetrics::new);

/// Returns full Prometheus exposition format text (ready for scraping)
pub fn get_metrics_text() -> String {
    let m = &*METRICS;
    format!(
        "# HELP nexus_tasks_total Total tasks processed by the NEXUS Cortex\n\
         # TYPE nexus_tasks_total counter\n\
         nexus_tasks_total {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_task_failures Total task execution failures\n\
         # TYPE nexus_task_failures counter\n\
         nexus_task_failures {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_arbiter_blocks Total arbiter safety interventions\n\
         # TYPE nexus_arbiter_blocks counter\n\
         nexus_arbiter_blocks {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_heal_successes Self-healing suture successes\n\
         # TYPE nexus_heal_successes counter\n\
         nexus_heal_successes {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_heal_failures Self-healing suture failures\n\
         # TYPE nexus_heal_failures counter\n\
         nexus_heal_failures {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_api_calls Total calls to Gemini API backend\n\
         # TYPE nexus_api_calls counter\n\
         nexus_api_calls {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_api_avg_latency_ms Average API latency in milliseconds\n\
         # TYPE nexus_api_avg_latency_ms gauge\n\
         nexus_api_avg_latency_ms {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_circuit_opens Circuit breaker trips\n\
         # TYPE nexus_circuit_opens counter\n\
         nexus_circuit_opens {}\n",
        m.tasks_total.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        m.task_failures.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        m.arbiter_blocks.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        m.heal_successes.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        m.heal_failures.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        m.api_calls.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        m.avg_latency_ms(),
        m.circuit_opens.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
    )
}

use crate::metrics::METRICS;
use anyhow::{Context, Result};
use futures_util::StreamExt;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::collections::VecDeque;
use std::sync::Mutex;
use tokio::sync::broadcast;

#[derive(Debug, Default, Clone)]
pub struct ContextPacket {
    pub system_prompt: Option<String>,
    pub model_override: Option<String>,
    pub current_file: String,
    pub current_content: Option<String>,
    pub recent_logs: Vec<String>,
    pub task_complexity: u8,
}

pub struct MicroAgent {
    pub memory: Mutex<VecDeque<Value>>,
    pub tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
}

impl MicroAgent {
    pub fn init(tx: broadcast::Sender<String>) -> Result<Self> {
        Ok(MicroAgent { memory: Mutex::new(VecDeque::with_capacity(16)), tx })
    }

    pub async fn prepare_and_run(
        &self,
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        user_text: &str,
        ctx: ContextPacket,
        _resonance: f32,
        model_override: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<(String, u128)> {
        let start = std::time::Instant::now();
        let model = model_override.or(ctx.model_override.as_deref()).unwrap_or("gemma-4-26b-a4b-it");
        let url = format!("https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{}:streamGenerateContent?alt=sse&key={}", model, api_key);

        let combined_text = format!("SYSTEM: {}\nUSER: {}", ctx.system_prompt.unwrap_or_default(), user_text);
        
        let payload = json!({
            "contents": [{ "role": "user", "parts": [{ "text": combined_text }] }],
            "generationConfig": { "temperature": 0.1, "maxOutputTokens": 4096 }
        });

        let response = client.post(&url).json(&payload).send().await.context("API request failed")?;
        let mut full_response = String::new();
        let mut byte_buffer = Vec::new();
        let mut stream = response.bytes_stream();

        while let Some(chunk_result) = stream.next().await {
            if let Ok(chunk) = chunk_result {
                byte_buffer.extend_from_slice(&chunk);
                while let Some(pos) = byte_buffer.iter().position(|&b| b == b'\n') {
                    let line_bytes: Vec<u8> = byte_buffer.drain(..=pos).collect();
                    let line = String::from_utf8_lossy(&line_bytes);
                    let clean = line.trim_start_matches("data: ").trim();
                    if clean.is_empty() || clean == "[DONE]" { continue; }

                    if let Ok(val) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(clean) {
                        if let Some(parts) = val.pointer("/candidates/0/content/parts").and_then(|v| v.as_array()) {
                            for part in parts {
                                // ⚡ SURGICAL FIX: Forward thoughts to the telemetry stream
                                if part["thought"].as_bool().unwrap_or(false) {
                                    if let Some(token) = part["text"].as_str() {
                                        let _ = self.tx.send(json!({"type": "NEURAL_THOUGHT", "payload": token}).to_string());
                                    }
                                    continue;
                                }
                                
                                if let Some(token) = part["text"].as_str() {
                                    full_response.push_str(token);
                                    let _ = self.tx.send(json!({"type": "NEURAL_STREAM", "payload": token}).to_string());
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        METRICS.record_api_call(start.elapsed().as_millis());
        Ok((full_response, start.elapsed().as_millis()))
    }
}

[FILE: ./crates/runtime_core/src/orchestrator.rs]

use crate::neural_agent::ContextPacket;
use crate::metrics::METRICS;
use futures_util::StreamExt;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::sync::atomic::Ordering;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use tokio::sync::broadcast;
use tracing::error;

pub struct Orchestrator;

impl Orchestrator {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self
    }

    pub async fn run(
        &self,
        prompt: String,
        ctx: ContextPacket,
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        tx: Option<broadcast::Sender<String>>,
    ) -> Result<String, String> {
        let model = ctx.model_override.as_deref().unwrap_or("gemma-4-26b-a4b-it");
        let url = format!(
            "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{}:streamGenerateContent?alt=sse&key={}",
            model, api_key
        );

        let payload = json!({
            "contents": [{ "role": "user", "parts": [{ "text": prompt }] }],
            "systemInstruction": {
                "parts": [{
                    "text": ctx.system_prompt.as_deref().unwrap_or("Output ONLY a JSON array. Zero prose.")
                }]
            },
            "generationConfig": { 
                "temperature": 0.1, 
                "maxOutputTokens": 4096,
            }
        });

        for _attempt in 1..=2u64 {
            let t_start = Instant::now();
            let res = client
                .post(&url)
                .timeout(Duration::from_secs(45))
                .json(&payload)
                .send()
                .await;

            match res {
                Ok(response) if response.status().is_success() => {
                    let mut full_text = String::new();
                    let mut stream = response.bytes_stream();
                    let mut buf: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();

                    while let Some(Ok(chunk)) = stream.next().await {
                        buf.extend_from_slice(&chunk);

                        while let Some(pos) = buf.iter().position(|&b| b == b'\n') {
                            let line_bytes: Vec<u8> = buf.drain(..=pos).collect();
                            let line = String::from_utf8_lossy(&line_bytes);
                            let clean = line.trim_start_matches("data: ").trim();

                            if clean.is_empty() || clean == "[DONE]" {
                                continue;
                            }

                            if let Ok(val) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(clean) {
                                if let Some(parts) = val.pointer("/candidates/0/content/parts")
                                    .and_then(|v| v.as_array())
                                {
                                    for part in parts {
                                        // ⚡ SURGICAL FIX: Forward thoughts instead of dropping them
                                        if part["thought"].as_bool().unwrap_or(false) {
                                            if let Some(token) = part["text"].as_str() {
                                                if let Some(sender) = &tx {
                                                    let _ = sender.send(
                                                        json!({"type": "NEURAL_THOUGHT", "payload": token}).to_string()
                                                    );
                                                }
                                            }
                                            continue;
                                        }
                                        
                                        if let Some(token) = part["text"].as_str() {
                                            full_text.push_str(token);
                                            if let Some(sender) = &tx {
                                                let _ = sender.send(
                                                    json!({"type": "NEURAL_STREAM", "payload": token}).to_string()
                                                );
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    METRICS.api_latency_ms_sum.fetch_add(
                        t_start.elapsed().as_millis() as u64,
                        Ordering::Relaxed,
                    );

                    let raw = full_text.trim();
                    let raw_lines: Vec<&str> = raw.lines().collect();
                    let json_start = raw_lines.iter().rposition(|l| {
                        let t = l.trim();
                        t.starts_with('[') && t.len() > 1 && {
                            let rest = t[1..].trim_start();
                            rest.starts_with('{') || rest.starts_with(']') || rest == "]"
                        }
                    });
                    let out = if let Some(si) = json_start {
                        let slice = raw_lines[si..].join("\n");
                        if let Some(e) = slice.rfind(']') { slice[..=e].to_string() } else { raw.to_string() }
                    } else { raw.to_string() };
                    return Ok(out.trim().to_string());
                }
                Ok(resp) => error!("API ERROR: {}", resp.status()),
                Err(e) => error!("NETWORK ERROR: {}", e),
            }
        }

        Err("CORTEX_UPLINK_FAILED".into())
    }
}

pub mod task_graph;

use self::task_graph::TaskGraph;
use crate::agent_runtime::engine::{ExecutableAgent, Task, TaskResult};
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use std::collections::HashMap;

pub struct OrchestratorV2 {
    // ⚡ DYNAMIC DISPATCH: Supports a diverse swarm of agents simultaneously
    agents: HashMap<String, Box<dyn ExecutableAgent>>,
}

impl OrchestratorV2 {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { agents: HashMap::new() }
    }

    pub fn register_agent(&mut self, name: &str, agent: Box<dyn ExecutableAgent>) {
        self.agents.insert(name.to_string(), agent);
    }

    pub async fn execute_graph(&self, graph: TaskGraph) -> Result<Vec<TaskResult>> {
        let order = graph.resolve_execution_order()?;
        let mut results = Vec::new();

        for id in order {
            let node = graph.nodes.get(&id).unwrap();

            let agent = self.agents.get(&node.required_agent).ok_or_else(|| {
                anyhow!("AGENT_MISSING: '{}' not registered in swarm.", node.required_agent)
            })?;

            let task = Task { id: id.clone(), payload: format!("Execute directive for {}", id) };

            let result = agent.execute(task).await?;
            results.push(result);
        }

        Ok(results)
    }
}

impl Default for OrchestratorV2 {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

[FILE: ./crates/runtime_core/src/orchestrator_v2/task_graph.rs]
========================================
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet, VecDeque};

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct TaskNode {
    pub id: String,
    pub deps: Vec<String>,
    pub required_agent: String,
}

pub struct TaskGraph {
    pub nodes: HashMap<String, TaskNode>,
}

impl TaskGraph {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { nodes: HashMap::new() }
    }
    pub fn add_task(&mut self, id: &str, deps: Vec<String>, required_agent: &str) {
        self.nodes.insert(
            id.to_string(),
            TaskNode { id: id.to_string(), deps, required_agent: required_agent.to_string() },
        );
    }

    pub fn resolve_execution_order(&self) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
        for node in self.nodes.values() {
            for dep in &node.deps {
                if !self.nodes.contains_key(dep.as_str()) {
                    return Err(anyhow!(
                        "DAG_ERROR: Task '{}' depends on unknown task '{}'.",
                        node.id,
                        dep
                    ));
                }
            }
        }

        let mut in_degree: HashMap<&str, usize> =
            self.nodes.keys().map(|k| (k.as_str(), 0)).collect();
        let mut dependents: HashMap<&str, Vec<&str>> = HashMap::new();

        for node in self.nodes.values() {
            for dep in &node.deps {
                dependents.entry(dep.as_str()).or_default().push(node.id.as_str());
                *in_degree.entry(node.id.as_str()).or_insert(0) += 1;
            }
        }

        let mut queue: VecDeque<&str> =
            in_degree.iter().filter(|(_, &d)| d == 0).map(|(&id, _)| id).collect();
        let mut order = Vec::with_capacity(self.nodes.len());

        while let Some(id) = queue.pop_front() {
            order.push(id.to_string());
            if let Some(kids) = dependents.get(id) {
                for &child in kids {
                    let deg = in_degree.get_mut(child).unwrap();
                    *deg -= 1;
                    if *deg == 0 {
                        queue.push_back(child);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if order.len() != self.nodes.len() {
            let resolved: HashSet<_> = order.iter().map(|s| s.as_str()).collect();
            let cycle_nodes: Vec<_> =
                self.nodes.keys().filter(|k| !resolved.contains(k.as_str())).cloned().collect();
            return Err(anyhow!(
                "DAG_DEADLOCK: Circular dependency detected among: {:?}",
                cycle_nodes
            ));
        }
        Ok(order)
    }
}

impl Default for TaskGraph {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

use std::io::{Error, ErrorKind, Result};
use std::process::{Command, Output};

pub struct PodmanShield;
impl PodmanShield {
    pub fn execute(command: &str) -> Result<Output> {
        let cmd = command.to_lowercase();

        // 1. The Ironclad Axiom Block (Never allow self-deletion)
        if cmd.contains("rm -rf src") || cmd.contains("rm -rf .git") || cmd.contains("rm -rf /") {
            return Err(Error::new(ErrorKind::PermissionDenied, "AXIOM_BLOCK: Destructive path."));
        }

        // 2. The Emancipation List (Allow safe host commands)
        let host_allowed = [
            "cargo ", "ls ", "cat ", "curl ", "wget ", "python3 ", "python ", "grep ", "find ",
            "git ", "pwd", "echo ",
        ];

        if host_allowed.iter().any(|&prefix| cmd.starts_with(prefix)) {
            return Command::new("sh").arg("-c").arg(command).output();
        }

        // 3. Fallback Sandbox (Restored network access so package managers work if needed)
        Command::new("podman")
            .args([
                "run",
                "--rm",
                "-v",
                ".:/workspace",
                "-w",
                "/workspace",
                "alpine:latest",
                "sh",
                "-c",
                command,
            ])
            .output()
    }
}

[FILE: ./crates/runtime_core/src/react_agent.rs]

#![allow(dead_code)] // Will be fully wired in next iterations

use crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use crate::json_parser::extract_actions;
use crate::neural_agent::{ContextPacket, MicroAgent};
use anyhow::{Context, Result};
use serde_json::json;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::broadcast;
use tracing::{info, warn};

const MAX_REACT_STEPS: usize = 12;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum ReActStep {
    Thought(String),
    Action(AgentAction),
    Observation(String),
    FinalAnswer(String),
}

pub struct ReActAgent {
    micro_agent: Arc<MicroAgent>,
    client: reqwest::Client,
    api_key: String,
    tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
}

impl ReActAgent {
    pub fn new(
        micro_agent: Arc<MicroAgent>,
        client: reqwest::Client,
        api_key: String,
        tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self { micro_agent, client, api_key, tx }
    }

    pub async fn run(&self, task: &str, ctx: ContextPacket) -> Result<String> {
        let mut history: Vec<ReActStep> = Vec::new();
        let mut step = 0usize;

        let _ = self.tx.send(json!({"type": "REACT_START", "payload": {"task": task}}).to_string());

        while step < MAX_REACT_STEPS {
            step += 1;
            info!("🔄 ReAct Step {}/{}", step, MAX_REACT_STEPS);

            let prompt = self.build_react_prompt(task, &history, &ctx);
            let response = self.call_llm(&prompt, &ctx).await?;

            let thought = self.extract_thought(&response);
            let _ = self.tx.send(json!({"type": "NEURAL_THOUGHT", "payload": thought}).to_string());
            history.push(ReActStep::Thought(thought));

            if let Some(answer) = self.extract_final_answer(&response) {
                let _ = self.tx.send(json!({"type": "REACT_FINAL_ANSWER", "payload": {"answer": answer}}).to_string());
                return Ok(answer);
            }

            match extract_actions(&response) {
                Ok(actions) if !actions.is_empty() => {
                    for action in actions {
                        history.push(ReActStep::Action(action.clone()));
                        let obs = self.execute_action(action, &ctx).await?;
                        history.push(ReActStep::Observation(obs.clone()));
                        let _ = self.tx.send(json!({"type": "REACT_OBSERVATION", "payload": obs}).to_string());
                    }
                }
                _ => warn!("No actions parsed in this ReAct turn"),
            }
        }

        Ok("Task reached maximum ReAct steps.".to_string())
    }

    fn build_react_prompt(&self, task: &str, history: &[ReActStep], _ctx: &ContextPacket) -> String {
        let mut p = format!("You are NEXUS ReAct Agent.\nTask: {}\n\n", task);
        p.push_str("Use Thought → Action → Observation format.\n");
        p.push_str("Available actions: read_file, write_file, vmo_create, diff_patch, etc.\n\n");

        if !history.is_empty() {
            p.push_str("History:\n");
            for step in history.iter().take(10) {
                match step {
                    ReActStep::Thought(t) => p.push_str(&format!("Thought: {}\n", t)),
                    ReActStep::Action(a) => p.push_str(&format!("Action: {:?}\n", a)),
                    ReActStep::Observation(o) => p.push_str(&format!("Observation: {}\n", o)),
                    ReActStep::FinalAnswer(a) => p.push_str(&format!("Final Answer: {}\n", a)),
                }
            }
        }

        p.push_str("\nNext response format:\nThought: ...\nAction: [JSON array of AgentAction] OR Final Answer: ...");
        p
    }

    async fn call_llm(&self, prompt: &str, ctx: &ContextPacket) -> Result<String> {
        let (response, _) = self.micro_agent
            .prepare_and_run(&self.client, &self.api_key, prompt, ctx.clone(), 0.0, None)
            .await
            .context("LLM call failed")?;

        Ok(response)
    }

    async fn execute_action(&self, action: AgentAction, _ctx: &ContextPacket) -> Result<String> {
        let _ = self.tx.send(json!({"type": "ACTION_EXECUTE", "payload": format!("{:?}", action)}).to_string());
        crate::executor::ToolExecutor::execute(&action).await
    }

    fn extract_thought(&self, resp: &str) -> String {
        resp.lines()
            .find(|l| l.trim_start().starts_with("Thought:"))
            .map(|l| l.trim_start_matches("Thought:").trim().to_string())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| "Thinking step-by-step...".into())
    }

    fn extract_final_answer(&self, resp: &str) -> Option<String> {
        resp.lines()
            .find(|l| l.trim_start().starts_with("Final Answer:"))
            .map(|l| l.trim_start_matches("Final Answer:").trim().to_string())
    }
}

// src/router.rs — Intelligent Agent Router v4.1
// use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum DirectiveClass {
    ShellOp,
    TargetedEdit { file_hint: String },
    RepoDoctor,
    SecurityAudit,
    WasmForge, 
    Inquiry,
    FeatureBuild,
    General,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ContextBudget {
    pub max_file_chars: usize,
    pub max_obs_lines: usize,
    pub max_schema_chars: usize,
    pub max_output_tokens: u32,
}

impl ContextBudget {
    pub fn minimal() -> Self { Self { max_file_chars: 1_500, max_obs_lines: 2, max_schema_chars: 400, max_output_tokens: 1024 } }
    pub fn standard() -> Self { Self { max_file_chars: 8_000, max_obs_lines: 6, max_schema_chars: 1_200, max_output_tokens: 4096 } }
    pub fn doctor() -> Self { Self { max_file_chars: 15_000, max_obs_lines: 10, max_schema_chars: 1_500, max_output_tokens: 8192 } }
    pub fn security() -> Self { Self { max_file_chars: 20_000, max_obs_lines: 10, max_schema_chars: 2_000, max_output_tokens: 8192 } }
}

pub struct AgentProfile {
    pub class: DirectiveClass,
    pub primary_model: &'static str,
    pub max_turns: u8,
    pub budget: ContextBudget,
    pub persona: String,
}

pub struct IntelligentRouter;

impl IntelligentRouter {
    pub fn classify(actual_directive: &str) -> DirectiveClass {
        let d = actual_directive.to_lowercase();
        if ["diagnose", "doctor", "fix repo", "repair workspace", "suture"].iter().any(|k| d.contains(k)) {
            return DirectiveClass::RepoDoctor;
        }
        if ["synthesize", "forge", "wasm cell"].iter().any(|k| d.contains(k)) {
            return DirectiveClass::WasmForge;
        }
        if ["cargo check", "cargo test", "git status", "ls ", "find "].iter().any(|k| d.contains(k)) {
            return DirectiveClass::ShellOp;
        }
        if ["audit ", "vulnerability", "memory safe"].iter().any(|k| d.contains(k)) {
            return DirectiveClass::SecurityAudit;
        }
        if ["fix", "patch", "update", "edit"].iter().any(|k| d.contains(k)) {
            return DirectiveClass::TargetedEdit { file_hint: "target".into() };
        }
        DirectiveClass::General
    }

    pub fn route(directive: &str) -> AgentProfile {
        let start_idx = directive.find("DIRECTIVE: ").map(|i| i + 11).unwrap_or(0);
        let user_cmd = &directive[start_idx..];
        let class = Self::classify(user_cmd);
        match class {
            DirectiveClass::RepoDoctor => AgentProfile {
                class,
                primary_model: "gemma-4-26b-a4b-it",
                max_turns: 8,
                budget: ContextBudget::doctor(),
                persona: "IDENTITY: NEXUS Repo Doctor. Goal: Diagnose and patch compilation errors.".into(),
            },
            _ => AgentProfile {
                class,
                primary_model: "gemma-4-26b-a4b-it",
                max_turns: 8,
                budget: ContextBudget::standard(),
                persona: "IDENTITY: NEXUS Sovereign Agent. Goal: Fulfill the directive.".into(),
            },
        }
    }
}

use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};

pub struct RuntimeMetrics {
    pub active_tasks: AtomicU64,
    pub crashed_agents: AtomicU64,
    pub emitted_events: AtomicU64,
}

impl RuntimeMetrics {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            active_tasks: AtomicU64::new(0),
            crashed_agents: AtomicU64::new(0),
            emitted_events: AtomicU64::new(0),
        }
    }

    pub fn inc_tasks(&self) {
        self.active_tasks.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }
}

impl Default for RuntimeMetrics {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

[FILE: ./crates/runtime_core/src/system_monitor.rs]

use sysinfo::{System, SystemExt, CpuExt};
use std::sync::Mutex;

pub struct SystemMonitor {
    sys: Mutex<System>,
}

impl SystemMonitor {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        let mut sys = System::new_all();
        sys.refresh_all();
        Self { sys: Mutex::new(sys) }
    }

    pub fn get_vitals(&self) -> serde_json::Value {
        let mut sys = self.sys.lock().unwrap();
        sys.refresh_cpu();
        sys.refresh_memory();

        let cpu_usage = sys.global_cpu_info().cpu_usage();
        let total_mem = sys.total_memory() / 1048576; // MB
        let used_mem = sys.used_memory() / 1048576;   // MB

        // Attempt to read the ARM64 thermal zone directly
        let temp = std::fs::read_to_string("/sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone0/temp")
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "0".to_string())
            .trim()
            .parse::<f32>()
            .unwrap_or(0.0) / 1000.0;

        serde_json::json!({
            "cpu_usage": cpu_usage,
            "memory_used_mb": used_mem,
            "memory_total_mb": total_mem,
            "temperature_c": temp,
            "timestamp": std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap().as_secs()
        })
    }
}

[FILE: ./crates/runtime_core/src/task_manager.rs]
========================================
use crate::executor::RoboticExecutor;
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;
use crate::{
    agent_protocol::AgentAction, cli_utils, json_parser, logic_engine::LogicEngine,
    metrics::METRICS, orchestrator::Orchestrator, 
    shared_state::VmoState, distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler,
    conductor::{Conductor, TurnContext}, router::IntelligentRouter,
};
use reqwest::Client;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};
use tracing::{info, error};

// Unified TaskContext for Nexus Singularity v1.9
pub struct TaskContext {
    pub client: Client,
    pub api_key: String,
    pub memory: Arc<Mutex<MemoryPalace>>,
    pub vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
    pub tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
    pub dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
}

pub async fn send_task(
    orchestrator: Arc<Orchestrator>,
    ctx: TaskContext,
    raw_directive: &str,
) -> String {
    METRICS.inc_task();
    let mut session_obs: Vec<String> = vec![];
    let mut history: Vec<(String, String)> = vec![]; 
    let mut turn = 0usize;
    let mut consecutive_fractures = 0usize;

    let _execution_law = cli_utils::get_execution_law_v6().await;
    let mut executor = RoboticExecutor::new(String::new(), ctx.tx.clone());
    let profile = IntelligentRouter::route(raw_directive);

    'mission: while turn < 12 {
        turn += 1;
        info!("=== Turn {} / 12 ===", turn);

        if LogicEngine::detect_deadlock(&session_obs) { break 'mission; }
        if consecutive_fractures >= 3 { break 'mission; }

        let turn_prompt = if turn == 1 { raw_directive.to_string() } 
                          else { format!("The last action resulted in: {}. What is the next logical step?", 
                                         session_obs.last().cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| "SUCCESS".into())) };
        
        let turn_context = TurnContext {
            orch: &orchestrator,
            client: &ctx.client,
            api_key: &ctx.api_key,
            tx: Some(ctx.tx.clone()),
        };

        let response = match Conductor::run_turn(&profile, turn_prompt.clone(), turn, turn_context, consecutive_fractures).await {
            Ok(resp) => resp,
            Err(e) => { error!("Uplink failed: {}", e); break 'mission; }
        };

        match json_parser::extract_actions(&response) {
            Ok(actions) => {
                if actions.is_empty() { break 'mission; }
                history.push((turn_prompt, response.clone()));
                consecutive_fractures = 0;
                match executor.execute_handle(actions.clone(), ctx.memory.clone(), ctx.vmo_state.clone(), ctx.dist.clone()).await {
                    Ok(observations) => { for obs in observations { session_obs.push(obs); } }
                    Err(e) => session_obs.push(format!("EXECUTOR_FAULT: {}", e)),
                }
                if actions.iter().any(|a| matches!(a, AgentAction::Halt { .. })) { break 'mission; }
            }
            Err(_) => {
                consecutive_fractures += 1;
                session_obs.push("[FORMAT_VIOLATION] Output ONLY a raw JSON array.".into());
            }
        }
    }
    "TASK_COMPLETE".to_string()
}

[FILE: ./crates/runtime_core/src/thermal_governor.rs]

use std::time::Duration;
use tokio::time::sleep;
use tracing::{warn, info};

pub struct ThermalGovernor;

impl ThermalGovernor {
    const CRITICAL_TEMP: f32 = 65.0; // Lenovo Duet throttles heavily at 70C

    pub async fn enforce_cooldown() {
        let temp_str = tokio::fs::read_to_string("/sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone0/temp").await.unwrap_or_else(|_| "0".to_string());
        let temp_c = temp_str.trim().parse::<f32>().unwrap_or(0.0) / 1000.0;

        if temp_c >= Self::CRITICAL_TEMP {
            warn!("\x1b[31m[THERMAL GOVERNOR] CRITICAL HEAT DETECTED: {:.1}°C. Enforcing 15-second DAG pause.\x1b[0m", temp_c);
            let _ = crate::metrics::METRICS.inc_failure(); // Log a thermal strain as a system fault
            sleep(Duration::from_secs(15)).await;
            info!("\x1b[32m[THERMAL GOVERNOR] Cooldown complete. Resuming operations.\x1b[0m");
        }
    }
}

[FILE: ./crates/runtime_core/src/tool_registry.rs]
========================================
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use serde_json::Value;
use std::collections::HashMap;

pub trait Tool: Send + Sync {
    fn name(&self) -> &'static str;
    fn description(&self) -> &'static str;
    fn input_schema(&self) -> Value;
    
    /// Executes the tool using the strictly validated JSON payload.
    /// In Phase 2, this will bridge to WASI capabilities.
    fn execute(&self, input: Value) -> Result<String>;
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct ToolRegistry {
    tools: HashMap<String, Box<dyn Tool>>,
}

impl ToolRegistry {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            tools: HashMap::new(),
        }
    }

    pub fn register(&mut self, tool: Box<dyn Tool>) {
        self.tools.insert(tool.name().to_string(), tool);
    }

    pub fn get_system_prompt_schema(&self) -> String {
        let mut schemas = Vec::new();
        for tool in self.tools.values() {
            schemas.push(serde_json::json!({
                "name": tool.name(),
                "description": tool.description(),
                "parameters": tool.input_schema()
            }));
        }
        format!("Available Capabilities:\n{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&schemas).unwrap())
    }

    pub fn dispatch(&self, tool_name: &str, input: Value) -> Result<String> {
        if let Some(tool) = self.tools.get(tool_name) {
            // Note: Schema validation logic against `input` will be injected here.
            tool.execute(input)
        } else {
            Err(anyhow!("CAPABILITY_DENIED: Tool '{}' is not registered or agent lacks clearance.", tool_name))
        }
    }
}

[FILE: ./crates/runtime_core/src/v5_engine/actor.rs]
========================================
use sqlx::{sqlite::SqliteConnectOptions, SqlitePool};
use std::str::FromStr;
use tokio::sync::{mpsc, oneshot};

pub enum MemoryCommand {
    CommitEvent {
        kind: String,
        object_id: String,
        respond_to: oneshot::Sender<bool>,
    },
    CommitArtifact {
        task_id: String,
        artifact_type: String,
        payload: Vec<u8>,
        respond_to: oneshot::Sender<bool>,
    },
}

pub struct MemoryActor {
    rx: mpsc::Receiver<MemoryCommand>,
    pool: SqlitePool,
}

impl MemoryActor {
    pub async fn ignite(db_url: &str) -> Result<mpsc::Sender<MemoryCommand>, sqlx::Error> {
        // Enforce WAL mode for ARM64 IO performance
        let options = SqliteConnectOptions::from_str(db_url)?
            .create_if_missing(true)
            .journal_mode(sqlx::sqlite::SqliteJournalMode::Wal)
            .synchronous(sqlx::sqlite::SqliteSynchronous::Normal);

        let pool = SqlitePool::connect_with(options).await?;

        // MSK Core Schema
        sqlx::query(
            "
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tasks (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                status TEXT NOT NULL,
                objective TEXT NOT NULL,
                created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
            );
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events (
                seq INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                kind TEXT NOT NULL,
                object_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                ts INTEGER NOT NULL
            );
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS artifacts (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                task_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                artifact_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                payload BLOB NOT NULL
            );
        ",
        )
        .execute(&pool)
        .await?;

        // Bound the queue to 64 to enforce backpressure
        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel(64);

        let actor = Self { rx, pool };

        tokio::spawn(async move {
            actor.run_loop().await;
        });

        println!(
            "\x1b[32m>> [ACTOR] MSK SQLite WAL Substrate Online. Single-writer active.\x1b[0m"
        );
        Ok(tx)
    }

    async fn run_loop(mut self) {
        while let Some(cmd) = self.rx.recv().await {
            match cmd {
                MemoryCommand::CommitEvent { kind, object_id, respond_to } => {
                    let ts = std::time::SystemTime::now()
                        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
                        .unwrap()
                        .as_secs() as i64;
                    let res =
                        sqlx::query("INSERT INTO events (kind, object_id, ts) VALUES (?, ?, ?)")
                            .bind(kind)
                            .bind(object_id)
                            .bind(ts)
                            .execute(&self.pool)
                            .await
                            .is_ok();
                    let _ = respond_to.send(res);
                }
                MemoryCommand::CommitArtifact { task_id, artifact_type, payload, respond_to } => {
                    let id = format!("{}_{}", task_id, artifact_type); // Simple compound ID
                    let res = sqlx::query("INSERT INTO artifacts (id, task_id, artifact_type, payload) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)")
                        .bind(id).bind(task_id).bind(artifact_type).bind(payload)
                        .execute(&self.pool).await.is_ok();
                    let _ = respond_to.send(res);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Hash, Eq, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum Capability {
    ReadFile,
    WriteFile,
    ExecuteProcess,
    NetworkAccess,
    MemoryModify,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct IsolationBoundary {
    pub allowed_capabilities: std::collections::HashSet<Capability>,
    pub run_as_wasm: bool,
}

impl IsolationBoundary {
    pub fn enforce(&self, intent: &Capability) -> Result<(), String> {
        if !self.allowed_capabilities.contains(intent) {
            return Err(format!(
                "SECURITY FAULT: Capability {:?} denied by sandbox isolation.",
                intent
            ));
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

[FILE: ./crates/runtime_core/src/v5_engine/graph/mod.rs]
========================================
use std::collections::{HashMap, VecDeque};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ExecutionNode {
    pub id: String,
    pub deps: Vec<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct ExecutionGraph {
    pub nodes: HashMap<String, ExecutionNode>,
}

impl ExecutionGraph {
    pub fn validate_and_sort(&self) -> Result<Vec<String>, String> {
        let mut indegree: HashMap<String, usize> = HashMap::new();
        let mut sorted = Vec::new();

        // Initialize indegrees
        for (id, node) in &self.nodes {
            indegree.entry(id.clone()).or_insert(0);
            for dep in &node.deps {
                *indegree.entry(dep.clone()).or_insert(0) += 1;
            }
        }

        let mut queue = VecDeque::new();
        for (node, degree) in &indegree {
            if *degree == 0 {
                queue.push_back(node.clone());
            }
        }

        let mut visited = 0;
        while let Some(node) = queue.pop_front() {
            visited += 1;
            sorted.push(node.clone());

            if let Some(n) = self.nodes.get(&node) {
                for dep in &n.deps {
                    if let Some(v) = indegree.get_mut(dep) {
                        *v -= 1;
                        if *v == 0 {
                            queue.push_back(dep.clone());
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if visited != self.nodes.len() {
            return Err("FRACTURE: Cyclic dependency detected in DAG. Execution aborted.".into());
        }

        // Reverse to get execution order (dependencies must run first)
        sorted.reverse();
        Ok(sorted)
    }
}

use anyhow::Result;
use wasmtime::*;

pub struct WasmExecutor;

impl WasmExecutor {
    pub fn execute(wasm: &[u8], fn_name: &str) -> Result<()> {
        let mut config = Config::new();
        config.consume_fuel(true);
        let engine = Engine::new(&config)?;
        
        let module = Module::from_binary(&engine, wasm)?;
        let mut store = Store::new(&engine, ());
        store.set_fuel(50_000)?;

        let instance = Instance::new(&mut store, &module, &[])?;
        let func = instance.get_typed_func::<(), ()>(&mut store, fn_name)?;
        func.call(&mut store, ())?;

        Ok(())
    }
}

// Stubbed for build success - original logic preserved where possible
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use base64::{engine::general_purpose, Engine as _};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
use tokio::fs;

pub struct WasmForge;

impl WasmForge {
    pub async fn synthesize_cell(logic_code: &str) -> Result<String> {
        if logic_code.trim().starts_with("const std") || logic_code.contains("export fn") {
            return Self::synthesize_zig(logic_code).await;
        }
        Self::synthesize_rust(logic_code).await
    }

    async fn synthesize_zig(zig_code: &str) -> Result<String> {
        let ts = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)?.as_secs();
        let tmp_path = format!("/tmp/nexus_zig_cell_{}.zig", ts);
        fs::write(&tmp_path, zig_code).await?;

        let output = tokio::process::Command::new("zig")
            .args(["build-exe", &tmp_path, "-target", "wasm32-freestanding", "-O", "ReleaseSmall", "--name", "cell"])
            .current_dir("/tmp")
            .output().await?;

        if !output.status.success() {
            let _ = fs::remove_file(&tmp_path).await;
            return Err(anyhow!("Zig compile error"));
        }

        let wasm_bytes = fs::read("/tmp/cell.wasm").await?;
        let b64 = general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(&wasm_bytes);
        let _ = fs::remove_file(&tmp_path).await;
        Ok(b64)
    }

    async fn synthesize_rust(_logic_code: &str) -> Result<String> {
        Err(anyhow!("Rust WASM synthesis stubbed for build"))
    }
}

[FILE: ./crates/runtime_core/src/wasm_runtime.rs]
========================================
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use wasmtime::*;

pub struct WasmRuntime;

impl WasmRuntime {
    fn make_engine() -> Result<Engine> {
        let mut config = Config::new();
        config.consume_fuel(true);
        Ok(Engine::new(&config)?)
    }

    pub fn run_bytes(bytes: &[u8], func_name: &str, input: f32) -> Result<f32> {
        let engine = Self::make_engine()?;
        let module = Module::from_binary(&engine, bytes)?;
        let mut store = Store::new(&engine, ());
        store.set_fuel(50_000_000)?;
        let instance = Instance::new(&mut store, &module, &[])?;
        let func = instance.get_typed_func::<f32, f32>(&mut store, func_name)?;
        Ok(func.call(&mut store, input)?)
    }

    pub fn run_with_buffer(bytes: &[u8], func_name: &str, data: &[u8]) -> Result<f32> {
        let engine = Self::make_engine()?;
        let module = Module::from_binary(&engine, bytes)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_LOAD_FAULT: {}", e))?;
        let mut store = Store::new(&engine, ());
        store.set_fuel(50_000_000)?;
        let instance = Instance::new(&mut store, &module, &[])
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_INST_FAULT: {}", e))?;

        let memory = instance.get_memory(&mut store, "memory")
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("WASM module must export memory"))?;

        let get_ptr = instance.get_typed_func::<(), i32>(&mut store, "get_buffer_ptr")
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("Missing get_buffer_ptr export: {}", e))?;
        let ptr = get_ptr.call(&mut store, ())? as usize;

        if data.len() > 65_536 {
            return Err(anyhow!("VMO payload exceeds 64KB sandbox."));
        }

        // WASMtime 43+ API: Use .write() directly on the memory slice
        memory.write(&mut store, ptr, data)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_MEMORY_WRITE_FAULT: {}", e))?;

        let func = instance.get_typed_func::<i32, f32>(&mut store, func_name)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_FUNC_MISSING: {}", e))?;

        Ok(func.call(&mut store, data.len() as i32)?)
    }
}

use axum::{
    extract::{Json, State},
    response::IntoResponse,
    routing::post,
    Router,
};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct ScrapeRequest {
    url: String,
}

#[derive(Serialize)]
struct ScrapeResponse {
    content: String,
}

async fn scrape(
    // AXUM RULE: State usually comes last or must match the Router's state type exactly
    State(_state): State<()>,
    Json(payload): Json<ScrapeRequest>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    // For now, we return a heartbeat to prove the drone is alive
    let response = ScrapeResponse {
        content: format!("Nexus Drone acknowledged URL: {}", payload.url),
    };
    Json(response)
}

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() {
    // Define the state explicitly as empty tuple ()
    let app = Router::new()
        .route("/scrape", post(scrape))
        .with_state(());

    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind("0.0.0.0:5000").await.unwrap();
    println!("Nexus Scraper Drone ignited on port 5000");
    axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap();
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash

echo ">> 1. Injecting sqlx dependency into bins/ide_backend/Cargo.toml..."
# Add sqlx if it's missing so the Lazarus recovery block in main.rs can compile
if ! grep -q 'sqlx =' bins/ide_backend/Cargo.toml; then
    sed -i '/\[dependencies\]/a sqlx = { version = "0.8", features = ["sqlite", "runtime-tokio"] }' bins/ide_backend/Cargo.toml
fi

echo ">> 2. Restoring bins/ide_backend/src/system_monitor.rs (v3 API + Thermal Check)..."
cat << 'INNER_EOF' > bins/ide_backend/src/system_monitor.rs
use std::fs;

pub struct Vitals {
    pub cpu_load: f32,
    pub ram_gb: f32,
    pub total_ram_gb: f32,
    pub temperature: f32,
}

pub struct SystemMonitor {
    pub thermal_zone_path: String,
}

impl SystemMonitor {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        let zone = fs::read_dir("/sys/class/thermal/")
            .ok()
            .and_then(|entries| entries.filter_map(|e| e.ok()).find(|e| 
                fs::read_to_string(e.path().join("type")).map(|s| s.contains("cpu")).unwrap_or(false)
            ))
            .map(|e| e.path().join("temp").to_string_lossy().into_owned())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| "/sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone0/temp".to_string());
        Self { thermal_zone_path: zone }
    }

    pub fn get_vitals(&mut self) -> Vitals {
        let temp = fs::read_to_string(&self.thermal_zone_path).ok()
            .and_then(|s| s.trim().parse::<f32>().ok()).map(|m| m / 1000.0).unwrap_or(60.0);
        Vitals {
            cpu_load: 0.0,
            ram_gb: 1.0,
            total_ram_gb: 4.0, // Hardcoded stubs based on your v3 audit
            temperature: temp,
        }
    }
}
INNER_EOF

echo ">> 3. Restoring bins/ide_backend/src/server.rs (Full v3 AppState)..."
cat << 'INNER_EOF' > bins/ide_backend/src/server.rs
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;
use axum::{
    extract::{State, Query},
    http::{header, StatusCode, Uri},
    response::IntoResponse,
    routing::{get, post},
    Router, Json,
};
use rust_embed::RustEmbed;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::net::TcpListener;
use nexus_runtime_core::shared_state::VmoState;
use serde_json::json;
use serde::Deserialize;

use crate::{ws_bridge, vitals_handler, cluster_api};

#[derive(RustEmbed)]
#[folder = "ui/dist/"] 
struct Assets;

pub struct AppState {
    pub client:            reqwest::Client,
    pub monitor:           Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<crate::system_monitor::SystemMonitor>>,
    pub agent:             Arc<nexus_runtime_core::neural_agent::MicroAgent>,
    pub orchestrator:      Arc<nexus_runtime_core::orchestrator::Orchestrator>,
    pub memory:            Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<MemoryPalace>>,
    pub api_key:           String,
    pub tx:                tokio::sync::broadcast::Sender<String>,
    pub arbiter:           Arc<nexus_runtime_core::arbiter_agent::ArbiterAgent>,
    pub workspace_root:    PathBuf,
    pub vmo_state:         Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<VmoState>>,
    pub cluster:           Arc<nexus_runtime_core::cluster::ClusterMesh>,
    pub dist:              Arc<nexus_runtime_core::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler>,
}

#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct Intent {
    pub directive: Option<String>,
}

pub async fn unified_gateway(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Json(intent): Json<Intent>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let directive_str = intent.directive.unwrap_or_default();
    let s = state.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let d = nexus_runtime_core::cli_utils::inject_workspace_dna(&directive_str).await;
        nexus_runtime_core::task_manager::send_task(
            s.orchestrator.clone(), &s.client, &s.api_key, &d, 
            s.memory.clone(), s.vmo_state.clone(), s.tx.clone(), s.dist.clone()
        ).await;
    });
    (StatusCode::OK, Json(json!({ "status": "QUEUED" }))).into_response()
}

async fn metrics_handler() -> impl IntoResponse {
    let text = nexus_runtime_core::metrics::get_metrics_text();
    ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "text/plain")], text)
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct FsQuery { path: Option<String> }

fn safe_path(workspace_root: &PathBuf, user_path: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, (StatusCode, Json<serde_json::Value>)> {
    if user_path.is_empty() { return Err((StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, Json(json!({ "error": "PATH_EMPTY" })))); }
    let path = std::path::Path::new(user_path);
    if path.is_absolute() || path.components().any(|c| matches!(c, std::path::Component::ParentDir)) {
        return Err((StatusCode::FORBIDDEN, Json(json!({ "error": "PATH_TRAVERSAL_DENIED" }))));
    }
    Ok(workspace_root.join(path))
}

pub async fn fs_read_handler(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Query(q): Query<FsQuery>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let user_path = q.path.unwrap_or_default();
    match safe_path(&state.workspace_root, &user_path) {
        Err(e) => e.into_response(),
        Ok(safe) => match tokio::fs::read_to_string(&safe).await {
            Ok(content) => Json(json!({ "content": content })).into_response(),
            Err(e) => (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, Json(json!({ "error": e.to_string() }))).into_response(),
        },
    }
}

pub async fn fs_write_handler(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Json(payload): Json<serde_json::Value>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let user_path = payload["path"].as_str().unwrap_or_default();
    let content   = payload["content"].as_str().unwrap_or_default();
    match safe_path(&state.workspace_root, user_path) {
        Err(e) => e.into_response(),
        Ok(safe) => {
            if let Some(parent) = safe.parent() {
                let _ = tokio::fs::create_dir_all(parent).await;
            }
            match tokio::fs::write(&safe, content).await {
                Ok(_) => Json(json!({ "status": "success" })).into_response(),
                Err(e) => (StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, Json(json!({ "error": e.to_string() }))).into_response(),
            }
        }
    }
}

pub async fn fs_list_handler(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Query(q): Query<FsQuery>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let user_path = q.path.unwrap_or_else(|| ".".into());
    match safe_path(&state.workspace_root, &user_path) {
        Err(e) => e.into_response(),
        Ok(safe) => {
            let mut entries = Vec::new();
            if let Ok(mut read_dir) = tokio::fs::read_dir(&safe).await {
                while let Ok(Some(entry)) = read_dir.next_entry().await {
                    let meta = entry.metadata().await.ok();
                    entries.push(json!({
                        "name": entry.file_name().to_string_lossy(),
                        "path": entry.path().strip_prefix(&state.workspace_root).unwrap_or(&entry.path()).to_string_lossy(),
                        "is_dir": meta.map(|m| m.is_dir()).unwrap_or(false)
                    }));
                }
            }
            Json(entries).into_response()
        }
    }
}

pub async fn start_server(state: Arc<AppState>) {
    let app = Router::new()
        .route("/api/task", post(unified_gateway))
        .route("/api/metrics", get(metrics_handler))
        .route("/api/fs/read", get(fs_read_handler))
        .route("/api/fs/write", post(fs_write_handler))
        .route("/api/fs/list", get(fs_list_handler))
        .route("/api/vitals", get(vitals_handler::handle_vitals))
        .route("/api/cluster/nodes", get(cluster_api::list_nodes))
        .route("/ws/telemetry", get(ws_bridge::ws_telemetry_handler))
        .fallback(static_handler)
        .with_state(state);
        
    let listener = TcpListener::bind("0.0.0.0:3000").await.unwrap();
    axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap();
}

async fn static_handler(uri: Uri) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let path = uri.path().trim_start_matches('/').to_string();
    let final_path = if path.is_empty() { "index.html".to_string() } else { path };
    
    match Assets::get(&final_path) {
        Some(content) => {
            let mime = mime_guess::from_path(&final_path).first_or_octet_stream();
            ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, mime.as_ref())], content.data).into_response()
        }
        None => match Assets::get("index.html") {
            Some(content) => ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "text/html")], content.data).into_response(),
            None => StatusCode::NOT_FOUND.into_response(),
        },
    }
}

#          the path is already in scope. The offending line is a double-write
#          that was never cleaned up.

import pathlib, re

p = pathlib.Path("crates/runtime_core/src/executor.rs")
txt = p.read_text()

# ── Ensure anyhow::Result is imported so single-arg Result<T> compiles ──
if "use anyhow::Result;" not in txt and "use anyhow::{" not in txt:
    # Prepend the import after the first `use` block
    txt = "use anyhow::Result;\n" + txt
    print("  added anyhow::Result import")

# ── Fix .awaitpath double-write typo ──
# Bad line (from audit):
# tokio::fs::write(tokio::fs::write(&path, content).awaitpath, content).await...
bad = re.compile(
    r'tokio::fs::write\(\s*tokio::fs::write\(&path,\s*content\)\.awaitpath,\s*content\)'
    r'\.await\.map_err\(Into::into\)\.map\(\|_\|\s*format'
)
if bad.search(txt):
    # Replace entire bad expression with the correct single write + format
    txt = bad.sub(
        'tokio::fs::write(&path, content).await.map_err(anyhow::Error::from).map(|_| format',
        txt
    )
    print("  fixed .awaitpath double-write")
else:
    # Looser fallback: fix just the .awaitpath field access
    txt = txt.replace(".awaitpath", "")
    print("  removed stray .awaitpath token")

p.write_text(txt)
print(f"  patched {p}")
PY
ok "executor.rs errors fixed"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
echo ""
info "Running cargo clippy to verify…"
echo ""
if cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings 2>&1; then
    echo ""
    echo -e "${GREEN}${BOLD}✓ All errors resolved. Workspace is clean.${RESET}"
else
    echo ""
    echo -e "${RED}Some errors remain — see output above.${RESET}"
    exit 1

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

log() { echo -e "\033[36m[NEXUS-FIX]\033[0m $*"; }
ok()  { echo -e "\033[32m[OK]\033[0m $*"; }

log "Patching Section 4 resonance wiring (manual safe version)..."

tm="crates/runtime_core/src/task_manager.rs"

if [[ -f "$tm" ]]; then
    if ! grep -q "RESONANCE_UPDATE" "$tm"; then
        # Safer append-style patch instead of fragile sed
        cat >> "$tm" << 'RUST'

        // === RESONANCE PULSE (added by remediation) ===
        if let Some(ref tx) = tx {
            let _ = tx.send(serde_json::json!({
                "type": "RESONANCE_UPDATE",
                "payload": turn
            }).to_string());
        }
RUST
        ok "task_manager.rs — RESONANCE_UPDATE event added"
    else
        ok "task_manager.rs — resonance already wired"
    fi
fi

# Ensure frontend handlers exist
rt="bins/ide_backend/ui/src/lib/core/runtime.ts"
if [[ -f "$rt" ]]; then
    if ! grep -q "RESONANCE_UPDATE" "$rt"; then
        sed -i '/\/\/ Task completed/i\                // Resonance pulse on task completion\n                if (msg.type === '\''RESONANCE_UPDATE'\'') {\n                    const pulse = Math.min(1.0, (systemVitals.resonance || 0) + 0.05);\n                    systemVitals.resonance = pulse;\n                    setTimeout(() => { systemVitals.resonance = Math.max(0, systemVitals.resonance - 0.02); }, 5000);\n                }' "$rt"
        ok "runtime.ts — RESONANCE_UPDATE handler injected"
    fi

    if ! grep -q "msg\.resonance" "$rt"; then
        sed -i 's|systemVitals\.resonance\s*=\s*msg\.resonance|systemVitals.resonance = msg.resonance ?? systemVitals.resonance|g' "$rt" 2>/dev/null || true
        ok "runtime.ts — resonance from VITALS wired"
    fi
fi

ok "Section 4 remediation completed manually"

#!/bin/bash

# Project Nexus Singularity v1.9 - Cortex Manifest
# Target: Root Setup Diagnostics

set -e # Exit immediately on uncontrolled errors

# 1. Dependency Validation
for cmd in curl jq awk; do
    if ! command -v "$cmd" &> /dev/null; then
        echo -e "\033[31m[ERROR] Critical dependency '$cmd' is not installed.\033[0m"
        exit 1
    fi
done

# 2. Environment Sourcing
if [ -f .env ]; then
    export $(grep -v '^#' .env | xargs)
fi

if [ -z "$GEMINI_API_KEY" ]; then
    echo -e "\033[31m[ERROR] GEMINI_API_KEY is not set in .env\033[0m"
    exit 1
fi

echo -e "\033[1;36m>> GENERATING FULL CORTEX MANIFEST [NEXUS v1.9 | DATE: $(date +'%b %Y')]\033[0m"
echo -e "\033[90mRetrieving all available models and capabilities via API...\033[0m\n"

# 3. Robust API Fetching
# Uses -sS to hide progress but show errors, -f to fail silently on HTTP errors
RAW_DATA=$(curl -sS -f "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key=$GEMINI_API_KEY" || echo "")

if [ -z "$RAW_DATA" ]; then
    echo -e "\033[31m[ERROR] Failed to fetch data. Verify network connection and API Key validity.\033[0m"
    exit 1
fi

# 4. Dynamic Token Formatter
format_tokens() {
    local num=$1
    if [ "$num" -ge 1000000 ]; then
        awk "BEGIN {printf \"%.1fM\", $num/1000000}"
    elif [ "$num" -ge 1000 ]; then
        awk "BEGIN {printf \"%.1fK\", $num/1000}"
    else
        echo "$num"
    fi
}

# 5. Output Generation
printf "\033[1;37m%-45s | %-8s | %-8s | %-7s | %-15s\033[0m\n" "MODEL ID" "IN_TOK" "OUT_TOK" "THINK" "METHODS"
printf "%-45s-|-%-8s-|-%-8s-|-%-7s-|-%-15s\n" "---------------------------------------------" "--------" "--------" "-------" "---------------"

# Use jq with @tsv for bulletproof array parsing, avoiding delimiter collisions
echo "$RAW_DATA" | jq -r '
    .models[] | 
    [
        .name, 
        (.inputTokenLimit // 0), 
        (.outputTokenLimit // 0), 
        (.thinking // false), 
        (.supportedGenerationMethods | join(","))
    ] | @tsv
' | while IFS=$'\t' read -r name in_lim out_lim think methods; do

    # Logic-based Color Coding
    if [[ "$name" == *"3.1"* ]] || [[ "$name" == *"3-pro"* ]]; then
        COLOR="\033[1;32m" # Green (Cutting Edge)
    elif [[ "$name" == *"gemma-4"* ]]; then
        COLOR="\033[1;35m" # Purple (Open Weights Reasoning)
    elif [[ "$name" == *"deep-research"* ]]; then
        COLOR="\033[1;33m" # Yellow (Specialist)
    elif [[ "$name" == *"image"* ]] || [[ "$name" == *"veo"* ]]; then
        COLOR="\033[1;34m" # Blue (Multimodal/Video)
    else
        COLOR="\033[0m"     # Default
    fi

    in_fmt=$(format_tokens "$in_lim")
    out_fmt=$(format_tokens "$out_lim")

    # Safe string slicing
    methods_fmt="${methods:0:15}"
    if [ "${#methods}" -gt 15 ]; then methods_fmt="${methods_fmt}..."; fi

    printf "${COLOR}%-45s\033[0m | %-8s | %-8s | %-7s | %-15s\n" "$name" "$in_fmt" "$out_fmt" "$think" "$methods_fmt"
done

echo -e "\n\033[1;36m>> CRITICAL UPDATES DETECTED FOR NEXUS BACKEND:\033[0m"
echo -e "1. \033[1;35mGemma 4 31B-it\033[0m: Peak local-style reasoning routed via API for Micro-Agents."
echo -e "2. \033[1;33mDeep Research\033[0m: Available for high-complexity structural audits."
echo -e "3. \033[1;32mGemini 3.1\033[0m: Extended 1M context with native thinking capabilities."

[FILE: ./nexus_audit_fixes.sh]

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# nexus_audit_fixes.sh — Surgical patch for all 17 audit findings
# Run from the workspace root: ~/nexus_singularity-dev/
# Each section is labeled by finding ID and can be applied independently.
# Dry-run first: bash nexus_audit_fixes.sh --dry-run
# Apply:         bash nexus_audit_fixes.sh

set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(pwd)"
DRY_RUN=false
[[ "${1:-}" == "--dry-run" ]] && DRY_RUN=true

log()  { echo -e "\e[36m[FIX]\e[0m  $*"; }
ok()   { echo -e "\e[32m[OK]\e[0m   $*"; }
warn() { echo -e "\e[33m[WARN]\e[0m $*"; }
skip() { echo -e "\e[90m[SKIP]\e[0m $*"; }
die()  { echo -e "\e[31m[ERR]\e[0m  $*" >&2; exit 1; }

apply() {
  # apply <file> <description> <old_pattern> <new_content_heredoc_var>
  # Uses Python for reliable multi-line sed replacement
  local file="$1" desc="$2" old="$3" new="$4"
  if ! grep -qF "$old" "$ROOT/$file" 2>/dev/null; then
    warn "Pattern not found in $file — already patched or file changed: $desc"
    return 0
  fi
  if $DRY_RUN; then
    skip "(dry-run) Would patch $file: $desc"
    return 0
  fi
  python3 - "$ROOT/$file" "$old" "$new" <<'PYEOF'
import sys
path, old, new = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]
with open(path, 'r') as f: src = f.read()
if old not in src:
    sys.exit(0)
with open(path, 'w') as f: f.write(src.replace(old, new, 1))
PYEOF
  ok "$file — $desc"
}

append_dep() {
  # append_dep <cargo_toml> <dep_line>
  local file="$1" dep="$2"
  if grep -qF "${dep%%[[:space:]]*=}" "$ROOT/$file" 2>/dev/null; then
    warn "Dependency already present in $file: $dep"
    return 0
  fi
  if $DRY_RUN; then skip "(dry-run) Would add dep to $file: $dep"; return 0; fi
  # Insert after [dependencies] header
  python3 - "$ROOT/$file" "$dep" <<'PYEOF'
import sys, re
path, dep = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
with open(path, 'r') as f: src = f.read()
patched = re.sub(r'(\[dependencies\]\n)', r'\1' + dep + '\n', src, count=1)
with open(path, 'w') as f: f.write(patched)
PYEOF
  ok "$file — added $dep"
}

log "CRIT-2: Replace per-call load_key() with a startup-validated, zeroized key"
apply \
  "bins/ide_backend/src/auth.rs" \
  "add ZeroizingKey type and startup validate fn" \
  'const MIN_KEY_BYTES: usize = 32;' \
'use zeroize::Zeroizing;
const MIN_KEY_BYTES: usize = 32;

/// Call once at startup. Returns a heap-allocated key that zeroes on drop.
/// Aborts with a descriptive error if the env var is missing or too short.
pub fn load_key_at_startup() -> anyhow::Result<Zeroizing<String>> {
    let key = std::env::var("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY")
        .map_err(|_| anyhow::anyhow!("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY env var is not set — refusing to start"))?;
    if key.len() < MIN_KEY_BYTES {
        return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
            "NEXUS_AUTH_KEY is too short ({} bytes). Minimum is {} bytes.",
            key.len(), MIN_KEY_BYTES
        ));
    }
    Ok(Zeroizing::new(key))
}'

log "CRIT-2: Update load_key() to read from Zeroizing<String> instead of env"

    let key = std::env::var("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY")
        .map_err(|_| anyhow!("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY env var is not set"))?;
    if key.len() < MIN_KEY_BYTES {
        return Err(anyhow!(
            "NEXUS_AUTH_KEY is too short ({} bytes). Minimum is {} bytes.",
            key.len(),
            MIN_KEY_BYTES
        ));
    }
    Ok(key)
}' \
  'fn load_key() -> Result<String> {
    // Key is validated and loaded once at startup via load_key_at_startup().
    // This shim reads it again for backward compatibility; prefer passing the
    // Zeroizing<String> from AppState directly in new code.
    let key = std::env::var("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY")
        .map_err(|_| anyhow!("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY env var is not set"))?;
    if key.len() < MIN_KEY_BYTES {
        return Err(anyhow!(
            "NEXUS_AUTH_KEY is too short ({} bytes). Minimum is {} bytes.",
            key.len(), MIN_KEY_BYTES
        ));
    }
    Ok(key)
}'

        if data.len() > 65_536 {
            return Err(anyhow!("VMO payload ({} bytes) exceeds the 64KB WASM sandbox buffer.", data.len()));
        }

  '        // Guard 1: payload size cap
        if data.len() > 65_536 {
            return Err(anyhow!("VMO payload ({} bytes) exceeds the 64KB WASM sandbox buffer.", data.len()));
        }

        // Guard 2: ptr must be within linear memory bounds
        let mem_size = memory.data_size(&store);
        if ptr.saturating_add(data.len()) > mem_size {
            return Err(anyhow!(
                "WASM_PTR_BOUNDS_FAULT: ptr=0x{:x} + len={} exceeds linear memory size={}",
                ptr, data.len(), mem_size
            ));
        }

        // 3. Write our host data directly into the WASM sandbox
        memory.write(&mut store, ptr, data)'

# MED-2: muscle.zig — shard-at-a-time snapshot in get_all_handles
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
log "MED-2: muscle.zig — replace global lock in zx_vmo_get_all_handles with per-shard scan"
apply \
  "crates/nexus_substrate/src/kernel/muscle.zig" \
  "replace all-shard lock with per-shard scan" \
  'export fn zx_vmo_get_all_handles(out_handles: [*]u32, max_count: usize) usize {
    for (&shard_mutexes) |*m| m.lock();
    defer for (&shard_mutexes) |*m| m.unlock();
    var count: usize = 0;
    for (handle_table, 0..) |maybe_meta, i| {
        if (maybe_meta) |meta| {
            if (meta.magic == SENTINEL_MAGIC and count < max_count) {
                const slot: u32 = @intCast(i);
                out_handles[count] = slot | (meta.generation << GENERATION_SHIFT);
                count += 1;
            }
        }
    }
    return count;
}' \
  '// Snapshot one shard at a time to avoid a stop-the-world pause on all 16 shards.
// Callers receive an eventually-consistent view; sufficient for monitoring use cases.
export fn zx_vmo_get_all_handles(out_handles: [*]u32, max_count: usize) usize {
    var count: usize = 0;
    for (0..SHARD_COUNT) |shard_idx| {
        shard_mutexes[shard_idx].lock();
        var slot: usize = shard_idx;
        while (slot < MAX_SLOTS) : (slot += SHARD_COUNT) {
            if (handle_table[slot]) |meta| {
                if (meta.magic == SENTINEL_MAGIC and count < max_count) {
                    out_handles[count] = @as(u32, @intCast(slot)) | (meta.generation << GENERATION_SHIFT);
                    count += 1;
                }
            }
        }
        shard_mutexes[shard_idx].unlock();
    }
    return count;
}'

# MED-3: token_ledger.rs — return Result from wait_for_quota
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
log "MED-3: token_ledger.rs — make wait_for_quota return Result so quota bypass is visible"
apply \
  "crates/nexus_substrate/src/token_ledger.rs" \
  "change wait_for_quota return type to Result" \
  'pub async fn wait_for_quota(model: &str, estimated_tokens: u32) {
    let mut attempts = 0;
    loop {
        match LEDGER.check_and_reserve(model, estimated_tokens) {
            Ok(()) => return,
            Err(wait_ms) => {
                let jitter = (generate_seed() % 500) as u64;
                println!(
                    "\x1b[33m[LEDGER] {} ceiling hit. Waiting {}ms (attempt {})...\x1b[0m",
                    model, wait_ms + jitter, attempts + 1
                );
                tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(wait_ms + jitter)).await;
                attempts += 1;
                if attempts > 10 { break; }
            }
        }
    }
}' \
  '/// Returns Err if quota could not be reserved after 10 attempts.
/// Callers MUST handle the error — proceeding on Err bypasses rate limiting
/// and will cause 429 responses from the upstream API.
pub async fn wait_for_quota(model: &str, estimated_tokens: u32) -> Result<(), String> {
    let mut attempts = 0;
    loop {
        match LEDGER.check_and_reserve(model, estimated_tokens) {
            Ok(()) => return Ok(()),
            Err(wait_ms) => {
                let jitter = (generate_seed() % 500) as u64;
                println!(
                    "\x1b[33m[LEDGER] {} ceiling hit. Waiting {}ms (attempt {})...\x1b[0m",
                    model, wait_ms + jitter, attempts + 1
                );
                tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(wait_ms + jitter)).await;
                attempts += 1;
                if attempts > 10 {
                    return Err(format!(
                        "QUOTA_EXHAUSTED: {} — could not reserve {} tokens after 10 attempts",
                        model, estimated_tokens
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
    }
}'

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# MED-5: diff.rs — tighten fuzzy_lines to require interior match ratio
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
log "MED-5: diff.rs — require ≥60% interior line match in apply_fuzzy_lines"
apply \
  "crates/runtime_core/src/diff.rs" \
  "add interior match ratio guard to fuzzy tier" \
  '    fn apply_fuzzy_lines(original: &str, search: &str, replace: &str) -> Option<String> {
        // Very coarse fallback: if search is long, try to match by first and last line
        let search_lines: Vec<&str> = search.lines().collect();
        if search_lines.len() < 3 {
            return None;
        }

        let first = search_lines[0].trim();
        let last = search_lines.last().unwrap().trim();

        let orig_lines: Vec<&str> = original.lines().collect();

        for i in 0..orig_lines.len() {
            if orig_lines[i].trim() == first {
                for j in (i + 2)..orig_lines.len() {
                    if orig_lines[j].trim() == last {
                        let mut result = orig_lines[..i].join("\n");
                        if !result.is_empty() { result.push('\n'); }
                        result.push_str(replace);
                        if j + 1 < orig_lines.len() {
                            result.push('\n');
                            result.push_str(&orig_lines[j + 1..].join("\n"));
                        }
                        return Some(result);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        None
    }' \
  '    fn apply_fuzzy_lines(original: &str, search: &str, replace: &str) -> Option<String> {
        let search_lines: Vec<&str> = search.lines().collect();
        // Require at least 8 lines — shorter blocks risk false-positive matches
        // on boilerplate lines like "}", "Ok(())", or blank lines.
        if search_lines.len() < 8 {
            return None;
        }

        let first = search_lines[0].trim();
        let last  = search_lines.last().unwrap().trim();
        let orig_lines: Vec<&str> = original.lines().collect();

        for i in 0..orig_lines.len() {
            if orig_lines[i].trim() != first { continue; }
            for j in (i + 2)..orig_lines.len() {
                if orig_lines[j].trim() != last { continue; }
                let window = &orig_lines[i..=j];
                if window.len() < search_lines.len() { continue; }
                // Require ≥60% of interior search lines to appear in the window.
                let interior = &search_lines[1..search_lines.len() - 1];
                let matched = interior.iter().filter(|&&sl| {
                    window.iter().any(|&wl| wl.trim() == sl.trim())
                }).count();
                let required = (interior.len() * 6 + 9) / 10; // ceil(60%)
                if matched < required { continue; }

                let mut result = orig_lines[..i].join("\n");
                if !result.is_empty() { result.push('\n'); }
                result.push_str(replace);
                if j + 1 < orig_lines.len() {
                    result.push('\n');
                    result.push_str(&orig_lines[j + 1..].join("\n"));
                }
                return Some(result);
            }
        }
        None
    }'

log "LOW-3: token_ledger.rs — fix misleading CSPRNG comment on generate_seed"
apply \
  "crates/nexus_substrate/src/token_ledger.rs" \
  "fix misleading CSPRNG comment on generate_seed" \
  '/// generate_seed: OS-entropy random u64 with timestamp mixing for per-call uniqueness.
/// RandomState seeds from getrandom() syscall; writing the current nanos ensures
/// two calls within the same nanosecond still return different values.
fn generate_seed() -> u64 {
    use std::hash::{BuildHasher, Hasher};
    let t = SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(SystemTime::UNIX_EPOCH)
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .as_nanos() as u64;
    let mut h = std::collections::hash_map::RandomState::new().build_hasher();
    h.write_u64(t);
    h.finish()
}' \
  '/// generate_seed: Returns a non-cryptographic random u64 suitable only for jitter.
/// Uses SipHash-1-3 (RandomState) seeded from OS entropy, mixed with the current
/// timestamp for per-call uniqueness. NOT a CSPRNG — do not use for tokens, nonces,
/// or any security-sensitive purpose. Use rand::thread_rng() for those.
fn generate_seed() -> u64 {
    use std::hash::{BuildHasher, Hasher};
    let t = SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(SystemTime::UNIX_EPOCH)
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .as_nanos() as u64;
    let mut h = std::collections::hash_map::RandomState::new().build_hasher();
    h.write_u64(t);
    h.finish()
}'

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# LOW-4: wasm_forge.rs — deduplicate cell.wasm output via timestamp name
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
log "LOW-4: wasm_forge.rs — use timestamped output name to avoid concurrent synthesis collision"
apply \
  "crates/runtime_core/src/wasm_forge.rs" \
  "use per-call timestamp in Zig WASM output filename" \
  '        let output = tokio::process::Command::new("zig")
            .args([
                "build-exe", &tmp_path,
                "-target", "wasm32-freestanding",
                "-O", "ReleaseSmall",
                "-fno-entry", "-rdynamic",
                "--name", "cell"
            ])
            .current_dir("/tmp")
            .output().await?;

        if !output.status.success() {
            let _ = fs::remove_file(&tmp_path).await;
            return Err(anyhow!("Zig compile error: {}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr)));
        }

        // Zig 0.15 writes cell.wasm to its own cwd, not /tmp
        // Try /tmp first, then fall back to current dir
        let wasm_path = if tokio::fs::metadata("/tmp/cell.wasm").await.is_ok() {
            "/tmp/cell.wasm".to_string()
        } else {
            "cell.wasm".to_string()
        };
        let wasm_bytes = fs::read(&wasm_path).await?;
        let b64 = general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(&wasm_bytes);

        let _ = fs::remove_file(&tmp_path).await;
        let _ = fs::remove_file("/tmp/cell.wasm").await;
        let _ = fs::remove_file("/tmp/cell.wasm.o").await;
        let _ = fs::remove_file("cell.wasm").await;' \
  '        // Use a per-invocation name so concurrent synthesis tasks do not overwrite
        // each other'\''s output. The source file already uses $ts; match the wasm name.
        let cell_name = format!("cell_{}", ts);
        let wasm_out  = format!("/tmp/{}.wasm", cell_name);

        let output = tokio::process::Command::new("zig")
            .args([
                "build-exe", &tmp_path,
                "-target", "wasm32-freestanding",
                "-O", "ReleaseSmall",
                "-fno-entry", "-rdynamic",
                "--name", &cell_name,
            ])
            .current_dir("/tmp")
            .output().await?;

        if !output.status.success() {
            let _ = fs::remove_file(&tmp_path).await;
            return Err(anyhow!("Zig compile error: {}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr)));
        }

        let wasm_bytes = fs::read(&wasm_out).await?;
        let b64 = general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(&wasm_bytes);

        let _ = fs::remove_file(&tmp_path).await;
        let _ = fs::remove_file(&wasm_out).await;
        let _ = fs::remove_file(format!("/tmp/{}.wasm.o", cell_name)).await;'

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# nexus_fix_secrecy_types.sh — Fix 3 type errors from secrecy 0.10 API
# Run from workspace root: ~/nexus_singularity-dev/
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(pwd)"

ok()  { echo -e "\e[32m[OK]\e[0m   $*"; }
run() { echo -e "\e[36m[RUN]\e[0m  $*"; }

python3 - "$ROOT/bins/ide_backend/src/server.rs" <<'PYEOF'
import sys
path = sys.argv[1]
with open(path, 'r') as f: src = f.read()

# Fix 1: drop the unused ExposeSecret import
src = src.replace(
    'use secrecy::{ExposeSecret, SecretString};',
    'use secrecy::SecretString;'
)

# Fix 2: api_key field is SecretString (SecretBox<str>); expose it when cloning
# The spawn closure passes api_key into send_task which expects &str.
# expose_secret() gives &str; we clone to owned String for the async move.
src = src.replace(
    'api_key: s.api_key.clone(),',
    'api_key: s.api_key.expose_secret().to_string(),'
)

# Fix 3: re-add ExposeSecret to the import now that we use it in the clone site
src = src.replace(
    'use secrecy::SecretString;',
    'use secrecy::{ExposeSecret, SecretString};'
)

with open(path, 'w') as f: f.write(src)
PYEOF
ok "server.rs — ExposeSecret usage corrected"

python3 - "$ROOT/bins/ide_backend/src/main.rs" <<'PYEOF'
import sys
path = sys.argv[1]
with open(path, 'r') as f: src = f.read()

# Fix: SecretString::new() in secrecy 0.10 takes Box<str>, not String directly.
# The compiler already suggested .into() — apply it.
src = src.replace(
    '        api_key,',
    '        api_key: api_key.into(),'
)

with open(path, 'w') as f: f.write(src)
PYEOF
ok "main.rs — api_key coerced to SecretBox<str> via .into()"

run "cargo check --workspace --quiet"
cargo check --workspace --quiet \
  && echo -e "\e[32m[OK]\e[0m   cargo check passed — workspace clean." \
  || echo -e "\e[31m[ERR]\e[0m  cargo check still failing — see output above."
