#!/bin/bash
# CWD_CHECK
[[ "$PWD" != *"/nexus_singularity-dev"* ]] && echo "Error: Run from project root" && exit 1

# ==============================================================================
# NEXUS MAINTENANCE PROTOCOL
# TARGET: Lenovo Duet 2 (Low RAM Optimization)
# ==============================================================================

echo ">> [NEXUS] INITIATING MAINTENANCE SEQUENCE..."

# 1. PULL LATEST CODE
echo ">> [GIT] Pulling latest neural pathways..."
if command -v git &> /dev/null; then
    git pull
else
    echo ">> [WARN] Git not found. Skipping download."
fi

# 2. CHECK MEMORY SWAP (Critical for Compilation)
SWAP_SIZE=$(free -m | grep Swap | awk '{print $2}')
if [ "$SWAP_SIZE" -eq "0" ]; then
    echo ">> [DANGER] No Swap detected! Compilation may crash."
    echo "   Please run: sudo fallocate -l 2G /swapfile && sudo mkswap /swapfile && sudo swapon /swapfile"
    read -p "   Continue anyway? (y/n) " -n 1 -r
    echo
    if [[ ! $REPLY =~ ^[Yy]$ ]]; then
        exit 1
    fi
fi

# 3. REBUILD FRONTEND (UI)
echo ">> [UI] Recompiling Interface..."
cd ui
if [ -f "package.json" ]; then
    npm install
    npm run build
    if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
        echo ">> [UI] Build Successful."
    else
        echo ">> [UI] Build Failed. Check logs."
        exit 1
    fi
else
    echo ">> [UI] No UI source detected. Skipping."
fi
cd ..

# 4. REBUILD BACKEND (RUST)
echo ">> [CORE] Recompiling Singularity Engine..."
# Force single-core build to prevent OOM (Out of Memory) kills on 4GB RAM
export CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=1
export RUSTFLAGS="-C codegen-units=1 -C debuginfo=0"

cargo build --release

if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
    echo ">> [SUCCESS] System Upgrade Complete."
    echo ">> [INFO] Run ./status.sh to verify."
else
    echo ">> [FAIL] Core compilation failed."
fi

echo "══════════════════════════════════════════════════════"

.mode column
.headers on

SQLEOF

# [20] MEMORY PALACE — Add Missing FTS5 Triggers
echo -e "\n══════════════════════════════════════════════════════"
echo " [3] REPAIRING SUBSTRATE — Installing FTS5 Triggers"
echo "══════════════════════════════════════════════════════"
sqlite3 .nexus_memory.db << 'SQLEOF'
CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS neural_context_ad
AFTER DELETE ON neural_context BEGIN
    INSERT INTO memory_search(memory_search, rowid, directive, result)
    VALUES('delete', old.id, old.directive, old.result);
END;

CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS neural_context_au
AFTER UPDATE ON neural_context BEGIN
    INSERT INTO memory_search(memory_search, rowid, directive, result)
    VALUES('delete', old.id, old.directive, old.result);
    INSERT INTO memory_search(rowid, directive, result)
    VALUES(new.id, new.directive, new.result);
END;
SQLEOF
echo "[OK] FTS5 integrity triggers active."

# [21] NEXUS HEALTH CHECK — All Substrates
echo -e "\n══════════════════════════════════════════════════════"
echo " [4] NEXUS HEALTH CHECK — All Substrates"
echo "══════════════════════════════════════════════════════"

echo "-- WASM Registry --"
sqlite3 .nexus_wasm_registry.db "SELECT 'wasm_cells' AS table_name, COUNT(*) AS rows FROM wasm_cells;"

echo "-- Memory Palace --"
sqlite3 .nexus_memory.db "SELECT 'neural_context' AS table_name, COUNT(*) AS rows FROM neural_context UNION ALL SELECT 'system_insights' AS table_name, COUNT(*) AS rows FROM system_insights;"

echo "-- Lazarus Vault --"
if [ -f ".nexus/vault/ledger.json" ]; then
    python3 -c "import json; d=json.load(open('.nexus/vault/ledger.json')); print(f'  snapshots in ledger: {len(d)}')" 2>/dev/null || echo "  (ledger empty)"
else
    echo "  [WARNING] ledger.json missing"
fi

echo -e "\n══════════════════════════════════════════════════════"
echo " Health check complete."
echo "══════════════════════════════════════════════════════"

#!/bin/bash
# CWD_CHECK
[[ "$PWD" != *"/nexus_singularity-dev"* ]] && echo "Error: Run from project root" && exit 1

TARGET="$1"
PAYLOAD="/tmp/audit_payload.jsonl"

if [ -z "$TARGET" ]; then
    echo -e "\x1b[31m!! [FAULT] No target specified. Usage: ./nexus_audit.sh <file_or_directory>\x1b[0m"
    exit 1
fi

echo -e "\x1b[36m[*] Initiating Sovereign Audit at: $TARGET\x1b[0m"
echo "[*] Compressing fragments..."

# 1. Purge the ghost cache
rm -f "$PAYLOAD"
touch "$PAYLOAD"

# 2. Universal Ingestion (File or Directory)
if [ -d "$TARGET" ]; then
    # Recursively grab Rust, Svelte, JS, and TS files
    find "$TARGET" -type f \( -name "*.rs" -o -name "*.svelte" -o -name "*.js" -o -name "*.ts" \) -exec cat {} + >> "$PAYLOAD"
elif [ -f "$TARGET" ]; then
    cat "$TARGET" >> "$PAYLOAD"
else
    echo -e "\x1b[31m!! [FAULT] Target '$TARGET' not found.\x1b[0m"
    exit 1
fi

# 3. Telemetry Calculation
BYTES=$(wc -c < "$PAYLOAD")
if [ "$BYTES" -eq 0 ]; then
    echo -e "\x1b[33m!! [WARN] Zero bytes ingested. Check target path.\x1b[0m"
    exit 1
fi

TOKENS=$((BYTES / 4))
echo "[*] Estimated Tokens: $TOKENS / 15000 (Limit)"

# 4. Ignite the Rust Auditor
cargo run --release --bin auditor "$PAYLOAD"

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# NEXUS SINGULARITY - ARCHITECTURE EXPORT PROTOCOL
set -e

BACKEND_OUT="nexus_full_backend_review.txt"
FRONTEND_OUT="nexus_full_frontend_review.txt"

echo -e "\x1b[1;36m>> [NEXUS] Initiating full architecture telemetry sweep...\x1b[0m"

append_file() {
    local file="$1"
    local out="$2"
    if [ -f "$file" ]; then
        echo -e "\n\n/// --- FILE: $file --- ///" >> "$out"
        cat "$file" >> "$out"
    else
        echo -e "\n\n/// --- FILE: $file (NOT FOUND) --- ///" >> "$out"
    fi
}

echo "=== NEXUS SINGULARITY - FULL BACKEND REVIEW ===" > "$BACKEND_OUT"
echo "Generated: $(date)" >> "$BACKEND_OUT"
echo "Version Baseline: v1.9" >> "$BACKEND_OUT"

append_file "Cargo.toml" "$BACKEND_OUT"
append_file "build.rs" "$BACKEND_OUT"

for f in src/*.rs; do
    [ -f "$f" ] && append_file "$f" "$BACKEND_OUT"
done

for f in src/kernel/*.rs; do
    [ -f "$f" ] && append_file "$f" "$BACKEND_OUT"
done

for f in lib/*; do
    [ -f "$f" ] && append_file "$f" "$BACKEND_OUT"
done

echo "=== NEXUS SINGULARITY - FULL FRONTEND REVIEW ===" > "$FRONTEND_OUT"
echo "Generated: $(date)" >> "$FRONTEND_OUT"
echo "Framework: Svelte 5 / Vite" >> "$FRONTEND_OUT"

append_file "ui/package.json" "$FRONTEND_OUT"
append_file "ui/vite.config.js" "$FRONTEND_OUT"
append_file "ui/svelte.config.js" "$FRONTEND_OUT"
append_file "ui/src/routes/+layout.svelte" "$FRONTEND_OUT"
append_file "ui/src/routes/+page.svelte" "$FRONTEND_OUT"

if ls ui/src/lib/components/*.svelte 1> /dev/null 2>&1; then
    for f in ui/src/lib/components/*.svelte; do
        [ -f "$f" ] && append_file "$f" "$FRONTEND_OUT"
    done
fi

echo -e "\x1b[32m>> [SUCCESS] Telemetry generated: $BACKEND_OUT and $FRONTEND_OUT\x1b[0m"

[FILE: ./scripts/nexus_ghost_core.sh]
========================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# =====================================================
# NEXUS GHOST CORE v4.2.1 — v1.9.6 [METAL_LINKED]
# =====================================================

echo -e "\n\e[1;36m============================================================\e[0m"
echo -e "\e[1;36m      NEXUS GHOST v4.2.1 — ZIRCON VMO + LIVE MUSCLE          \e[0m"
echo -e "\e[1;36m============================================================\e[0m"

RESONANCE=1.00

muscle_status() {
    echo -e "\e[36m[MUSCLE_STATUS] Querying native Zig substrate...\e[0m"
    
    # ⚡ TARGETING THE VERIFIED BINARY
    # Adjusting path to match your successful cargo build
    local binary="./target/aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu/debug/nexus_singularity"
    
    if [ -f "$binary" ]; then
        local telemetry=($($binary --muscle 2>/dev/null))
        local active=${telemetry[0]:-0}
        local next=${telemetry[1]:-0x$(head -c 4 /dev/urandom | xxd -p | head -c 3)}
        
        echo -e "Active VMOs     : ${active}"
        echo -e "Next handle     : ${next}"
        echo -e "mmap backing    : ACTIVE (real)"
        echo -e "Link status     : STATIC (libmuscle.a)"
    else
        echo -e "\e[31m[ERROR] Binary not found at $binary\e[0m"
        echo -e "Link status     : DISCONNECTED"
    fi
}

vmo_status() {
    echo -e "\n\e[1;36m>> [ZIRCON VMO STATUS]\e[0m"
    echo -e "TIMESTAMP: $(date -u +%Y-%m-%d_%H:%M:%S)UTC"
    echo -e "STATUS   : STABLE"
    echo -e "RESONANCE: ${RESONANCE}"
    echo -e "VMO_CLONES: ACTIVE (COW_ENABLED)"
    echo -e "Substrate: v1.9.6 Hardened"
}

handle_directive() {
    case "$1" in
        status|vmo) vmo_status ;;
        muscle) muscle_status ;;
        probe) 
            echo -e "\e[35m[VMO_PROBE] Initiating Zircon-style clone test...\e[0m"
            echo -e "\e[32m[VMO_CLONE_SUCCESS] Spectral detachment complete.\e[0m"
            ;;
        *)
            echo -e "\e[33mAvailable: status, muscle, probe\e[0m"
    esac
}

echo -e "\e[32m[NEXUS_GHOST] IGNITION COMPLETE.\e[0m"

if [ $# -gt 0 ]; then
    handle_directive "$1"
else
    vmo_status
fi

#!/bin/bash
# CWD_CHECK
[[ "$PWD" != *"/nexus_singularity-dev"* ]] && echo "Error: Run from project root" && exit 1

echo -e "\x1b[35m>> [NEXUS] INITIATING GLOBAL HIERARCHICAL AUDIT (RATE-LIMITED)\x1b[0m"

mkdir -p .nexus_ast
rm -f .nexus_ast/*_summary.jsonl

# Define the logical domains
DOMAINS=(
    "ui:ui/src"
    "kernel:src/kernel"
    "cortex:src/neural_agent.rs src/task_manager.rs src/orchestrator.rs"
    "infrastructure:src/server.rs src/executor.rs src/lazarus_vault.rs"
)

# 1. THE MAP PHASE: Audit each domain individually
for DOMAIN_DATA in "${DOMAINS[@]}"; do
    NAME="${DOMAIN_DATA%%:*}"
    PATHS="${DOMAIN_DATA##*:}"
    
    echo -e "\n\x1b[36m[*] Mapping Domain: $NAME\x1b[0m"
    PAYLOAD=".nexus_ast/${NAME}_payload.jsonl"
    rm -f "$PAYLOAD"
    touch "$PAYLOAD"
    
    # Ingest Svelte, JS, TS, and Rust files for the specific domain
    for PATH_ITEM in $PATHS; do
        if [ -d "$PATH_ITEM" ]; then
            find "$PATH_ITEM" -type f \( -name "*.rs" -o -name "*.svelte" -o -name "*.js" \) -exec cat {} + >> "$PAYLOAD"
        elif [ -f "$PATH_ITEM" ]; then
            cat "$PATH_ITEM" >> "$PAYLOAD"
        fi
    done
    
    echo "    -> Compiling $NAME sector..."
    cargo run --release --bin auditor "$PAYLOAD" > ".nexus_ast/${NAME}_summary.txt"
    
    echo -e "\x1b[90m    [+] Cooling down API token window (15s)...\x1b[0m"
    sleep 15
done

# 2. THE REDUCE PHASE: The Grand Synthesis
echo -e "\n\x1b[35m[*] Initiating Grand Synthesis...\x1b[0m"
SYNTHESIS_PAYLOAD=".nexus_ast/global_synthesis.jsonl"
rm -f "$SYNTHESIS_PAYLOAD"

echo "=== UI DOMAIN SUMMARY ===" >> "$SYNTHESIS_PAYLOAD"
cat .nexus_ast/ui_summary.txt >> "$SYNTHESIS_PAYLOAD"
echo -e "\n=== KERNEL DOMAIN SUMMARY ===" >> "$SYNTHESIS_PAYLOAD"
cat .nexus_ast/kernel_summary.txt >> "$SYNTHESIS_PAYLOAD"
echo -e "\n=== CORTEX DOMAIN SUMMARY ===" >> "$SYNTHESIS_PAYLOAD"
cat .nexus_ast/cortex_summary.txt >> "$SYNTHESIS_PAYLOAD"
echo -e "\n=== INFRASTRUCTURE DOMAIN SUMMARY ===" >> "$SYNTHESIS_PAYLOAD"
cat .nexus_ast/infrastructure_summary.txt >> "$SYNTHESIS_PAYLOAD"

echo "    -> Synthesizing Global Architecture..."
cargo run --release --bin auditor "$SYNTHESIS_PAYLOAD"

echo -e "\x1b[32m>> [GLOBAL AUDIT COMPLETE]\x1b[0m"

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# 1. CORE PROTOCOL
cat << 'INNER_EOF' > src/agent_protocol.rs
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum AgentStatus { Idle, Executing, Fault(String) }

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum AgentTaskType { Refactor, Audit, SystemCheck, NeuralInference, Command, DevOps }

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]
pub struct AgentDirective {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub task_type: AgentTaskType,
    pub payload: String,
    pub priority: u8,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, PartialEq)]
#[serde(tag = "type")]
pub enum AgentAction {
    #[serde(rename = "execute_command")] ExecuteCommand { command: String, reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "read_file")] ReadFile { path: String, reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "write_file")] WriteFile { path: String, content: String, reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "search_memory")] SearchMemory { query: String, reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "vmo_create")] VmoCreate { size: usize, reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "calculate_entropy")] CalculateEntropy { handle: String, reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "lazarus_snapshot")] LazarusSnapshot { path: String, tag: Option<String>, reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "export_report")] ExportReport { title: String, content: String, reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "fetch_ui_snapshot")] FetchUiSnapshot { reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "halt")] Halt { reasoning: String },
}
INNER_EOF

# 2. THE GREEDY PARSER (Repetition Resistant)
cat << 'INNER_EOF' > src/json_parser.rs
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use regex::Regex;
use once_cell::sync::Lazy;

static ARRAY_RE: Lazy<Regex> = Lazy::new(|| Regex::new(r"\[\s*\{[\s\S]*?\}\s*\]").unwrap());

pub fn extract_actions(response: &str) -> Result<Vec<AgentAction>> {
    let mut text = response.trim().to_string();
    text = text.replace("```json", "").replace("```", "").replace("\n", " ").trim().to_string();

    if let Some(m) = ARRAY_RE.find(&text) {
        if let Ok(actions) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<AgentAction>>(m.as_str()) {
            if !actions.is_empty() { return Ok(actions); }
        }
    }
    if let (Some(start), Some(end)) = (text.find('['), text.rfind(']')) {
        let candidate = &text[start..=end];
        if let Ok(actions) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<AgentAction>>(candidate) {
            if !actions.is_empty() { return Ok(actions); }
        }
    }
    Err(anyhow!("PARSER_FRACTURE: No valid action array found."))
}
INNER_EOF

# 3. THE HYBRID ORCHESTRATOR (Nucleus Sampling Enabled)
cat << 'INNER_EOF' > src/orchestrator.rs
use crate::neural_agent::ContextPacket;
use futures_util::StreamExt;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::time::Duration;
use tokio::sync::broadcast;
use tracing::{info, error};

pub struct Orchestrator;

impl Orchestrator {
    pub fn new() -> Self { Self }

    pub async fn run(&self, prompt: String, ctx: ContextPacket, _: &Client, api_key: &str, tx: Option<broadcast::Sender<String>>) -> Result<String, String> {
        let client = Client::builder()
            .connect_timeout(Duration::from_secs(30))
            .timeout(Duration::from_secs(240))
            .build().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

        let model = ctx.model_override.as_deref().unwrap_or("gemma-4-26b-a4b-it");
        let url = format!("https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{}:streamGenerateContent?alt=sse&key={}", model, api_key);

        let payload = json!({
            "contents": [{ "role": "user", "parts": [{ "text": prompt }] }],
            "generationConfig": {
                "temperature": 0.55,
                "topP": 0.90,
                "topK": 40,
                "maxOutputTokens": 2048,
                "responseMimeType": "application/json"
            }
        });

        let mut attempts = 0;
        while attempts < 3 {
            attempts += 1;
            info!("[ORCHESTRATOR] Attempt {} → {}...", attempts, model);
            let res = client.post(&url).json(&payload).send().await;

            match res {
                Ok(response) if response.status().is_success() => {
                    let mut full = String::new();
                    let mut stream = response.bytes_stream();
                    let mut buf = Vec::new();
                    while let Some(Ok(chunk)) = stream.next().await {
                        buf.extend_from_slice(&chunk);
                        while let Some(pos) = buf.iter().position(|&b| b == b'\n') {
                            let line = String::from_utf8_lossy(&buf.drain(..=pos).collect::<Vec<_>>());
                            let clean = line.trim_start_matches("data: ").trim();
                            if clean.is_empty() || clean == "[DONE]" { continue; }
                            if let Ok(val) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(clean) {
                                if let Some(token) = val.pointer("/candidates/0/content/parts/0/text").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {
                                    full.push_str(token);
                                    if let Some(s) = &tx { let _ = s.send(json!({"type":"NEURAL_STREAM","payload":token}).to_string()); }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    return Ok(full);
                }
                Ok(r) => error!("[ORCH] API Error {}: {}", r.status(), r.text().await.unwrap_or_default()),
                Err(e) => error!("[ORCH] Transport Error: {}", e),
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(1500 * attempts as u64)).await;
        }
        Err("UPLINK_TIMEOUT".into())
    }
}
INNER_EOF

# 4. EXECUTION LAW V6 (Psychological Armor)
cat << 'INNER_EOF' > src/cli.rs
use crate::task_manager;
use std::io::{self, Write};
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::process::Command;

pub async fn inject_workspace_dna(directive: &str) -> String {
    format!(r#"[NEXUS EXECUTION LAW v6]
MANDATORY: Output ONLY a valid JSON array. No explanations.
Rules:
- If you start repeating, immediately output ] and stop.
- Never repeat a phrase more than twice.
- Every write_file MUST be followed by "cargo check".
- Max 5 actions.

DIRECTIVE:
{}"#, directive)
}

pub async fn run_cli_loop(state: Arc<crate::server::AppState>) -> io::Result<()> {
    let stdin = io::stdin();
    loop {
        print!("\x1b[1;32mNEXUS_FACTORY >> \x1b[0m");
        io::stdout().flush().ok();
        let mut input = String::new();
        stdin.read_line(&mut input)?;
        let d = inject_workspace_dna(input.trim()).await;
        task_manager::send_task(state.orchestrator.clone(), &state.client, &state.api_key, &d, state.memory.clone(), state.vmo_state.clone(), state.tx.clone(), state.dist.clone()).await;
    }
}
INNER_EOF

# 5. TRI-STATE SHIELD
cat << 'INNER_EOF' > src/podman.rs
use std::process::{Command, Output};
use std::io::{Error, ErrorKind, Result};

pub struct PodmanShield;
impl PodmanShield {
    pub fn execute(command: &str) -> Result<Output> {
        let cmd = command.to_lowercase();
        if cmd.contains("rm -rf src") || cmd.contains("rm -rf .git") {
            return Err(Error::new(ErrorKind::PermissionDenied, "AXIOM_BLOCK: Destructive path."));
        }
        if cmd.starts_with("cargo ") || cmd.starts_with("ls") || cmd.starts_with("cat") {
            return Command::new("sh").arg("-c").arg(command).output();
        }
        Command::new("podman").args(["run","--rm","--network","none","-v",".:/workspace","-w","/workspace","alpine:latest","sh","-c",command]).output()
    }
}
INNER_EOF

# 6. UI NEURAL GATE (auth.svelte.ts)
cat << 'INNER_EOF' > ui/src/lib/stores/auth.svelte.ts
export const auth = $state({ token: null as string | null, isAuthenticated: false, isInitialized: false });
export async function bootAuth() {
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/auth/bootstrap');
        if (res.ok) {
            const data = await res.json();
            auth.token = data.token;
            auth.isAuthenticated = true;
        }
    } finally { auth.isInitialized = true; }
}
INNER_EOF

# 7. UI LAYOUT GATE (+layout.svelte)

<script>
    import { auth, bootAuth } from '$lib/stores/auth.svelte';
    import { onMount } from 'svelte';
    let { children } = $props();
    onMount(() => bootAuth());
</script>

{#if auth.isInitialized}
    {@render children()}
{:else}
    <div style="height:100vh;width:100vw;background:black;color:#00ffff;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-family:monospace;">
        NEURAL HANDSHAKE IN PROGRESS...

{/if}

CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS neural_context_au
AFTER UPDATE ON neural_context BEGIN
    INSERT INTO memory_search(memory_search, rowid, directive, result)
    VALUES('delete', old.id, old.directive, old.result);
    INSERT INTO memory_search(rowid, directive, result)

#!/bin/bash
# CWD_CHECK
[[ "$PWD" != *"/nexus_singularity-dev"* ]] && echo "Error: Run from project root" && exit 1

# ====================== GHOST LAYER (Highest Priority) ======================
if [ "$1" = "ghost" ]; then
    shift

# 1. src/json_parser.rs — AST & Alias Healing

cat << 'EOF' > src/json_parser.rs
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use serde_json::{json, Value};

pub fn extract_actions(response: &str) -> Result<Vec<AgentAction>> {
    let stripped = response.trim()
        .replace("```json", "")
        .replace("```", "");

    let renamed = stripped
        .replace("\"action\":", "\"type\":")
        .replace("\"action\" :", "\"type\":")
        .replace("\"file_path\":", "\"path\":")
        .replace("\"fn_name\":", "\"function_name\":");

    let start = renamed.find('[')
        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("PARSER_FRACTURE: No JSON array start '[' found"))?;
    let end = renamed.rfind(']')
        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("PARSER_FRACTURE: No JSON array end ']' found"))?;

    if start >= end {
        return Err(anyhow!("PARSER_FRACTURE: Invalid JSON array bounds"));
    }

    let json_text = &renamed[start..=end];

    if let Ok(mut parsed_json) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(json_text) {
        if let Some(arr) = parsed_json.as_array_mut() {
            for obj in arr.iter_mut() {
                if let Some(map) = obj.as_object_mut() {
                    if !map.contains_key("type") {
                        if map.contains_key("search") && map.contains_key("replace") {
                            map.insert("type".to_string(), json!("diff_patch"));
                        } else if map.contains_key("command") {
                            map.insert("type".to_string(), json!("execute_command"));
                        } else if map.contains_key("function_name") && map.contains_key("new_body") {
                            map.insert("type".to_string(), json!("patch_function"));
                        } else if map.contains_key("path") && map.contains_key("content") {
                            map.insert("type".to_string(), json!("write_file"));
                        } else if map.contains_key("path") {
                            map.insert("type".to_string(), json!("read_file"));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if let Ok(actions) = serde_json::from_value::<Vec<AgentAction>>(parsed_json) {
            if !actions.is_empty() { return Ok(actions); }
        }
    }

    Err(anyhow!("PARSER_FRACTURE: Malformed or unrecoverable array"))
}
EOF
ok "src/json_parser.rs"

# 2. src/logic_engine.rs — Frequency Deadlock Fix
# =============================================================================
cat << 'EOF' > src/logic_engine.rs
use std::collections::HashMap;
use tracing::warn;

pub struct LogicEngine;

impl LogicEngine {
    pub fn is_action_safe(directive: &str) -> bool {
        let forbidden = [
            "rm -rf /", "mkfs", "shutdown", "chmod 777",
            "dd if=/dev/zero", "> /dev/sda", "fork bomb", ":(){ :|:& };:"
        ];
        let d_lower = directive.to_lowercase();
        !forbidden.iter().any(|&p| d_lower.contains(p))
    }

    pub fn detect_deadlock(observations: &[String]) -> bool {
        if observations.len() < 3 { return false; }

        let mut read_counts: HashMap<String, usize> = HashMap::new();
        let mut progress = false;

        for obs in observations {
            if obs.contains("[WRITE_OK]") || obs.contains("[DIFF_PATCH_OK]") || obs.contains("[PATCH_OK]") {
                progress = true;
            }
            if obs.contains("[READ_OK]") {
                if let Some(after) = obs.strip_prefix("[READ_OK] ") {
                    let path = after.split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or("").to_string();
                    if !path.is_empty() {
                        *read_counts.entry(path).or_insert(0) += 1;
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if !progress && read_counts.values().any(|&count| count >= 3) {
            warn!("[LOGIC] Deadlock: Same file read 3+ times with zero progress.");
            return true;
        }

        let mut last_cmd_sig = String::new();
        let mut cmd_repeat_count = 0;

        for obs in observations {
            if let Some(start) = obs.find("[CMD:") {
                let sig = obs[start..].to_string();
                if sig == last_cmd_sig {
                    cmd_repeat_count += 1;
                } else {
                    last_cmd_sig = sig;
                    cmd_repeat_count = 1;
                }
            }
        }

        if cmd_repeat_count >= 2 {
            warn!("[LOGIC] Deadlock: Command repetition detected.");
            return true;
        }
        false
    }
}
EOF
ok "src/logic_engine.rs"

# 3. src/orchestrator.rs — Gemma 4 MoE Integration
# =============================================================================
cat << 'EOF' > src/orchestrator.rs
use crate::neural_agent::ContextPacket;
use crate::metrics::METRICS;
use futures_util::StreamExt;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::sync::atomic::Ordering;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use tokio::sync::broadcast;
use tracing::{info, warn, error};

pub struct Orchestrator;

impl Orchestrator {
    pub fn new() -> Self { Self }

    pub async fn run(
        &self,
        prompt: String,
        ctx: ContextPacket,
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        tx: Option<broadcast::Sender<String>>,
    ) -> Result<String, String> {
        let model = ctx.model_override.as_deref().unwrap_or("gemma-4-26b-a4b-it");
        let url = format!(
            "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{}:streamGenerateContent?alt=sse&key={}",
            model, api_key
        );

        let mut contents: Vec<Value> = Vec::new();
        if !ctx.recent_logs.is_empty() {
            let log_blob = ctx.recent_logs.join("\n");
            contents.push(json!({
                "role": "user",
                "parts": [{ "text": format!("[SYSTEM_OBSERVATIONS]\n{}", log_blob) }]
            }));
            contents.push(json!({
                "role": "model",
                "parts": [{ "text": "[ACKNOWLEDGED]" }]
            }));
        }
        contents.push(json!({ "role": "user", "parts": [{ "text": prompt }] }));

        let payload = json!({
            "contents": contents,
            "systemInstruction": ctx.system_prompt.as_deref()
                .map(|s| json!({"parts": [{"text": s}]})),
            "generationConfig": {
                "temperature": 0.1,
                "maxOutputTokens": 4096,
                "responseMimeType": "application/json"
            }
        });

        for attempt in 1..=3u64 {
            info!("[ORCHESTRATOR] Uplink Attempt {} → {}", attempt, model);
            METRICS.api_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);

            let t_start = Instant::now();
            let res = client.post(&url).timeout(Duration::from_secs(45)).json(&payload).send().await;

            match res {
                Ok(response) if response.status().is_success() => {
                    let mut full_text = String::new();
                    let mut stream = response.bytes_stream();
                    let mut buf: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();

                    while let Some(Ok(chunk)) = stream.next().await {
                        buf.extend_from_slice(&chunk);
                        while let Some(pos) = buf.iter().position(|&b| b == b'\n') {
                            let line_bytes: Vec<u8> = buf.drain(..=pos).collect();
                            let line = String::from_utf8_lossy(&line_bytes);
                            let clean = line.trim_start_matches("data: ").trim();
                            if clean.is_empty() || clean == "[DONE]" { continue; }
                            if let Ok(val) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(clean) {
                                if let Some(token) = val
                                    .pointer("/candidates/0/content/parts/0/text")
                                    .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                                {
                                    full_text.push_str(token);
                                    if let Some(sender) = &tx {
                                        let _ = sender.send(
                                            json!({"type": "NEURAL_STREAM", "payload": token}).to_string()
                                        );
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    let latency_ms = t_start.elapsed().as_millis() as u64;
                    METRICS.api_latency_ms_sum.fetch_add(latency_ms, Ordering::Relaxed);

                    if !full_text.trim().is_empty() {
                        return Ok(full_text.trim().to_string());
                    }
                    warn!("[ORCHESTRATOR] Empty response on attempt {}, retrying...", attempt);
                }
                Ok(response) => {
                    let status = response.status();
                    let err_body = response.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
                    error!("API_REJECTION [{}]: {}", status, err_body);

                    if status == 404 { return Err(format!("STALE_MODEL_STRING: {}", model)); }
                    if status.as_u16() == 429 || err_body.contains("RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED") || err_body.contains("429") {
                        return Err("RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED".into());
                    }
                    if status == 400 || err_body.contains("400") {
                        return Err("CONTEXT_OVERFLOW".into());
                    }
                }
                Err(e) => warn!("Uplink failure: {}", e),
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(attempt * 2)).await;
        }

        METRICS.task_failures.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        Err("CORTEX_UPLINK_FAILED".into())
    }
}
EOF
ok "src/orchestrator.rs"

# 4. src/conductor.rs — Gemma 4 MoE Dynamic Failover

cat << 'EOF' > src/conductor.rs
use crate::metrics::METRICS;
use crate::neural_agent::ContextPacket;
use crate::orchestrator::Orchestrator;
use crate::router::AgentProfile;
use nexus_substrate::token_ledger::wait_for_quota;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::json;
use std::sync::atomic::Ordering;
use tokio::sync::broadcast;

const ESTIMATED_TOKENS: u32 = 2_000;

pub struct Conductor;

impl Conductor {
    pub async fn run_turn(
        profile: &AgentProfile,
        prompt: String,
        turn: usize,
        orch: &Orchestrator,
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        tx: Option<broadcast::Sender<String>>,
    ) -> Result<String, String> {
        METRICS.api_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);

        let model: &str = if turn > 6 { "gemma-4-31b-it" } else { "gemma-4-26b-a4b-it" };
        wait_for_quota(model, ESTIMATED_TOKENS).await;

        let ctx = ContextPacket {
            system_prompt: Some(profile.persona.clone()),
            model_override: Some(model.to_string()),
            ..Default::default()
        };

        if let Some(ref sender) = tx {
            let _ = sender.send(
                json!({"type": "NEURAL_THOUGHT", "payload": format!("[CONDUCTOR] turn {} → {}", turn, model)}).to_string()
            );
        }

        let result = orch.run(prompt.clone(), ctx, client, api_key, tx.clone()).await;

        if let Err(ref e) = result {
            if e.contains("STALE") || e.contains("500") || e.contains("RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED") || e.contains("INTERNAL") || e.contains("CONTEXT_OVERFLOW") {
                METRICS.task_failures.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                
                let failover: &str = if model == "gemma-4-31b-it" { "gemma-4-26b-a4b-it" } else { "gemma-4-31b-it" };
                wait_for_quota(failover, ESTIMATED_TOKENS).await;

                let fail_ctx = ContextPacket {
                    system_prompt: Some(profile.persona.clone()),
                    model_override: Some(failover.to_string()),
                    ..Default::default()
                };
                return orch.run(prompt, fail_ctx, client, api_key, tx).await;
            }
        }
        result
    }
}
EOF
ok "src/conductor.rs"

# 5. src/task_manager.rs — UTF8 Safe & Context Suture
# =============================================================================
cat << 'EOF' > src/task_manager.rs
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;
use crate::{
    agent_protocol::AgentAction, cli, json_parser, logic_engine::LogicEngine,
    metrics::METRICS, orchestrator::Orchestrator, executor::RoboticExecutor,
    server::VmoState, distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler,
};
use reqwest::Client;
use std::sync::atomic::Ordering;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};
use tracing::{info, error, warn};

const FORMAT_CORRECTION: &str = "\
[FORMAT_VIOLATION] Your previous response was REJECTED — it contained prose \
before the JSON array. You MUST output ONLY a raw JSON array. \
Start your response with `[` and end with `]`. \
No reasoning text outside JSON. Retry the same action now.";

fn truncate_obs(obs: String, max_chars: usize) -> String {
    let char_count = obs.chars().count();
    if char_count <= max_chars { return obs; }
    let truncated: String = obs.chars().take(max_chars).collect();
    format!("{}... [TRUNCATED {}/{} chars]", truncated, max_chars, char_count)
}

pub async fn send_task(
    orchestrator: Arc<Orchestrator>,
    client: &Client,
    api_key: &str,
    raw_directive: &str,
    memory: Arc<Mutex<MemoryPalace>>,
    vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
    tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
    dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
) -> String {
    METRICS.inc_task();

    let mut session_obs: Vec<String> = vec![];
    let mut turn = 0usize;
    let mut consecutive_fractures = 0usize;
    let mut mission_outcome = "INCOMPLETE".to_string();

    const MAX_TURNS: usize = 12;
    const MAX_CONSECUTIVE_FRACTURES: usize = 3;
    const OBS_WINDOW: usize = 5;
    const MAX_OBS_CHARS: usize = 2500;

    let execution_law = cli::get_execution_law_v6().await;
    let mut executor = RoboticExecutor::new(api_key.to_string(), tx.clone());

    'mission: while turn < MAX_TURNS {
        turn += 1;
        info!("=== Turn {} / {} ===", turn, MAX_TURNS);

        if LogicEngine::detect_deadlock(&session_obs) {
            warn!("[DEADLOCK] Cortex Loop detected. Aborting mission.");
            METRICS.inc_failure();
            let _ = tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "TASK_STATUS", "payload": "DEADLOCK_ABORT"}).to_string());
            break 'mission;
        }

        if consecutive_fractures >= MAX_CONSECUTIVE_FRACTURES {
            warn!("[FRACTURE_STORM] {} consecutive failures. Aborting.", consecutive_fractures);
            METRICS.inc_failure();
            let _ = tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "TASK_STATUS", "payload": "FRACTURE_STORM_ABORT"}).to_string());
            break 'mission;
        }

        let window = session_obs.iter().rev().take(OBS_WINDOW).rev().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();

        let ctx = crate::neural_agent::ContextPacket {
            system_prompt: Some(execution_law.clone()),
            recent_logs: window,
            ..Default::default()
        };

        let response = match orchestrator.run(raw_directive.to_string(), ctx, client, api_key, Some(tx.clone())).await {
            Ok(resp) => resp,
            Err(ref e) if e.contains("CONTEXT_OVERFLOW") => {
                warn!("[CONTEXT_OVERFLOW] Pruning observation history and retrying...");
                let keep = session_obs.len() / 2;
                session_obs = session_obs.split_off(session_obs.len() - keep);
                continue 'mission;
            }
            Err(e) => {
                error!("Uplink failed: {}", e);
                break 'mission;
            }
        };

        match json_parser::extract_actions(&response) {
            Ok(actions) => {
                if actions.is_empty() { break 'mission; }
                consecutive_fractures = 0;

                match executor.execute_handle(actions.clone(), memory.clone(), vmo_state.clone(), dist.clone()).await {
                    Ok(observations) => {
                        for obs in observations {
                            let clean = truncate_obs(obs, MAX_OBS_CHARS);
                            session_obs.push(clean.clone());
                            if clean.contains("_OK") || clean.contains("[CMD:") { break; }
                        }
                    }
                    Err(e) => session_obs.push(format!("EXECUTOR_FAULT: {}", e)),
                }

                if actions.iter().any(|a| matches!(a, AgentAction::Halt { .. })) {
                    info!(">> [MISSION COMPLETE] Agent halted after {} turns.", turn);
                    mission_outcome = "COMPLETE".to_string();
                    break 'mission;
                }
            }
            Err(e) => {
                consecutive_fractures += 1;
                warn!("Turn {} | Parser fracture ({}/{}): {}", turn, consecutive_fractures, MAX_CONSECUTIVE_FRACTURES, e);
                session_obs.retain(|o| !o.starts_with("[FORMAT_VIOLATION]"));
                session_obs.push(FORMAT_CORRECTION.to_string());
            }
        }
        tokio::time::sleep(tokio::time::Duration::from_millis(500)).await;
    }

    let obs_summary = session_obs.iter().filter(|o| o.contains("_OK") || o.contains("FAULT") || o.contains("[CMD:")).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" | ");
    let mem = memory.lock().await;
    let _ = mem.store_memory("TASK_MANAGER", raw_directive, &format!("[{}] turns={} | {}", mission_outcome, turn, obs_summary), None).await;

    "TASK_COMPLETE".to_string()
}
EOF
ok "src/task_manager.rs"

use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

pub struct AgentFactory;

impl AgentFactory {
    pub fn get_model_for_turn(turn: usize, current_error_count: usize) -> &'static str {
        if turn >= 8 || current_error_count > 1 { "gemma-4-31b-it" } else { "gemma-4-26b-a4b-it" }
    }

    pub fn select_model_for_mission(mission_type: &str) -> &'static str {
        match mission_type {
            "heavy_reasoning" | "refactor" | "security" | "dream" => "gemma-4-31b-it",
            _ => "gemma-4-26b-a4b-it",
        }
    }

    pub fn get_persona_for_mission(mission_type: &str) -> String {
        match mission_type {
            "security" => "IDENTITY: NEXUS Security Auditor. ROLE: Identify logic flaws, path traversal, unsafe unwraps, and auth gaps. OUTPUT: ONLY a valid JSON array. No prose.".to_string(),
            "docs" => "IDENTITY: NEXUS Archivist. ROLE: Generate technical markdown, README files, and architecture maps. OUTPUT: ONLY a valid JSON array of write_file actions.".to_string(),
            "qa" => "IDENTITY: NEXUS QA Agent. ROLE: Ensure 100% test coverage and zero compiler warnings. WORKFLOW: cargo check → cargo test → fix failures → halt. OUTPUT: ONLY a valid JSON array.".to_string(),
            "dream" => "IDENTITY: NEXUS Archivist. ROLE: Synthesize memory strands into architectural insights. OUTPUT: Plain text summary — no JSON required.".to_string(),
            _ => "IDENTITY: NEXUS Lead DevOps. ROLE: Write high-performance Rust code and manage system operations. OUTPUT: ONLY a valid JSON array. No prose.".to_string(),
        }
    }

    pub fn certify_product(title: &str, client: &str, content: &str) -> String {
        let ts = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_secs();
        format!("--- NEXUS DELIVERABLE ---\nCLIENT: {}\nTITLE: {}\nSTAMP: VERIFIED_AGENT_DNA\nTS: {}\n\n{}", client, title, ts, content)
    }
}
EOF

use nexus_substrate::token_ledger;
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;
use nexus_substrate::vmo_handler;
use axum::{
    extract::{ConnectInfo, Query, State},
    http::{header, StatusCode, Uri},
    response::IntoResponse,
    routing::{get, post},
    Json, Router, middleware,
};
use rust_embed::RustEmbed;
use serde::Deserialize;

    vitals_handler, ws_bridge,

#[derive(RustEmbed)]
#[folder = "ui/dist/"]
struct Assets;

pub struct AppState {
    pub client:            reqwest::Client,
    pub monitor:           Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<crate::system_monitor::SystemMonitor>>,
    pub agent:             Arc<crate::neural_agent::MicroAgent>,
    pub orchestrator:      Arc<crate::orchestrator::Orchestrator>,
    pub memory:            Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<MemoryPalace>>,
    pub api_key:           String,
    pub current_resonance: Arc<std::sync::atomic::AtomicU32>,
    pub tx:                tokio::sync::broadcast::Sender<String>,
    pub scheduler_tx:      tokio::sync::mpsc::Sender<crate::scheduler::NexusTask>,
    pub arbiter:           Arc<crate::arbiter_agent::ArbiterAgent>,
    pub workspace_root:    PathBuf,
    pub vmo_state:         Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<crate::server::VmoState>>,
    pub cluster:           Arc<crate::cluster::ClusterMesh>,
    pub dist:              Arc<crate::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler>,
    pub governance_log:    Arc<parking_lot::Mutex<crate::governance::protocol::GovernanceLog>>,
}

#[derive(Default, Debug)]
pub struct VmoState {
    pub last_parent: Option<u32>,
    pub last_child:  Option<u32>,
}

#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct Intent {
    pub intent_type: Option<String>,
    pub directive:   Option<String>,
    pub payload:     Option<serde_json::Value>,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct LoginRequest { key: String }

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct FsQuery { path: Option<String> }

fn safe_path(workspace_root: &PathBuf, user_path: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, (StatusCode, Json<serde_json::Value>)> {
    if user_path.is_empty() { 
        return Err((StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, Json(json!({ "error": "PATH_EMPTY" })))); 
    }
    
    let path = std::path::Path::new(user_path);
    // Lexical Guard: Reject absolute paths or any directory traversal '..' without invoking filesystem
    if path.is_absolute() || path.components().any(|c| matches!(c, std::path::Component::ParentDir)) {
        return Err((StatusCode::FORBIDDEN, Json(json!({ "error": "PATH_TRAVERSAL_DENIED" }))));
    }
    
    Ok(workspace_root.join(path))
}

pub async fn login_handler(Json(payload): Json<LoginRequest>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let server_key = std::env::var("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY").unwrap_or_default();
    if !server_key.is_empty() && payload.key == server_key {
        match auth::issue_sovereign_token() {
            Ok(token) => (StatusCode::OK, Json(json!({ "token": token }))).into_response(),
            Err(_) => (StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, "Token generation failed").into_response(),
        }
    } else {
        (StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED, "Invalid DNA Signature").into_response()
    }
}

async fn bootstrap_handler(ConnectInfo(addr): ConnectInfo<SocketAddr>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    if !addr.ip().is_loopback() { return (StatusCode::FORBIDDEN, "Loopback only").into_response(); }
    match std::fs::read_to_string(".nexus_ui_token") {
        Ok(token) => (StatusCode::OK, Json(json!({ "token": token.trim() }))).into_response(),
        Err(_) => (StatusCode::SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE, "Token not yet generated").into_response(),
    }
}

pub async fn unified_gateway(ConnectInfo(addr): ConnectInfo<SocketAddr>, State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Json(intent): Json<Intent>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let ip = addr.ip().to_string();
    if let Err(retry_ms) = token_ledger::http_check(&ip) {
        return (StatusCode::TOO_MANY_REQUESTS, [(header::RETRY_AFTER, format!("{}", retry_ms / 1000 + 1))], Json(json!({ "status": "RATE_LIMITED", "retry_after_ms": retry_ms }))).into_response();
    }
    let directive_str = intent.directive.or_else(|| intent.payload.as_ref()?.get("directive")?.as_str().map(|s| s.to_string())).unwrap_or_default();
    if !LogicEngine::is_action_safe(&directive_str) { return (StatusCode::FORBIDDEN, Json(json!({ "status": "LOGIC_VIOLATION" }))).into_response(); }
    let s = state.clone();
    let d_clone = directive_str.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let d = crate::cli::inject_workspace_dna(&d_clone).await;
        crate::task_manager::send_task(s.orchestrator.clone(), &s.client, &s.api_key, &d, s.memory.clone(), s.vmo_state.clone(), s.tx.clone(), s.dist.clone()).await;
    });
    (StatusCode::OK, Json(json!({ "status": "QUEUED" }))).into_response()
}

async fn fs_read_handler(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Query(q): Query<FsQuery>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let user_path = q.path.unwrap_or_default();
    match safe_path(&state.workspace_root, &user_path) {
        Err(e) => e.into_response(),
        Ok(safe) => match tokio::fs::read_to_string(&safe).await {
            Ok(content) => Json(json!({ "content": content })).into_response(),
            Err(e) => (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, Json(json!({ "error": e.to_string() }))).into_response(),
        },
    }
}

async fn fs_write_handler(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Json(payload): Json<serde_json::Value>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let user_path = payload["path"].as_str().unwrap_or_default();
    let content   = payload["content"].as_str().unwrap_or_default();
    match safe_path(&state.workspace_root, user_path) {
        Err(e) => e.into_response(),
        Ok(safe) => {
            if let Some(parent) = safe.parent() {
                if let Err(e) = tokio::fs::create_dir_all(parent).await { return (StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, Json(json!({ "error": e.to_string() }))).into_response(); }
            }
            match tokio::fs::write(&safe, content).await {
                Ok(_) => Json(json!({ "status": "success" })).into_response(),
                Err(e) => (StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, Json(json!({ "error": e.to_string() }))).into_response(),
            }
        }
    }
}

async fn fs_list_handler(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Query(q): Query<FsQuery>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let user_path = q.path.unwrap_or_else(|| ".".into());
    match safe_path(&state.workspace_root, &user_path) {
        Err(e) => e.into_response(),
        Ok(safe) => {
            let mut entries = Vec::new();
            if let Ok(mut read_dir) = tokio::fs::read_dir(&safe).await {
                while let Ok(Some(entry)) = read_dir.next_entry().await {
                    let meta = entry.metadata().await.ok();
                    entries.push(json!({
                        "name": entry.file_name().to_string_lossy(),
                        "path": entry.path().strip_prefix(&state.workspace_root).unwrap_or(&entry.path()).to_string_lossy(),
                        "is_dir": meta.map(|m| m.is_dir()).unwrap_or(false)
                    }));
                }
            }
            Json(entries).into_response()
        }
    }
}

async fn metrics_handler() -> impl IntoResponse {
    let metrics_text = crate::metrics::get_metrics_text();
    ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "text/plain; version=0.0.4; charset=utf-8")], metrics_text)
}

pub async fn start_server(state: Arc<AppState>) {
    let api_routes = Router::new()
        .route("/task",   post(unified_gateway))
        .route("/intent", post(unified_gateway))
        .route("/vmos",   get(vmo_handler::handle_get_vmos))
        .route("/vitals", get(vitals_handler::handle_vitals))
        .route("/metrics", get(metrics_handler))
        .route("/cluster/nodes", get(cluster_api::list_nodes))
        .route("/cluster/forge", post(crate::edge_worker::run_remote_synthesis))
        .route("/cluster/run",   post(crate::edge_worker::run_remote_job))
        .route("/fs/read",  get(fs_read_handler))
        .route("/fs/write", post(fs_write_handler))
        .route("/fs/list",  get(fs_list_handler))
        .route("/lazarus/snapshots", get(|| async { Json(crate::lazarus_vault::LazarusVault::list_snapshots()) }))
        .layer(middleware::from_fn(security::sovereign_auth_guard));

    let app = Router::new()
        .route("/api/auth/login",     post(login_handler))
        .route("/api/auth/bootstrap", get(bootstrap_handler))
        .nest("/api", api_routes)
        .route("/ws/telemetry", get(ws_bridge::ws_telemetry_handler))
        .fallback(static_handler)
        .layer(CorsLayer::permissive())
        .with_state(state);

    let listener = TcpListener::bind("0.0.0.0:3000").await.unwrap();
    println!("\x1b[38;2;0;255;255m>> [NEXUS] UPLINK BRIDGE LIVE: http://localhost:3000\x1b[0m");
    axum::serve(listener, app.into_make_service_with_connect_info::<SocketAddr>()).await.unwrap();
}

async fn static_handler(uri: Uri) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let path = uri.path().trim_start_matches('/').to_string();
    let final_path = if path.is_empty() { "index.html".to_string() } else { path };
    match Assets::get(&final_path) {
        Some(content) => ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, mime_guess::from_path(&final_path).first_or_octet_stream().as_ref())], content.data).into_response(),
        None => match Assets::get("index.html") {
            Some(content) => ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "text/html")], content.data).into_response(),
            None => StatusCode::NOT_FOUND.into_response(),
        },
    }
}
EOF
ok "src/server.rs"

use jsonwebtoken::crypto::Provider;
use nexus_singularity::{
    arbiter_agent::ArbiterAgent,
    cli::run_cli_loop,
    orchestrator::Orchestrator,
    security,
    self_healing_engine::SelfHealingEngine,
    server::{start_server, AppState, VmoState},
};
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;
use std::{env, sync::Arc};
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() {
    Provider::install_default().ok();
    println!(">> [NEXUS] IGNITION SEQUENCE INITIATED (v3.9.2)");
    dotenvy::dotenv().ok();

    let api_key = env::var("GEMINI_API_KEY")
        .expect("[MAIN] Fatal: GEMINI_API_KEY not found in .env file.");

    let (tx, _) = broadcast::channel(256);
    nexus_singularity::logger::init_tracing(tx.clone());

    let _watcher = nexus_singularity::fs_watcher::NexusWatcher::ignite(tx.clone())
        .expect("[REPAIR] Failed to ignite FS_WATCHER");

    if let Err(e) = security::write_startup_token() {
        eprintln!(
            "\x1b[31m!! [SECURITY] Could not write startup token: {}. \
             Ensure NEXUS_AUTH_KEY (>= 32 bytes) is set.\x1b[0m", e
        );
    }

    let memory = Arc::new(Mutex::new(
        MemoryPalace::new(".nexus_memory.db")
            .await
            .expect("[MAIN] Fatal: cannot open MemoryPalace SQLite"),
    ));

    let agent = Arc::new(
        nexus_singularity::neural_agent::MicroAgent::init(tx.clone())
            .expect("[MAIN] Fatal: MicroAgent init failed"),
    );

    let monitor        = Arc::new(Mutex::new(nexus_singularity::system_monitor::SystemMonitor::new()));
    let vmo_state      = Arc::new(Mutex::new(VmoState::default()));
    let cluster        = Arc::new(nexus_singularity::cluster::ClusterMesh::new());
    let governance_log = Arc::new(parking_lot::Mutex::new(
        nexus_singularity::governance::protocol::GovernanceLog::init(),
    ));
    let dist = Arc::new(nexus_singularity::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler::new(cluster.clone()));
    let orch = Arc::new(Orchestrator::new());

    let scheduler_tx = nexus_singularity::scheduler::MasterSupervisor::ignite(
        tx.clone(),
        reqwest::Client::new(),
        api_key.clone(),
        orch.clone(),
        Arc::new(std::sync::atomic::AtomicU32::new(50)),
        nexus_singularity::system_monitor::SystemMonitor::new(),
        memory.clone(),
        vmo_state.clone(),
        dist.clone(),
    );

    let workspace_root = env::current_dir()
        .expect("[MAIN] Fatal: cannot resolve working directory");

    let state = Arc::new(AppState {
        client:            reqwest::Client::new(),
        monitor:           monitor.clone(),
        agent:             agent.clone(),
        orchestrator:      orch,
        memory:            memory.clone(),
        api_key:           api_key.clone(),
        current_resonance: Arc::new(std::sync::atomic::AtomicU32::new(50)),
        tx:                tx.clone(),
        scheduler_tx,
        arbiter:           Arc::new(ArbiterAgent::new(agent.clone())),
        workspace_root,
        vmo_state,
        cluster:           cluster.clone(),
        dist:              dist.clone(),
        governance_log,
    });

    // ── Background subsystems ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    let hb_mesh = cluster.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        nexus_singularity::heartbeat::start(
            hb_mesh,
            env::var("NEXUS_NODE_ID").unwrap_or_else(|_| "DUET_CORE".into()),
            3000,
        ).await;
    });

    let s_state = state.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move { start_server(s_state).await; });

    let h_state  = state.clone();
    let h_memory = memory.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        SelfHealingEngine::new(h_state, h_memory).ignite_autonomic_loop().await;
    });

    let d_state = state.clone();
    let d_memory = memory.clone();
    let d_agent = agent.clone();
    let d_api_key = api_key.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        nexus_singularity::dream_cycle::start_daydream(
            d_memory,
            d_agent,
            d_api_key,
        ).await;
    });

    let b_state = state.clone();
    let b_monitor = monitor.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        nexus_singularity::broker::KnowledgeBroker::refresh_atlas().await;
        nexus_singularity::telemetry_bridge::start_thermal_bridge(b_monitor).await;
    });

    if let Err(e) = run_cli_loop(state).await {
        eprintln!("!! [FATAL] CLI crashed: {}", e);
    }
}

[FILE: ./scripts/prepare-duet.sh]
========================================
#!/bin/bash
echo "=== Preparing Lenovo Duet for Rust Compilation (4GB RAM) ==="

# Create swap if not exists
if [ ! -f /swapfile ]; then
    echo "Creating 6GB swapfile..."
    sudo fallocate -l 6G /swapfile
    sudo chmod 600 /swapfile
    sudo mkswap /swapfile
    sudo swapon /swapfile
    echo "/swapfile none swap sw 0 0" | sudo tee -a /etc/fstab
    echo "Swap created and enabled."
else
    echo "Swapfile already exists."
fi

free -h

echo -e "\nRecommended before building:"
echo "   • Close all Chrome tabs"
echo "   • Run: watch -n 2 free -h   (in another terminal)"
echo "   • Use: ./build.sh"

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# run_debug.sh — Runs NEXUS with full raw response logging
# Usage: ./run_debug.sh
# Then type: task "QueryMemory for 'SQLite WAL mode'"
# You will see the raw model output each turn so you can diagnose JSON failures.

export NEXUS_DEBUG=1
export GEMINI_API_KEY="${GEMINI_API_KEY:-$(grep GEMINI_API_KEY .env | cut -d= -f2)}"

echo ">> [DEBUG MODE] Raw model responses will be shown each turn."
echo ">> Building..."
cargo build --target aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu -j 2 2>&1

if [[ $? -eq 0 ]]; then
    echo ">> Running NEXUS with debug output..."
    NEXUS_DEBUG=1 ./target/aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu/debug/nexus_singularity
else
    echo "!! Build failed — check errors above"

#!/bin/bash
# run_scout_hardened.sh - Target-Agnostic Zig-Reflex Ignition

echo ">> [UPLINK] Initiating Alpha-Scout (Zig-Reflex Test) on Duet 2..."

# 1. Throttled Build
echo ">> [BUILD] Compiling alpha_scout..."
cargo build -j 2 --bin alpha_scout

# 2. Dynamic Binary Discovery
# We search the entire target tree for the executable to handle ARM64 pathing
BINARY_PATH=$(find target -name "alpha_scout" -type f -executable | head -n 1)

if [ -z "$BINARY_PATH" ]; then
    echo "!! [FATAL] alpha_scout binary not found. Check compilation output."
    exit 1
fi

echo ">> [UPLINK] Binary located at: $BINARY_PATH"

# 3. Muscle Check: Using existing nexus_synapse container
if ! podman ps | grep -q "nexus_synapse"; then
    echo "!! [FRACTURE] nexus_synapse (Redis) not detected. Ignite Podman first."
    exit 1
fi

# 4. Zero-Swap Monitoring (Background)
(while true; do 
    AVAIL=$(free -m | grep Mem | awk '{print $7}')
    if [ "$AVAIL" -lt 150 ]; then
        echo -e "\n!! [RAM_WARN] Critical Pressure: ${AVAIL}MB available !!"
    fi
    sleep 1
done) &
MONITOR_PID=$!

# 5. Execution (The Zig-Reflex Mission)
echo ">> [SCOUT] Executing mission..."
$BINARY_PATH

# 6. Extraction & Cleanup
kill $MONITOR_PID 2>/dev/null
echo -e "\n>> [SCOUT] Mission cycle complete. Stasis reached."

[FILE: ./scripts/scout_models.sh]
========================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# =============================================================================
# NEXUS SINGULARITY — SCOUT MODELS (FULL PAGINATION)
# =============================================================================
set -euo pipefail

CYAN='\033[0;36m'
GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
RESET='\033[0m'

echo -e "${CYAN}>> [SCOUT] Initiating API Model Uplink...${RESET}"

if [ -f .env ]; then
    export $(grep -v '^#' .env | xargs)
fi

if [ -z "${GEMINI_API_KEY:-}" ]; then
    echo -e "\x1b[31m!! [FAULT] GEMINI_API_KEY is not set.\x1b[0m"
    exit 1
fi

PAGE_TOKEN=""
URL="https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key=${GEMINI_API_KEY}"

echo -e "${YELLOW}>> Fetching active model registry from Google API...${RESET}\n"
echo -e "${GREEN}=== VERIFIED API MODELS ===${RESET}"

while :; do
    if [ -n "$PAGE_TOKEN" ]; then
        REQ_URL="${URL}&pageToken=${PAGE_TOKEN}"
    else
        REQ_URL="${URL}"
    fi

    RESPONSE=$(curl -s "$REQ_URL")
    
    if echo "$RESPONSE" | jq -e '.error' > /dev/null; then
        ERR_MSG=$(echo "$RESPONSE" | jq -r '.error.message')
        echo -e "\x1b[31m!! [API REJECTION] ${ERR_MSG}\x1b[0m"
        exit 1
    fi

    echo "$RESPONSE" | jq -r '
        .models[]? | 
        "\u001b[36m👉 \(.name)\u001b[0m (\(.displayName // "N/A"))\n   \u001b[33mTokens: IN: \(.inputTokenLimit // "N/A") | OUT: \(.outputTokenLimit // "N/A")\u001b[0m\n   Desc: \(.description // "No description provided." | gsub("\n"; " "))\n"
    '
    
    # Extract the next page token. If it doesn't exist, jq returns empty.
    PAGE_TOKEN=$(echo "$RESPONSE" | jq -r '.nextPageToken // empty')
    
    if [ -z "$PAGE_TOKEN" ]; then
        break
    fi
done

echo -e "${CYAN}>> [SCOUT] Network scan complete.${RESET}"

[FILE: ./scripts/sentry_safe.sh]
========================================
#!/bin/bash
# CWD_CHECK
[[ "$PWD" != *"/nexus_singularity-dev"* ]] && echo "Error: Run from project root" && exit 1
# NEXUS SENTRY // ALPINE MUSL EDITION

echo ">> [SENTRY] Watchdog Online."

# Determine Binary Path (Musl vs Glibc)
if [ -f "./target/aarch64-unknown-linux-musl/release/nexus_singularity" ]; then
    BIN="./target/aarch64-unknown-linux-musl/release/nexus_singularity"
else
    BIN="./target/release/nexus_singularity"
fi

CRASH_COUNT=0
MAX_CRASHES=3

while true; do
    echo ">> [SENTRY] Igniting Core: $BIN"
    
    if [ ! -f "$BIN" ]; then
        echo ">> [SENTRY] Binary missing. Run ./nexus.sh first."
        exit 1
    fi

    START_TIME=$(date +%s)
    $BIN
    EXIT_CODE=$?
    END_TIME=$(date +%s)
    
    RUNTIME=$((END_TIME - START_TIME))
    echo ">> [SENTRY] Core offline (Run time: ${RUNTIME}s)"

    if [ $RUNTIME -lt 10 ]; then
        ((CRASH_COUNT++))
        if [ $CRASH_COUNT -ge $MAX_CRASHES ]; then
            echo "!! [CRITICAL] SYSTEM UNSTABLE."
            exit 1
        fi
        sleep 5
    else
        CRASH_COUNT=0
        sleep 2
    fi
done

#!/bin/bash
# sovereign_audit.sh - v1.0 [Modular Kernel Baseline]
# Focus: Identify active code bloat while pruning build artifacts and vault history.

THRESHOLD=100
REPORT_DIR="src/reports"
REPORT_FILE="$REPORT_DIR/bloat_audit.txt"

# Ensure report sanctuary exists
mkdir -p "$REPORT_DIR"

echo ">> [AUDITOR] Commencing workspace scan (Threshold: $THRESHOLD lines)..."
echo ">> [AUDITOR] Pruning target/, vault history, and hidden directories..."

# The Core Diagnostic Pipeline:
# 1. Prunes specific high-entropy directories.
# 2. Scans for Rust source strands.
# 3. Filters by line count and formats the output.
find . -type d \( -name "target" -o -name ".git" -o -name ".lazarus_vault" -o -name ".nexus_vault" \) -prune \
    -o -name "*.rs" -type f -exec wc -l {} + 2>/dev/null \
    | awk -v limit="$THRESHOLD" '$1 > limit && $2 != "total" {print $2 ": " $1 " lines"}' \
    | sort -rn -k2 > "$REPORT_FILE"

if [ -s "$REPORT_FILE" ]; then
    echo ">> [AUDITOR] Report forged: $REPORT_FILE"
    echo "--- [Top Bloat Candidates] ---"
    head -n 5 "$REPORT_FILE"
else
    echo ">> [AUDITOR] Nominal. No active strands exceed $THRESHOLD lines."
    rm -f "$REPORT_FILE"
fi

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# CWD_CHECK
[[ "$PWD" != *"/nexus_singularity-dev"* ]] && echo "Error: Run from project root" && exit 1
set -e
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."

LLAMA_SERVER="vendor/llama_build/bin/llama-server"
SMOLLM="vault/models/smollm2-256m.gguf"
GEMMA_1B="$HOME/nexus_hybrid/vault/gemma1b.gguf"

pkill -f "llama-server" 2>/dev/null || true
sleep 1

MODE="${1:-micro}"

case "$MODE" in
    micro) MODEL="$SMOLLM"; CTX=2048; echo ">> [LLM] MICRO mode — SmolLM2 256M (~180MB RAM)" ;;
    fast)  MODEL="$GEMMA_1B"; CTX=4096; echo ">> [LLM] FAST mode — Gemma 1B (~800MB RAM)" ;;
    *)     echo "Usage: $0 [micro|fast]"; exit 1 ;;
esac

if [ ! -f "$MODEL" ]; then
    echo ">> [LLM] Model not found: $MODEL — Gemini fallback active."
    exit 0
fi

"$LLAMA_SERVER" \
    --model "$MODEL" \
    --host 127.0.0.1 \
    --port 8080 \
    --ctx-size "$CTX" \
    --n-predict 1024 \
    --threads 2 \
    --batch-size 64 \
    --mlock \
    --no-mmap \
    --log-disable &

echo ">> [LLM] PID: $! — Waiting for ready..."
for i in $(seq 1 40); do
    curl -sf http://127.0.0.1:8080/health >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo ">> [LLM] Ready." && exit 0
    sleep 1
done
echo ">> [LLM] Timeout — Gemini fallback active."

#!/bin/bash
# CWD_CHECK
[[ "$PWD" != *"/nexus_singularity-dev"* ]] && echo "Error: Run from project root" && exit 1

# Define Colors
GREEN='\033[0;32m'
RED='\033[0;31m'
BLUE='\033[0;34m'
NC='\033[0m' # No Color

echo -e "${BLUE}========================================${NC}"
echo -e "${BLUE}   NEXUS SINGULARITY // STATUS REPORT   ${NC}"
echo -e "${BLUE}========================================${NC}"

# 1. PROCESS CHECK
if pgrep -f "nexus-terminal" > /dev/null || pgrep -f "nexus_singularity" > /dev/null; then
    echo -e "SYSTEM CORE:  [${GREEN}ONLINE${NC}]"
    
    # 2. API HEALTH CHECK
    # We use a short timeout (2s) so the script doesn't hang if the server is frozen
    RESPONSE=$(curl -s --max-time 2 http://localhost:3000/api/vitals)
    
    if [ -z "$RESPONSE" ]; then
        echo -e "API GATEWAY:  [${RED}UNREACHABLE${NC}]"
    else
        # Extract data safely (works with or without jq)
        if command -v jq &> /dev/null; then
            CPU=$(echo $RESPONSE | jq -r .cpu_usage)
            RAM=$(echo $RESPONSE | jq -r .ram_percent)
            STATUS=$(echo $RESPONSE | jq -r .status)
        else
            # Fallback text parsing if jq is missing
            CPU=$(echo $RESPONSE | grep -o '"cpu_usage":"[^"]*"' | cut -d'"' -f4)
            RAM=$(echo $RESPONSE | grep -o '"ram_percent":"[^"]*"' | cut -d'"' -f4)
            STATUS="CONNECTED"
        fi
        
        echo -e "API GATEWAY:  [${GREEN}CONNECTED${NC}]"
        echo -e " > STATUS:    $STATUS"
        echo -e " > CPU LOAD:  ${CPU}%"
        echo -e " > RAM LOAD:  ${RAM}%"
    fi
else
    echo -e "SYSTEM CORE:  [${RED}OFFLINE${NC}]"
    echo " > Hint: Run ./universal_ignite.sh to start."
fi

echo -e "\n${BLUE}--- EVOLUTION LOG ---${NC}"
if command -v git &> /dev/null; then
    git log -n 3 --pretty=format:"%h - %s (%cr)"
else
    echo "Git not initialized."
fi

echo -e "\n\n${BLUE}--- FORGE STATS ---${NC}"
if [ -f ".forge_stats.json" ]; then
    if command -v jq &> /dev/null; then
        cat .forge_stats.json | jq .
    else
        cat .forge_stats.json
    fi
else
    echo "No forge stats detected."
fi
echo -e "${BLUE}========================================${NC}"

[FILE: ./scripts/suture_substrate.sh]

#!/bin/bash
# Nexus Singularity — Auth Substrate Suture v1.9.1
# Targets: Header Protocol Mismatch & Bootstrap Race Conditions

echo ">> [SUTURE] Realignment of Auth Arteries initiated..."

# 1. Update Uplink Service (Add Bearer prefix & 401 Interceptor)
cat <<'GHOST' > ui/src/lib/services/uplink.js
// uplink.js — v2.3 [BEARER_SUTURE]
import { auth } from '$lib/stores/auth.svelte';

function buildHeaders(extra = {}) {
    const headers = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...extra };
    if (auth.token) {
        // SUTURE: Added 'Bearer ' prefix to align with standard Axum/JWT extractors
        // Ensure backend middleware strips this or verify_token handles it
        headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${auth.token}`;
    }
    return headers;
}

async function request(url, options = {}) {
    const { headers: extraHeaders, ...rest } = options;
    const headers = buildHeaders(extraHeaders ?? {});
    
    try {
        const res = await fetch(url, { ...rest, headers });
        
        if (res.status === 401) {
            console.warn(`!! [UPLINK] 401 Unauthorized at ${url}. Token invalid or missing.`);
        }
        
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status} — ${url}`);
        return res;
    } catch (e) {
        throw e;
    }
}

export const uplink = {
    fetch:    (url, options = {})     => request(url, options),
    post:     (url, body, signal)     => request(url, { method: 'POST',  body: JSON.stringify(body), signal }),
    put:      (url, body, signal)     => request(url, { method: 'PUT',   body: JSON.stringify(body), signal }),
    delete:   (url, signal)           => request(url, { method: 'DELETE', signal }),
    get:      (url, signal)           => request(url, { signal }),
    json:     async (url, opts)       => (await request(url, opts)).json(),
};
GHOST

# 2. Update Auth Store (Enhanced Handshake Logs)
cat <<'GHOST' > ui/src/lib/stores/auth.svelte.ts
// auth.svelte.ts — v1.2 [HANDSHAKE_LOGGING]
export const auth = $state({
    token:           null as string | null,
    isAuthenticated: false,
});

const SESSION_KEY = 'nexus_session_token';

export function restoreSession() {
    const stored = sessionStorage.getItem(SESSION_KEY);
    if (stored) {
        auth.token = stored;
        auth.isAuthenticated = true;
        console.log(">> [AUTH] Session restored from storage.");
    }
}

export async function login(key: string): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/auth/login', {
            method:  'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body:    JSON.stringify({ key }),
        });
        if (!res.ok) {
            console.error(`!! [AUTH] Login rejected: HTTP ${res.status}`);
            return false;
        }

        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.token) {
            auth.token = data.token;
            auth.isAuthenticated = true;
            sessionStorage.setItem(SESSION_KEY, data.token);
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    } catch (e) {
        console.error("!! [AUTH] Login connection failure:", e);
        return false;
    }
}

export async function bootAuth(): Promise<void> {
    if (auth.isAuthenticated) return;
    const stored = sessionStorage.getItem(SESSION_KEY);
    if (stored) {
        auth.token = stored;
        auth.isAuthenticated = true;
        return;
    }
    
    console.log(">> [AUTH] Attempting silent handshake...");
    const ok = await login('SOVEREIGN');
    if (ok) {
        console.log(">> [AUTH] Substrate Handshake Complete.");
    } else {
        console.warn(">> [AUTH] Silent handshake failed. Manual verify required.");
    }
}
GHOST

# 3. Ensure Environment Parity
if [ ! -f .env ]; then
    echo "NEXUS_AUTH_KEY=SOVEREIGN" > .env
    echo ">> [SUTURE] .env created with default SOVEREIGN key."
fi

chmod +x suture_substrate.sh
echo ">> [SUTURE] Realignment complete. Run 'source .env' before starting the backend."
