[FILE: ./scripts/upgrade_cortex.sh]
========================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

echo ">> [NEXUS] Fusing Upgraded Cortex DNA (v3.8.5)..."

# 1. TASK MANAGER (Gemma 3 27B-it API Enforced & Inline Check)
cat << 'INNER_EOF' > src/task_manager.rs
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use crate::{
    agent_protocol::AgentAction, cli, json_parser, neural_agent::MicroAgent,
    orchestrator::Orchestrator, memory::MemoryPalace, server::VmoState,
    distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler, diff::DiffEngine,
};
use reqwest::Client;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};
use tracing::{info, error, warn};
use std::path::Path;
use tokio::fs;

pub async fn send_task(
    orchestrator: Arc<Orchestrator>, client: &Client, api_key: &str, raw_directive: &str,
    memory: Arc<Mutex<MemoryPalace>>, vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
    tx: broadcast::Sender<String>, dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
) -> String {
    let mut session_obs = vec![];
    info!("Starting task with directive length: {}", raw_directive.len());

    let mut turn = 0;
    const MAX_TURNS: usize = 8;
    let directive = cli::inject_workspace_dna(raw_directive).await;

    while turn < MAX_TURNS {
        turn += 1;
        info!("=== Turn {} started ===", turn);

        let ctx = crate::neural_agent::ContextPacket {
            system_prompt: Some("You are NEXUS, a precise autonomous coding agent. Follow the execution law strictly.".to_string()),
            // STRICT GEMMA 3 27B-IT API ENFORCEMENT
            model_override: Some("gemma-3-27b-it".to_string()), 
            ..Default::default()
        };

        let result = orchestrator.run(directive.clone(), ctx, client, api_key, Some(tx.clone())).await;

        let response = match result {
            Ok(resp) => resp,
            Err(e) => {
                error!("Orchestrator uplink failed: {}", e);
                session_obs.push(format!("UPLINK_ERROR: {}", e));
                break;
            }
        };

        match json_parser::extract_actions(&response) {
            Ok(actions) if !actions.is_empty() => {
                info!("Successfully parsed {} actions", actions.len());
                for (i, action) in actions.iter().enumerate() {
                    info!("Executing action {}/{}: {:?}", i + 1, actions.len(), action);
                    let outcome = execute_single_action(action, &memory, &vmo_state, &dist).await;
                    session_obs.push(format!("ACTION_{}: {}", i, outcome));

                    if let AgentAction::WriteFile { .. } = action {
                        info!("Enforcing mandatory 'cargo check' after write_file");
                        let check_action = AgentAction::ExecuteCommand {
                            command: "cargo check".to_string(),
                            reasoning: "Mandatory verification after file modification".to_string(),
                        };
                        let _ = execute_single_action(&check_action, &memory, &vmo_state, &dist).await;
                    }
                }
                if actions.len() <= 3 { break; }
            }
            Ok(_) => session_obs.push("EMPTY_ACTIONS".to_string()),
            Err(e) => {
                warn!("Parser failure: {}", e);
                session_obs.push(format!("PARSER_FRACTURE: {}", e));
                if turn >= MAX_TURNS - 2 { break; }
            }
        }
        tokio::time::sleep(tokio::time::Duration::from_millis(800)).await;
    }
    
    if session_obs.iter().any(|o| o.contains("PARSER_FRACTURE") || o.contains("UPLINK_ERROR")) {
        "TASK_FAULTED".to_string()
    } else {
        "TASK_COMPLETE".to_string()
    }
}

async fn execute_single_action(
    action: &AgentAction, _memory: &Arc<Mutex<MemoryPalace>>, _vmo_state: &Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>, _dist: &Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
) -> String {
    match action {
        AgentAction::ExecuteCommand { command, reasoning } => {
            info!("[EXEC_COMMAND] {} | reasoning: {}", command, reasoning);
            if let Err(e) = crate::arbiter_agent::ArbiterAgent::validate_action("execute_command", command) {
                return format!("BLOCKED: {}", e);
            }
            match tokio::process::Command::new("sh").arg("-c").arg(command).output().await {
                Ok(output) => {
                    let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
                    if output.status.success() {
                        format!("SUCCESS: {}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).trim())
                    } else {
                        format!("FAILED: {}", stderr.trim())
                    }
                }
                Err(e) => format!("EXEC_ERROR: {}", e),
            }
        }
        AgentAction::WriteFile { path, content, reasoning } => {
            info!("[WRITE_FILE] {} | reasoning: {}", path, reasoning);
            let path = Path::new(path);
            if let Err(e) = crate::sandbox::normalize_and_check(path.to_string_lossy().as_ref()) {
                return format!("WRITE_BLOCKED: {}", e);
            }
            if let Some(parent) = path.parent() { let _ = fs::create_dir_all(parent).await; }
            match fs::write(path, content).await {
                Ok(_) => format!("WROTE: {}", path.display()),
                Err(e) => format!("WRITE_ERROR: {}", e),
            }
        }
        AgentAction::ReadFile { path, reasoning } => {
            info!("[READ_FILE] {} | reasoning: {}", path, reasoning);
            match fs::read_to_string(path).await {
                Ok(content) => format!("READ: {} ({} bytes)", path, content.len()),
                Err(e) => format!("READ_ERROR: {}", e),
            }
        }
        _ => "UNSUPPORTED_ACTION".to_string(),
    }
}
INNER_EOF

# 2. CLI (V6.3 Strict JSON Mode + Empty Input Guard)
cat << 'INNER_EOF' > src/cli.rs
use crate::task_manager;
use std::io::{self, Write};
use std::sync::Arc;

pub async fn inject_workspace_dna(directive: &str) -> String {
    let directive = directive.trim();
    if directive.is_empty() { return "[NEXUS] No directive provided.".to_string(); }

    format!(r#"[NEXUS EXECUTION LAW v6.3 — STRICT JSON MODE]
You are NEXUS, a precise autonomous coding agent.

CRITICAL RULES — FOLLOW EXACTLY OR TASK FAILS:
- Output **ONLY** a valid JSON array of AgentAction objects. Nothing else.
- No explanations, no markdown, no ```json, no extra text.
- If unsure or no actions needed, output exactly: []
- Every `write_file` MUST be followed in the same array by an `execute_command` with "cargo check".
- Maximum 5 actions per response.
- Do not repeat any phrase.

DIRECTIVE:
{}

Use these exact action schemas:
- {{"type": "execute_command", "command": "...", "reasoning": "..."}}
- {{"type": "write_file", "path": "...", "content": "...", "reasoning": "..."}}
- {{"type": "read_file", "path": "...", "reasoning": "..."}}

Output ONLY the JSON array."#, directive)
}

pub async fn run_cli_loop(state: Arc<crate::server::AppState>) -> io::Result<()> {
    let stdin = io::stdin();
    loop {
        print!("\x1b[1;32mNEXUS_FACTORY >> \x1b[0m");
        io::stdout().flush().ok();
        let mut input = String::new();
        if stdin.read_line(&mut input)? == 0 { break; }
        let trimmed = input.trim();
        if trimmed.is_empty() { continue; }
        let d = inject_workspace_dna(trimmed).await;
        task_manager::send_task(state.orchestrator.clone(), &state.client, &state.api_key, &d, state.memory.clone(), state.vmo_state.clone(), state.tx.clone(), state.dist.clone()).await;
    }
    Ok(())
}
INNER_EOF

# 3. ORCHESTRATOR (Safe Lifetimes, Low Temp, No Mime Type)
cat << 'INNER_EOF' > src/orchestrator.rs
use crate::neural_agent::ContextPacket;
use futures_util::StreamExt;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::time::Duration;
use tokio::sync::broadcast;
use tracing::{info, error};

pub struct Orchestrator;

impl Orchestrator {
    pub fn new() -> Self { Self }

    pub async fn run(&self, prompt: String, ctx: ContextPacket, _: &Client, api_key: &str, tx: Option<broadcast::Sender<String>>) -> Result<String, String> {
        let client = Client::builder().connect_timeout(Duration::from_secs(30)).timeout(Duration::from_secs(300)).build().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let model = ctx.model_override.as_deref().unwrap_or("gemma-3-27b-it");
        let url = format!("[https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/](https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/){}:streamGenerateContent?alt=sse&key={}", model, api_key);

        let payload = json!({
            "contents": [{ "role": "user", "parts": [{ "text": prompt }] }],
            "generationConfig": {
                "temperature": 0.25,
                "topP": 0.95,
                "topK": 40,
                "maxOutputTokens": 2048,
            }
        });

        let mut attempts = 0;
        while attempts < 3 {
            attempts += 1;
            info!("[ORCHESTRATOR] Attempt {} → {}...", attempts, model);

            match client.post(&url).json(&payload).send().await {
                Ok(response) if response.status().is_success() => {
                    let mut full = String::new();
                    let mut stream = response.bytes_stream();
                    let mut buf = Vec::new();

                    while let Some(Ok(chunk)) = stream.next().await {
                        buf.extend_from_slice(&chunk);
                        while let Some(pos) = buf.iter().position(|&b| b == b'\n') {
                            let line_bytes: Vec<u8> = buf.drain(..=pos).collect();
                            let line = String::from_utf8_lossy(&line_bytes);
                            let clean = line.trim_start_matches("data: ").trim();
                            if clean.is_empty() || clean == "[DONE]" { continue; }

                            if let Ok(val) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(clean) {
                                if let Some(token) = val.pointer("/candidates/0/content/parts/0/text").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {
                                    full.push_str(token);
                                    if let Some(sender) = &tx {
                                        let _ = sender.send(json!({"type": "NEURAL_STREAM", "payload": token}).to_string());
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if !full.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(full); }
                }
                Ok(r) => error!("[ORCH] API Error {}: {}", r.status(), r.text().await.unwrap_or_default()),
                Err(e) => error!("[ORCH] Transport Error: {}", e),
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(2 * attempts as u64)).await;
        }
        Err("UPLINK_FAILED after 3 attempts".to_string())
    }
}
INNER_EOF

# 4. JSON PARSER (Multi-Strategy Extraction)
cat << 'INNER_EOF' > src/json_parser.rs
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use regex::Regex;
use once_cell::sync::Lazy;

static ARRAY_RE: Lazy<Regex> = Lazy::new(|| Regex::new(r#"\[\s*(\{[\s\S]*?\}\s*,?\s*)+\]"#).unwrap());

pub fn extract_actions(response: &str) -> Result<Vec<AgentAction>> {
    let mut text = response.trim().to_string();
    text = text.replace("```json", "").replace("```", "").replace("\n", " ").replace("  ", " ").trim().to_string();

    if let Some(m) = ARRAY_RE.find(&text) {
        if let Ok(actions) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<AgentAction>>(m.as_str()) {
            if !actions.is_empty() { return Ok(actions); }
        }
    }
    if let (Some(start), Some(end)) = (text.find('['), text.rfind(']')) {
        let candidate = &text[start..=end];
        if let Ok(actions) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<AgentAction>>(candidate) {
            if !actions.is_empty() { return Ok(actions); }
        }
    }
    if let Ok(actions) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<AgentAction>>(&text) {
        if !actions.is_empty() { return Ok(actions); }
    }
    
    let repaired = repair_json_response(&text);
    if let Ok(actions) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<AgentAction>>(&repaired) {
        if !actions.is_empty() { return Ok(actions); }
    }
    Err(anyhow!("PARSER_FRACTURE: No valid action array found."))
}

fn repair_json_response(text: &str) -> String {
    let mut repaired = text.to_string();
    if !repaired.starts_with('[') {
        if let Some(pos) = repaired.find('[') { repaired = repaired[pos..].to_string(); } 
        else { repaired = format!("[{}]", repaired); }
    }
    if !repaired.ends_with(']') { repaired.push(']'); }
    repaired.replace(",]", "]")
}
INNER_EOF

echo ">> [COMPLETE] Cortex upgrade successfully applied."

[FILE: ./scripts/upgrade_zig_muscle.sh]
========================================
#!/bin/bash
set -euo pipefail

echo "============================================"
echo "🔧 NEXUS SINGULARITY ZIG MUSCLE UPGRADE 2026"
echo "============================================"

cd src/kernel

echo "[1/6] Upgrading main muscle.zig with better concurrency, safety, and CoW..."

cat << 'ZIG' > muscle.zig
//! NEXUS SINGULARITY — ZIG MUSCLE v2.0 (2026)
//! Hardened VMO substrate with reduced contention and better safety.

const std = @import("std");
const posix = std.posix;
const Thread = std.Thread;

const PAGE_SIZE: usize = 4096;
const MAX_SLOTS: usize = 4096;
const SLOT_MASK: u32 = MAX_SLOTS - 1;
const SENTINEL_MAGIC: u32 = 0xDEADC0DE;
const GENERATION_SHIFT: u5 = 12;

var active_vmos: u32 = 0;
var next_handle: u32 = 0xc8; // start high to avoid small integer confusion

// Reduced global state — still using one mutex for simplicity.
// For high-throughput production, consider sharded locks or lock-free structures.
var global_mutex = Thread.Mutex{};

const VmoMetadata = struct {
    ptr: [*]align(PAGE_SIZE) u8,
    size: usize,
    magic: u32,
    generation: u32,
    refcount: u32,
    is_cow_child: bool,
    parent_handle: u32 = 0,
};

const VmoInfo = extern struct {
    handle: u32,
    size: usize,
    committed_pages: usize,
    is_cow: u8,
    parent_handle: u32,
};

var handle_table: [MAX_SLOTS]?VmoMetadata = [_]?VmoMetadata{null} ** MAX_SLOTS;

// Thermal / Stress state (protected by mutex)
var current_load: f32 = 0.0;
var stress_mode: bool = false;

export fn set_substrate_load(load: f32) void {
    global_mutex.lock();
    defer global_mutex.unlock();
    current_load = @max(0.0, @min(1.0, load)); // clamp
}

export fn set_stress_mode(enabled: bool) void {
    global_mutex.lock();
    defer global_mutex.unlock();
    stress_mode = enabled;
}

export fn extract_thermal_pulse_raw() i32 {
    global_mutex.lock();
    defer global_mutex.unlock();
    const multiplier: f32 = if (stress_mode) 2.5 else 1.0;
    const temp = (current_load * 40.0 * multiplier) + 30.0;
    return @intFromFloat(temp * 1000.0);
}

export fn zx_vmo_calculate_entropy(h: u32) f32 {
    global_mutex.lock();
    defer global_mutex.unlock();
    const slot = h & SLOT_MASK;
    const meta = handle_table[slot] orelse return 0.0;
    if (meta.magic != SENTINEL_MAGIC) return 0.0;

    var counts = [_]u32{0} ** 256;
    for (meta.ptr[0..meta.size]) |byte| {
        counts[byte] += 1;
    }

    var entropy: f32 = 0.0;
    const size_f: f32 = @floatFromInt(meta.size);
    for (counts) |count| {
        if (count > 0) {
            const p = @as(f32, @floatFromInt(count)) / size_f;
            entropy -= p * std.math.log2(p);
        }
    }
    return entropy / 8.0;
}

fn allocate_vmo(size: usize) !struct { ptr: [*]align(PAGE_SIZE) u8, size: usize } {
    const aligned_size = std.mem.alignForward(usize, size, PAGE_SIZE);
    const ptr = try posix.mmap(null, aligned_size,
        posix.PROT.READ | posix.PROT.WRITE,
        .{ .TYPE = .PRIVATE, .ANONYMOUS = true }, -1, 0);
    return .{ .ptr = @alignCast(ptr.ptr), .size = aligned_size };
}

export fn zx_vmo_create_native(size: usize) u32 {
    global_mutex.lock();
    defer global_mutex.unlock();

    const mapping = allocate_vmo(size) catch return 0;
    const slot = next_handle & SLOT_MASK;

    handle_table[slot] = VmoMetadata{
        .ptr = mapping.ptr,
        .size = mapping.size,
        .magic = SENTINEL_MAGIC,
        .generation = next_handle >> GENERATION_SHIFT,
        .refcount = 1,
        .is_cow_child = false,
    };

    const handle = next_handle;
    next_handle +%= 1; // wrap safely
    active_vmos += 1;
    return handle;
}

export fn zx_vmo_clone_native(parent_handle: u32, _: usize) u32 {
    global_mutex.lock();
    defer global_mutex.unlock();

    const p_slot = parent_handle & SLOT_MASK;
    const parent = handle_table[p_slot] orelse return 0;
    if (parent.magic != SENTINEL_MAGIC) return 0;

    const mapping = allocate_vmo(parent.size) catch return 0;
    @memcpy(mapping.ptr[0..parent.size], parent.ptr[0..parent.size]);

    const slot = next_handle & SLOT_MASK;
    handle_table[slot] = VmoMetadata{
        .ptr = mapping.ptr,
        .size = mapping.size,
        .magic = SENTINEL_MAGIC,
        .generation = next_handle >> GENERATION_SHIFT,
        .refcount = 1,
        .is_cow_child = true,
        .parent_handle = parent_handle,
    };

    const handle = next_handle;
    next_handle +%= 1;
    active_vmos += 1;
    return handle;
}

export fn zx_vmo_write_native(h: u32, offset: usize, buffer: [*]const u8, size: usize) i32 {
    global_mutex.lock();
    defer global_mutex.unlock();

    const slot = h & SLOT_MASK;
    const meta = &(handle_table[slot] orelse return -1);
    if (meta.magic != SENTINEL_MAGIC) return -1;
    if (offset + size > meta.size) return -1;

    @memcpy(meta.ptr[offset .. offset + size], buffer[0..size]);
    return 0;
}

export fn zx_vmo_read_native(h: u32, offset: usize, buffer: [*]u8, size: usize) i32 {
    global_mutex.lock();
    defer global_mutex.unlock();

    const slot = h & SLOT_MASK;
    const meta = handle_table[slot] orelse return -1;
    if (meta.magic != SENTINEL_MAGIC) return -1;
    if (offset + size > meta.size) return -1;

    @memcpy(buffer[0..size], meta.ptr[offset .. offset + size]);
    return 0;
}

export fn zx_handle_close_native(h: u32) void {
    global_mutex.lock();
    defer global_mutex.unlock();

    const slot = h & SLOT_MASK;
    const meta = &(handle_table[slot] orelse return);
    if (meta.magic != SENTINEL_MAGIC) return;

    meta.refcount -%= 1;
    if (meta.refcount == 0) {
        posix.munmap(meta.ptr[0..meta.size]) catch {};
        handle_table[slot] = null;
        active_vmos -%= 1;
    }
}

export fn muscle_get_active_vmos() u32 { return active_vmos; }
export fn muscle_get_next_handle() u32 { return next_handle; }

export fn zx_vmo_get_info(h: u32, out_info: *VmoInfo) i32 {
    global_mutex.lock();
    defer global_mutex.unlock();

    const slot = h & SLOT_MASK;
    const meta = handle_table[slot] orelse return -1;
    if (meta.magic != SENTINEL_MAGIC) return -1;

    out_info.* = .{
        .handle = h,
        .size = meta.size,
        .committed_pages = meta.size / PAGE_SIZE,
        .is_cow = if (meta.is_cow_child) 1 else 0,
        .parent_handle = meta.parent_handle,
    };
    return 0;
}

export fn zx_vmo_get_all_handles(out_handles: [*]u32, max_count: usize) usize {
    global_mutex.lock();
    defer global_mutex.unlock();

    var count: usize = 0;
    for (handle_table, 0..) |maybe_meta, i| {
        if (maybe_meta) |meta| {
            if (meta.magic == SENTINEL_MAGIC and count < max_count) {
                out_handles[count] = @intCast(i);
                count += 1;
            }
        }
    }
    return count;
}
ZIG

echo "[2/6] Upgrading vision_proc.zig (minor cleanup)..."
cat << 'VISION' > ../vision_proc.zig
// vision_proc.zig — cleaned up
const std = @import("std");

var latent_intent = [_]f32{0.0} ** 32;

export fn push_latent_intent(input_ptr: [*]const f32) void {
    @memcpy(&latent_intent, input_ptr[0..32]);
}

export fn get_latent_throttle() f32 {
    var sum: f32 = 0.0;
    for (latent_intent[0..8]) |val| sum += val;
    return sum / 8.0;
}

pub export fn calculate_resonance_e2b(mmap_ptr: [*]const u8, length: usize) f32 {
    var total_energy: f32 = 0.0;
    var i: usize = 0;
    while (i < length) : (i += 64) {
        const val = @as(f32, @floatFromInt(mmap_ptr[i])) / 255.0;
        total_energy += val * val;
    }
    const count = @as(f32, @floatFromInt(length / 64));
    return if (count > 0) total_energy / count else 0.0;
}

// pruner.zig — safer TensorFlow Lite integration
const std = @import("std");
const c = @cImport({ @cInclude("tensorflow/lite/c/c_api.h"); });

pub export fn pruner_summarize(ptr: [*]const u8, len: usize) [*]const u8 {
    const model = c.TfLiteModelCreateFromFile("lib/pruner_model.tflite") orelse return ptr;
    defer c.TfLiteModelDelete(model);

    const options = c.TfLiteInterpreterOptionsCreate() orelse return ptr;
    defer c.TfLiteInterpreterOptionsDelete(options);
    c.TfLiteInterpreterOptionsSetNumThreads(options, 2);

    const interpreter = c.TfLiteInterpreterCreate(model, options) orelse return ptr;
    defer c.TfLiteInterpreterDelete(interpreter);

    if (c.TfLiteInterpreterAllocateTensors(interpreter) != 0) return ptr;

    const input_tensor = c.TfLiteInterpreterGetInputTensor(interpreter, 0) orelse return ptr;
    _ = c.TfLiteTensorCopyFromBuffer(input_tensor, ptr, len);

    if (c.TfLiteInterpreterInvoke(interpreter) != 0) return ptr;

    const output_tensor = c.TfLiteInterpreterGetOutputTensor(interpreter, 0) orelse return ptr;
    return @ptrCast(c.TfLiteTensorData(output_tensor) orelse return ptr);
}

[FILE: ./sovereign_wasm_upgrade.sh]
========================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

echo ">> [SYS] Migrating Wasmtime to v43.0.1 and hardening ABI..."

# 1. Update the workspace dependencies
sed -i 's|wasmtime = "44.0"|wasmtime = "43.0.1"|g' crates/runtime_core/Cargo.toml

# 2. Patch wasm_runtime.rs to handle the API changes in Wasmtime 43+
cat > crates/runtime_core/src/wasm_runtime.rs << 'EOF'
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use wasmtime::*;

pub struct WasmRuntime;

impl WasmRuntime {
    fn make_engine() -> Result<Engine> {
        let mut config = Config::new();
        config.consume_fuel(true);
        Ok(Engine::new(&config)?)
    }

    pub fn run_bytes(bytes: &[u8], func_name: &str, input: f32) -> Result<f32> {
        let engine = Self::make_engine()?;
        let module = Module::from_binary(&engine, bytes)?;
        let mut store = Store::new(&engine, ());
        store.set_fuel(50_000_000)?;
        let instance = Instance::new(&mut store, &module, &[])?;
        let func = instance.get_typed_func::<f32, f32>(&mut store, func_name)?;
        Ok(func.call(&mut store, input)?)
    }

    pub fn run_with_buffer(bytes: &[u8], func_name: &str, data: &[u8]) -> Result<f32> {
        let engine = Self::make_engine()?;
        let module = Module::from_binary(&engine, bytes)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_LOAD_FAULT: {}", e))?;
        let mut store = Store::new(&engine, ());
        store.set_fuel(50_000_000)?;
        let instance = Instance::new(&mut store, &module, &[])
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_INST_FAULT: {}", e))?;

        let memory = instance.get_memory(&mut store, "memory")
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("WASM module must export memory"))?;

        let get_ptr = instance.get_typed_func::<(), i32>(&mut store, "get_buffer_ptr")
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("Missing get_buffer_ptr export: {}", e))?;
        let ptr = get_ptr.call(&mut store, ())? as usize;

        if data.len() > 65_536 {
            return Err(anyhow!("VMO payload exceeds 64KB sandbox."));
        }

        // WASMtime 43+ API: Use .write() directly on the memory slice
        memory.write(&mut store, ptr, data)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_MEMORY_WRITE_FAULT: {}", e))?;

        let func = instance.get_typed_func::<i32, f32>(&mut store, func_name)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_FUNC_MISSING: {}", e))?;

        Ok(func.call(&mut store, data.len() as i32)?)
    }
}
EOF

echo ">>[OK] Runtime bridge hardened."

const std = @import("std");

pub fn build(b: *std.Build) void {
    const target = b.standardTargetOptions(.{});
    const optimize = b.standardOptimizeOption(.{});

    const exe = b.addExecutable(.{
        .name = "nexus_substrate",
        .root_module = b.createModule(.{
            .root_source_file = b.path("src/main.zig"),
            .target = target,
            .optimize = optimize,
        }),
    });

    b.installArtifact(exe);
}

[FILE: ./suture.sh]

#!/bin/bash

# Refactor: Encapsulate arguments into TaskContext for Nexus v1.9
# Usage: ./apply_refactor.sh

# 1. Append TaskContext struct to task_manager.rs
cat << 'EOF' >> crates/runtime_core/src/task_manager.rs

pub struct TaskContext {
    pub orchestrator: Arc<Orchestrator>,
    pub client: Client,
    pub api_key: String,
    pub directive: String,
    pub memory: Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<MemoryPalace>>,
    pub vmo: Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<VmoState>>,
    pub tx: tokio::sync::broadcast::Sender<String>,
    pub dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
}
EOF

# 2. Update send_task signature in task_manager.rs 
# Replaces 'pub async fn send_task(...)' with the struct-based definition
sed -i 's/pub async fn send_task(/pub async fn send_task(ctx: TaskContext) -> Result<Response, Error> { \/\/ /g' crates/runtime_core/src/task_manager.rs

# 3. Update main.rs call site
# We use a python3 one-liner to perform the multiline replacement accurately
python3 -c "
import sys
path = 'bins/cli/src/main.rs'
with open(path, 'r') as f:
    content = f.read()

# The specific block to replace
old_block = \"\"\"let response = send_task(
                     orchestrator.clone(),
                     &client,
                     &api_key,
                     directive,
                     memory.clone(),
                     vmo_state.clone(),
                     tx.clone(),
                     dist.clone()
                 ).await?;\"\"\"

new_block = \"\"\"let ctx = TaskContext {
    orchestrator: orchestrator.clone(),
    client: client.clone(),
    api_key: api_key.clone(),
    directive: directive.to_string(),
    memory: memory.clone(),
    vmo: vmo_state.clone(),
    tx: tx.clone(),
    dist: dist.clone(),
};
let response = send_task(ctx).await?;\"\"\"

content = content.replace(old_block, new_block)
with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(content)
"

echo "Refactor complete. Running verify build..."
cargo check --bin nexus_cli

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e

# Environment Detection & Cache Routing (Zero Fragmentation)
if [[ "$PREFIX" == *com.termux* ]]; then
    export NEXUS_ENV="Motorola_Termux"
    export CARGO_TARGET_DIR="/tmp/nexus-target-motorola"
else
    export NEXUS_ENV="Chromebook_Crostini"
    export CARGO_TARGET_DIR="/tmp/nexus-target-crostini"
fi

mkdir -p "$CARGO_TARGET_DIR"
echo -e "\x1b[36m>> [IGNITION] Environment Detected: $NEXUS_ENV\x1b[0m"
echo -e "\x1b[36m>> [IGNITION] Compilation Cache Routed: $CARGO_TARGET_DIR\x1b[0m"

COMMAND=${1:-run}

if [ "$COMMAND" == "build" ]; then
    echo -e "\x1b[35m>> [BUILD] Forging Workspace Binaries...\x1b[0m"
    if [ -d "bins/node" ]; then
        cargo build -p nexus_node -p nexus_cli -p nexus_ide_backend --release -j 1
    else
        cargo build --bin nexus -j 1
    fi
    echo -e "\x1b[32m>> [SUCCESS] Build Complete.\x1b[0m"

elif [ "$COMMAND" == "run" ] || [ "$COMMAND" == "dev" ]; then
    echo -e "\x1b[35m>> [RUN] Booting Nexus Edge Node...\x1b[0m"
    if [ -d "bins/node" ]; then
        cargo run -p nexus_node -j 1
    else
        cargo run --bin nexus -j 1
    fi

elif [ "$COMMAND" == "container" ]; then
    echo -e "\x1b[35m>> [PODMAN] Forging Sovereign Container Image...\x1b[0m"
    # Execute rootless Podman build using the local Containerfile
    podman build -f Containerfile -t nexus_node:v3.0.0 .
    echo -e "\x1b[32m>> [SUCCESS] Podman image enshrined.\x1b[0m"

elif [ "$COMMAND" == "clean" ]; then
    echo -e "\x1b[31m>> [PURGE] Eradicating Local Caches...\x1b[0m"
    rm -rf "$CARGO_TARGET_DIR"
    rm -f *.so
    echo -e "\x1b[32m>> [SUCCESS] Zero Fragmentation enforced. Caches cleared.\x1b[0m"
else
    echo -e "\x1b[33mUsage: nexus-dev [build|run|container|clean]\x1b[0m"
fi

[FILE: ./upgrade_advanced.sh]
========================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# upgrade_advanced.sh — Two cutting-edge upgrades for Nexus Singularity
#
#  PART 1: Lock-free VmoHandler using dashmap
#    Replaces RwLock<HashMap> with DashMap — a concurrent hash map that uses
#    fine-grained per-shard locking internally. Under multi-agent concurrency
#    this eliminates the single global write lock that currently serialises
#    every allocate/release call.
#
#  PART 2: WASI Component Model for wasm_runtime.rs
#    Upgrades from raw wasmtime linker (hostcall ABI, f32 in/out) to the
#    wasmtime component model API. Guest .wasm modules now export typed
#    interfaces defined in WIT, removing the stringly-typed func.call() calls
#    and making the ABI machine-checked.
#
# Usage: bash upgrade_advanced.sh  (from ~/nexus_singularity-dev)

set -Eeuo pipefail

RED='\033[0;31m'; GREEN='\033[0;32m'; YELLOW='\033[1;33m'; CYAN='\033[0;36m'; NC='\033[0m'
info()    { echo -e "${CYAN}[UPG]${NC} $*"; }
success() { echo -e "${GREEN}[OK]${NC}  $*"; }
warn()    { echo -e "${YELLOW}[WARN]${NC} $*"; }
die()     { echo -e "${RED}[ERR]${NC}  $*" >&2; exit 1; }

[[ -f "Cargo.toml" ]] || die "Run from workspace root."

# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# PART 1 — LOCK-FREE VmoHandler with DashMap
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
info "PART 1: Upgrading VmoHandler to lock-free DashMap..."

# 1a. Add dashmap to nexus_substrate's Cargo.toml
SUBSTRATE_TOML="crates/nexus_substrate/Cargo.toml"
if ! grep -q 'dashmap' "$SUBSTRATE_TOML"; then
    # Insert after the [dependencies] header
    perl -i -pe '
        if (/^\[dependencies\]/ && !$done) {
            $_ .= "dashmap = \"6\"\n";
            $done = 1;
        }
    ' "$SUBSTRATE_TOML"
    success "Added dashmap = \"6\" to $SUBSTRATE_TOML"
else
    info "dashmap already in $SUBSTRATE_TOML"
fi

# 1b. Rewrite vmo_handler.rs with DashMap
info "Rewriting crates/nexus_substrate/src/vmo_handler.rs..."
cat << 'EOF' > crates/nexus_substrate/src/vmo_handler.rs
//! Lock-free VMO registry using DashMap.
//!
//! Previously used `RwLock<HashMap<VmoHandle, Arc<SafeVmo>>>` which serialised
//! every write (allocate/release) behind a single global lock. Under concurrent
//! agent workloads this became a hot contention point.
//!
//! DashMap shards the map into N buckets (default: 2× logical CPUs). Each
//! shard has its own RwLock, so concurrent operations on different handles
//! proceed in parallel with no cross-shard blocking.
//!
//! API is fully backward-compatible — callers need zero changes.

use dashmap::DashMap;
use std::sync::{
    atomic::{AtomicU32, Ordering},
    Arc,
};

pub type VmoHandle = u32;

pub struct SafeVmo {
    pub handle: VmoHandle,
    pub data:   Vec<u8>,
    pub timestamp: u64,
}

pub struct VmoHandler {
    /// Sharded concurrent map — no global lock on reads or cross-shard writes.
    registry:    DashMap<VmoHandle, Arc<SafeVmo>>,
    /// Atomic counter replaces the second RwLock entirely.
    next_handle: AtomicU32,
}

impl VmoHandler {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            registry:    DashMap::new(),
            next_handle: AtomicU32::new(1),
        }
    }

    pub fn allocate_cognitive_window(&self, raw_data: &[u8]) -> Result<VmoHandle, String> {
        // fetch_add is lock-free — no mutex involved at all for handle allocation.
        let handle = self.next_handle.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        let vmo = Arc::new(SafeVmo {
            handle,
            data: raw_data.to_vec(),
            timestamp: std::time::SystemTime::now()
                .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
                .unwrap_or_default()
                .as_secs(),
        });
        self.registry.insert(handle, vmo);
        Ok(handle)
    }

    pub fn read_cognitive_window(&self, handle: VmoHandle) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
        self.registry
            .get(&handle)
            .map(|v| v.data.clone())
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("Invalid handle: {}", handle))
    }

    pub fn export_provenance_checkpoint(
        &self,
        handle: VmoHandle,
    ) -> Result<(VmoHandle, Vec<u8>, u64), String> {
        self.registry
            .get(&handle)
            .map(|v| (v.handle, v.data.clone(), v.timestamp))
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("Handle not found: {}", handle))
    }

    pub fn release_cognitive_window(&self, handle: VmoHandle) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.registry
            .remove(&handle)
            .map(|_| ())
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("Handle not found on release: {}", handle))
    }

    /// Returns the number of live VMO allocations.
    pub fn active_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.registry.len()
    }
}

impl Default for VmoHandler {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}
EOF
success "vmo_handler.rs rewritten with DashMap."

# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# PART 2 — WASI Component Model for wasm_runtime.rs
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
info "PART 2: Upgrading wasm_runtime.rs to WASI Component Model..."

# 2a. Add wasmtime component feature + wit-bindgen to runtime_core Cargo.toml
RT_TOML="crates/runtime_core/Cargo.toml"

# Enable the component-model feature on the existing wasmtime dep
if grep -q 'wasmtime' "$RT_TOML"; then
    perl -i -pe '
        # Match lines like: wasmtime = { version = "43.0.1", features = [...] }
        # or bare: wasmtime = "43.0.1"
        if (/^wasmtime\s*=/) {
            if (/features\s*=\s*\[([^\]]*)\]/) {
                # Already has features — append component-model if missing
                unless (/component.model/) {
                    s/(features\s*=\s*\[)([^\]]*)(\])/$1$2, "component-model"$3/;
                }
            } else {
                # Bare version string — expand to table form
                s/(wasmtime\s*=\s*)"([^"]+)"/$1{ version = "$2", features = ["component-model"] }/;
            }
        }
    ' "$RT_TOML"
    success "Enabled component-model feature on wasmtime in $RT_TOML"
fi

# Add wit-bindgen-rt for the generated bindings glue
if ! grep -q 'wit-bindgen' "$RT_TOML"; then
    perl -i -pe '
        if (/^\[dependencies\]/ && !$done) {
            $_ .= "wit-bindgen-rt = { version = \"0.39\", features = [\"bitflags\"] }\n";
            $done = 1;
        }
    ' "$RT_TOML"
    success "Added wit-bindgen-rt to $RT_TOML"
fi

# 2b. Write the WIT interface definition
mkdir -p crates/runtime_core/wit
info "Writing WIT interface definition..."
cat << 'EOF' > crates/runtime_core/wit/nexus-tool.wit
// nexus-tool.wit — WIT interface for Nexus WASM tool components
// Defines the typed contract between the host (Rust) and guest (.wasm) modules.
// The host imports nothing; guests export the `nexus-tool` interface.
package nexus:tools@0.1.0;

interface nexus-tool {
    /// Run a named inference step with a scalar input, return a scalar output.
    run-inference: func(input: f32) -> f32;

    /// Run a buffer-based computation; input length is the control parameter.
    run-buffer: func(data: list<u8>) -> f32;
}

world nexus-component {
    export nexus-tool;
}
EOF
success "WIT definition written to crates/runtime_core/wit/nexus-tool.wit"

# 2c. Rewrite wasm_runtime.rs using the Component Model API
info "Rewriting crates/runtime_core/src/wasm_runtime.rs..."
cat << 'EOF' > crates/runtime_core/src/wasm_runtime.rs
//! WASM runtime using the wasmtime Component Model.
//!
//! # Why Component Model?
//! The previous implementation used raw `Func::typed::<(f32,), f32>()` calls
//! — a stringly-typed, ABI-fragile approach where mismatches only surface at
//! runtime. The Component Model replaces this with:
//!
//!   • WIT-defined interfaces (nexus-tool.wit) — machine-checked contract
//!   • `bindgen!` macro generates typed Rust bindings at compile time
//!   • Guest modules export `nexus-tool` world; host calls typed methods
//!   • No `unsafe` required in the calling code
//!
//! # Migration for guest .wasm modules
//! Existing guests compiled against the raw hostcall ABI need to be
//! recompiled targeting the `nexus-component` WIT world. Use:
//!   `cargo component build --release`  (with cargo-component installed)

use anyhow::Result;
use wasmtime::{
    component::{bindgen, Component, Linker, ResourceTable},
    Config, Engine, Store,
};
use wasmtime_wasi::{WasiCtx, WasiCtxBuilder, WasiView};

// Generate typed host bindings from the WIT definition at compile time.
// The path is relative to CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR.
bindgen!({
    path: "wit/nexus-tool.wit",
    world: "nexus-component",
    async: false,
});

/// Host state passed into every component store.
struct NexusHostState {
    wasi: WasiCtx,
    table: ResourceTable,
}

impl WasiView for NexusHostState {
    fn ctx(&mut self)   -> &mut WasiCtx      { &mut self.wasi }
    fn table(&mut self) -> &mut ResourceTable { &mut self.table }
}

fn make_engine() -> Result<Engine> {
    let mut config = Config::new();
    config.wasm_component_model(true);
    // Cranelift optimising compiler — same as before
    config.cranelift_opt_level(wasmtime::OptLevel::Speed);
    Ok(Engine::new(&config)?)
}

fn make_linker(engine: &Engine) -> Result<Linker<NexusHostState>> {
    let mut linker = Linker::new(engine);
    // Add WASI preview-2 host functions (filesystem, clocks, etc.)
    wasmtime_wasi::add_to_linker_sync(&mut linker)?;
    Ok(linker)
}

fn make_store(engine: &Engine) -> Store<NexusHostState> {
    let wasi = WasiCtxBuilder::new()
        .inherit_stderr()
        .build();
    Store::new(engine, NexusHostState { wasi, table: ResourceTable::new() })
}

/// Runtime handle — cheap to clone, engine is Arc-backed internally.
pub struct WasmRuntime {
    engine: Engine,
    linker: Linker<NexusHostState>,
}

impl WasmRuntime {
    pub fn new() -> Result<Self> {
        let engine = make_engine()?;
        let linker = make_linker(&engine)?;
        Ok(Self { engine, linker })
    }

    /// Instantiate a component from raw .wasm bytes and call `run-inference`.
    /// The guest must export the `nexus:tools/nexus-tool` interface.
    pub fn run_bytes(&self, bytes: &[u8], input: f32) -> Result<f32> {
        let component = Component::from_binary(&self.engine, bytes)?;
        let mut store = make_store(&self.engine);

        // bindgen! generated NexusComponent::instantiate — fully typed, no unsafe
        let (instance, _) = NexusComponent::instantiate(&mut store, &component, &self.linker)?;

        // Typed method call — ABI verified at instantiation, not at runtime
        Ok(instance.nexus_tools_nexus_tool().call_run_inference(&mut store, input)?)
    }

    /// Call `run-buffer` with a byte slice; returns a scalar result.
    pub fn run_with_buffer(&self, bytes: &[u8], data: &[u8]) -> Result<f32> {
        let component = Component::from_binary(&self.engine, bytes)?;
        let mut store = make_store(&self.engine);
        let (instance, _) = NexusComponent::instantiate(&mut store, &component, &self.linker)?;

        Ok(instance.nexus_tools_nexus_tool().call_run_buffer(&mut store, data)?)
    }
}

impl Default for WasmRuntime {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new().expect("WasmRuntime::default — engine init failed")
    }
}

// ── Backward-compatible free functions (existing call sites unchanged) ────────

/// Convenience wrapper matching the old `WasmRuntime::run_bytes` free-function API.
pub fn run_bytes(bytes: &[u8], _func_name: &str, input: f32) -> Result<f32> {
    WasmRuntime::new()?.run_bytes(bytes, input)
}

/// Convenience wrapper matching the old `run_with_buffer` free-function API.
pub fn run_with_buffer(bytes: &[u8], _func_name: &str, data: &[u8]) -> Result<f32> {
    WasmRuntime::new()?.run_with_buffer(bytes, data)
}
EOF
success "wasm_runtime.rs rewritten for Component Model."

# 2d. Add wasmtime-wasi to runtime_core deps (needed for WasiView)
if ! grep -q 'wasmtime-wasi' "$RT_TOML"; then
    perl -i -pe '
        if (/^wasmtime\s*=/ && !$done) {
            $_ .= "wasmtime-wasi = { version = \"43.0.1\", features = [\"preview2\"] }\n";
            $done = 1;
        }
    ' "$RT_TOML"
    success "Added wasmtime-wasi to $RT_TOML"
fi

# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# FINAL CHECKS
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
echo ""
info "Running cargo check to validate both upgrades..."
if cargo check --workspace 2>&1; then
    success "cargo check passed — ready for full clippy run."
else
    echo ""
    warn "Check failed. Most likely causes:"
    warn "  1. wasmtime-wasi version mismatch — ensure it matches your wasmtime version exactly."
    warn "     Fix: grep 'wasmtime' Cargo.lock | head -5"
    warn "  2. wit-bindgen-rt version — check compatibility with wasmtime 43.x:"
    warn "     Fix: cargo add wit-bindgen-rt --features bitflags -p nexus_runtime_core"
    warn "  3. Guest .wasm files compiled against old ABI will need recompiling"
    warn "     against the new WIT world (nexus-component) — this does NOT break"
    warn "     the host build, only runtime instantiation of old guests."
fi

echo ""
echo -e "${CYAN}=================================================================${NC}"
echo -e "${CYAN}Summary of changes:${NC}"
echo ""
echo "  PART 1 — Lock-free VmoHandler:"
echo "    crates/nexus_substrate/src/vmo_handler.rs  (DashMap, AtomicU32)"
echo "    crates/nexus_substrate/Cargo.toml           (dashmap = \"6\")"
echo ""
echo "  PART 2 — WASI Component Model:"
echo "    crates/runtime_core/src/wasm_runtime.rs     (bindgen!, typed API)"
echo "    crates/runtime_core/wit/nexus-tool.wit       (WIT interface)"
echo "    crates/runtime_core/Cargo.toml               (component-model feature,"
echo "                                                   wasmtime-wasi, wit-bindgen-rt)"
echo ""
echo "  Next steps:"
echo "    cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings"
echo "    cargo test --workspace"
echo -e "${CYAN}=================================================================${NC}"

const std = @import("std");

// Compact Swarm Unit Definition
pub const SwarmUnit = struct {
    id: u32,
    status: u8,   // 0: Offline, 1: Idle, 2: Active
    load: u8,     // 0-100 percentage
};

// Fixed memory allocation for 68 units (The Hive)
var hive_memory: [68]SwarmUnit = undefined;

export fn init_hive() void {
    for (&hive_memory, 0..) |*unit, i| {
        unit.* = SwarmUnit{
            .id = @intCast(i),
            .status = 1, // Start in Idle
            .load = 0,
        };
    }
}

export fn get_hive_ptr() [*]SwarmUnit {
    return &hive_memory;
}

export fn update_unit(id: u32, status: u8, load: u8) void {
    if (id < 68) {
        hive_memory[id].status = status;
        hive_memory[id].load = load;
    }
}
